                      - link "BastionMachineImage" [ref=e1074] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#bastionmachineimage"
                  - cell [ref=e1075]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e1076]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e1077]: MachineImage contains the bastions machine image properties
                - row [ref=e1078]:
                  - cell [ref=e1079]:
                    - code [ref=e1080]: machineType
                    - emphasis [ref=e1081]:
                      - link "BastionMachineType" [ref=e1082] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#bastionmachinetype"
                  - cell [ref=e1083]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e1084]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e1085]: MachineType contains the bastions machine type properties
            - heading "BastionMachineImage" [level=3] [ref=e1086]
            - paragraph [ref=e1087]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e1088]: "Appears on:"
              - link "Bastion" [ref=e1089] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#bastion"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e1090]: BastionMachineImage contains the bastions machine image properties
            - table [ref=e1091]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e1092]:
                - row [ref=e1093]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e1094]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e1095]
              - rowgroup [ref=e1096]:
                - row [ref=e1097]:
                  - cell [ref=e1098]:
                    - code [ref=e1099]: name
                    - emphasis [ref=e1100]: string
                  - cell [ref=e1101]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e1102]: Name of the machine image
                - row [ref=e1103]:
                  - cell [ref=e1104]:
                    - code [ref=e1105]: version
                    - emphasis [ref=e1106]: string
                  - cell [ref=e1107]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e1108]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e1109]: Version of the machine image
            - heading "BastionMachineType" [level=3] [ref=e1110]
            - paragraph [ref=e1111]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e1112]: "Appears on:"
              - link "Bastion" [ref=e1113] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#bastion"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e1114]: BastionMachineType contains the bastions machine type properties
            - table [ref=e1115]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e1116]:
                - row [ref=e1117]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e1118]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e1119]
              - rowgroup [ref=e1120]:
                - row [ref=e1121]:
                  - cell [ref=e1122]:
                    - code [ref=e1123]: name
                    - emphasis [ref=e1124]: string
                  - cell [ref=e1125]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e1126]: Name of the machine type
            - heading "CARotation" [level=3] [ref=e1127]
            - paragraph [ref=e1128]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e1129]: "Appears on:"
              - link "ShootCredentialsRotation" [ref=e1130] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#shootcredentialsrotation"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e1131]: CARotation contains information about the certificate authority credential rotation.
            - table [ref=e1132]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e1133]:
                - row [ref=e1134]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e1135]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e1136]
              - rowgroup [ref=e1137]:
                - row [ref=e1138]:
                  - cell [ref=e1139]:
                    - code [ref=e1140]: phase
                    - emphasis [ref=e1141]:
                      - link "CredentialsRotationPhase" [ref=e1142] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#credentialsrotationphase"
                  - cell [ref=e1143]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e1144]: Phase describes the phase of the certificate authority credential rotation.
                - row [ref=e1145]:
                  - cell [ref=e1146]:
                    - code [ref=e1147]: lastCompletionTime
                    - emphasis [ref=e1148]:
                      - link "Time" [ref=e1149] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#time-v1-meta
                  - cell [ref=e1150]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e1151]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e1152]: LastCompletionTime is the most recent time when the certificate authority credential rotation was successfullycompleted.
                - row [ref=e1153]:
                  - cell [ref=e1154]:
                    - code [ref=e1155]: lastInitiationTime
                    - emphasis [ref=e1156]:
                      - link "Time" [ref=e1157] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#time-v1-meta
                  - cell [ref=e1158]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e1159]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e1160]: LastInitiationTime is the most recent time when the certificate authority credential rotation was initiated.
                - row [ref=e1161]:
                  - cell [ref=e1162]:
                    - code [ref=e1163]: lastInitiationFinishedTime
                    - emphasis [ref=e1164]:
                      - link "Time" [ref=e1165] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#time-v1-meta
                  - cell [ref=e1166]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e1167]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e1168]: LastInitiationFinishedTime is the recent time when the certificate authority credential rotation initiation wascompleted.
                - row [ref=e1169]:
                  - cell [ref=e1170]:
                    - code [ref=e1171]: lastCompletionTriggeredTime
                    - emphasis [ref=e1172]:
                      - link "Time" [ref=e1173] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#time-v1-meta
                  - cell [ref=e1174]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e1175]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e1176]: LastCompletionTriggeredTime is the recent time when the certificate authority credential rotation completion wastriggered.
                - row [ref=e1177]:
                  - cell [ref=e1178]:
                    - code [ref=e1179]: pendingWorkersRollouts
                    - emphasis [ref=e1180]:
                      - link "PendingWorkersRollout" [ref=e1181] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#pendingworkersrollout"
                      - text: array
                  - cell [ref=e1182]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e1183]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e1184]: PendingWorkersRollouts contains the name of a worker pool and the initiation time of their last rollout due tocredentials rotation.
            - heading "CRI" [level=3] [ref=e1185]
            - paragraph [ref=e1186]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e1187]: "Appears on:"
              - link "MachineImageVersion" [ref=e1188] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#machineimageversion"
              - text: ","
              - link "Worker" [ref=e1189] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#worker"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e1190]: CRI contains information about the Container Runtimes.
            - table [ref=e1191]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e1192]:
                - row [ref=e1193]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e1194]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e1195]
              - rowgroup [ref=e1196]:
                - row [ref=e1197]:
                  - cell [ref=e1198]:
                    - code [ref=e1199]: name
                    - emphasis [ref=e1200]:
                      - link "CRIName" [ref=e1201] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#criname"
                  - cell [ref=e1202]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e1203]: "The name of the CRI library. Supported values are `containerd`."
                - row [ref=e1204]:
                  - cell [ref=e1205]:
                    - code [ref=e1206]: containerRuntimes
                    - emphasis [ref=e1207]:
                      - link "ContainerRuntime" [ref=e1208] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#containerruntime"
                      - text: array
                  - cell [ref=e1209]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e1210]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e1211]: ContainerRuntimes is the list of the required container runtimes supported for a worker pool.
            - heading "CRIName" [level=3] [ref=e1212]
            - paragraph [ref=e1213]:
              - emphasis [ref=e1214]: "Underlying type: string"
            - paragraph [ref=e1215]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e1216]: "Appears on:"
              - link "CRI" [ref=e1217] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#cri"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e1218]: CRIName is a type alias for the CRI name string.
            - heading "Capabilities" [level=3] [ref=e1219]
            - paragraph [ref=e1220]:
              - emphasis [ref=e1221]:
                - text: "Underlying type: object (keys:string, values:"
                - link "CapabilityValues" [ref=e1222] [cursor=pointer]:
                  - /url: "#capabilityvalues"
                - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e1223]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e1224]: "Appears on:"
              - link "MachineType" [ref=e1225] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#machinetype"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e1226]: Capabilities of a machine type or machine image.
            - heading "CapabilityDefinition" [level=3] [ref=e1227]
            - paragraph [ref=e1228]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e1229]: "Appears on:"
              - link "CloudProfileSpec" [ref=e1230] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#cloudprofilespec"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e1231]: CapabilityDefinition contains the Name and Values of a capability.
            - table [ref=e1232]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e1233]:
                - row [ref=e1234]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e1235]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e1236]
              - rowgroup [ref=e1237]:
                - row [ref=e1238]:
                  - cell [ref=e1239]:
                    - code [ref=e1240]: name
                    - emphasis [ref=e1241]: string
                  - cell [ref=e1242]:
                    - paragraph
                - row [ref=e1243]:
                  - cell [ref=e1244]:
                    - code [ref=e1245]: values
                    - emphasis [ref=e1246]:
                      - link "CapabilityValues" [ref=e1247] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#capabilityvalues"
                  - cell [ref=e1248]:
                    - paragraph
            - heading "CapabilityValues" [level=3] [ref=e1249]
            - paragraph [ref=e1250]:
              - emphasis [ref=e1251]: "Underlying type: string array"
            - paragraph [ref=e1252]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e1253]: "Appears on:"
              - link "Capabilities" [ref=e1254] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#capabilities"
              - text: ","
              - link "CapabilityDefinition" [ref=e1255] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#capabilitydefinition"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e1256]: CapabilityValues contains capability values. This is a workaround as the Protobuf generator can't handle a map with slice values.
            - heading "CloudProfile" [level=3] [ref=e1257]
            - paragraph [ref=e1258]: CloudProfile represents certain properties about a provider environment.
            - table [ref=e1259]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e1260]:
                - row [ref=e1261]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e1262]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e1263]
              - rowgroup [ref=e1264]:
                - row [ref=e1265]:
                  - cell [ref=e1266]:
                    - code [ref=e1267]: metadata
                    - emphasis [ref=e1268]:
                      - link "ObjectMeta" [ref=e1269] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#objectmeta-v1-meta
                  - cell [ref=e1270]:
                    - text: Refer to the Kubernetes API documentation for the fields of the
                    - code [ref=e1271]: metadata
                    - text: field.
                - row [ref=e1272]:
                  - cell [ref=e1273]:
                    - code [ref=e1274]: spec
                    - emphasis [ref=e1275]:
                      - link "CloudProfileSpec" [ref=e1276] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#cloudprofilespec"
                  - cell [ref=e1277]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e1278]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e1279]: Spec defines the provider environment properties.
                - row [ref=e1280]:
                  - cell [ref=e1281]:
                    - code [ref=e1282]: status
                    - emphasis [ref=e1283]:
                      - link "CloudProfileStatus" [ref=e1284] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#cloudprofilestatus"
                  - cell [ref=e1285]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e1286]: Status contains the current status of the cloud profile.
            - heading "CloudProfileControlPlane" [level=3] [ref=e1287]
            - paragraph [ref=e1288]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e1289]: "Appears on:"
              - link "CloudProfileSpec" [ref=e1290] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#cloudprofilespec"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e1291]: CloudProfileControlPlane holds control-plane-related settings for a CloudProfile.
            - table [ref=e1292]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e1293]:
                - row [ref=e1294]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e1295]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e1296]
              - rowgroup [ref=e1297]:
                - row [ref=e1298]:
                  - cell [ref=e1299]:
                    - code [ref=e1300]: allowZonePinning
                    - emphasis [ref=e1301]: boolean
                  - cell [ref=e1302]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e1303]: AllowZonePinning enables shoots to set spec.controlPlane.zones to explicitly pintheir control plane to specific seed zones. Only set to true for providers wherezone names are globally consistent across all users of the provider.
            - heading "CloudProfileMachineControllerManagerSettings" [level=3] [ref=e1304]
            - paragraph [ref=e1305]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e1306]: "Appears on:"
              - link "MachineType" [ref=e1307] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#machinetype"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e1308]: CloudProfileMachineControllerManagerSettings contains a subset of the MachineControllerManagerSettings which can be defaulted for a machine type in a CloudProfile.
            - table [ref=e1309]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e1310]:
                - row [ref=e1311]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e1312]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e1313]
              - rowgroup [ref=e1314]:
                - row [ref=e1315]:
                  - cell [ref=e1316]:
                    - code [ref=e1317]: machineCreationTimeout
                    - emphasis [ref=e1318]:
                      - link "Duration" [ref=e1319] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#duration-v1-meta
                  - cell [ref=e1320]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e1321]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e1322]: MachineCreationTimeout is the period after which creation of a machine of this machine type is declared failed.
            - heading "CloudProfileReference" [level=3] [ref=e1323]
            - paragraph [ref=e1324]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e1325]: "Appears on:"
              - link "NamespacedCloudProfileSpec" [ref=e1326] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#namespacedcloudprofilespec"
              - text: ","
              - link "ShootSpec" [ref=e1327] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#shootspec"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e1328]: CloudProfileReference holds the information about a CloudProfile or a NamespacedCloudProfile.
            - table [ref=e1329]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e1330]:
                - row [ref=e1331]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e1332]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e1333]
              - rowgroup [ref=e1334]:
                - row [ref=e1335]:
                  - cell [ref=e1336]:
                    - code [ref=e1337]: kind
                    - emphasis [ref=e1338]: string
                  - cell [ref=e1339]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e1340]: Kind contains a CloudProfile kind.
                - row [ref=e1341]:
                  - cell [ref=e1342]:
                    - code [ref=e1343]: name
                    - emphasis [ref=e1344]: string
                  - cell [ref=e1345]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e1346]: Name contains the name of the referenced CloudProfile.
            - heading "CloudProfileSpec" [level=3] [ref=e1347]
            - paragraph [ref=e1348]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e1349]: "Appears on:"
              - link "CloudProfile" [ref=e1350] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#cloudprofile"
              - text: ","
              - link "NamespacedCloudProfileStatus" [ref=e1351] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#namespacedcloudprofilestatus"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e1352]: CloudProfileSpec is the specification of a CloudProfile. It must contain exactly one of its defined keys.
            - table [ref=e1353]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e1354]:
                - row [ref=e1355]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e1356]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e1357]
              - rowgroup [ref=e1358]:
                - row [ref=e1359]:
                  - cell [ref=e1360]:
                    - code [ref=e1361]: caBundle
                    - emphasis [ref=e1362]: string
                  - cell [ref=e1363]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e1364]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e1365]: CABundle is a certificate bundle which will be installed onto every host machine of shoot cluster targeting this profile.
                - row [ref=e1366]:
                  - cell [ref=e1367]:
                    - code [ref=e1368]: kubernetes
                    - emphasis [ref=e1369]:
                      - link "KubernetesSettings" [ref=e1370] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#kubernetessettings"
                  - cell [ref=e1371]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e1372]: Kubernetes contains constraints regarding allowed values of the 'kubernetes' block in the Shoot specification.
                - row [ref=e1373]:
                  - cell [ref=e1374]:
                    - code [ref=e1375]: machineImages
                    - emphasis [ref=e1376]:
                      - link "MachineImage" [ref=e1377] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#machineimage"
                      - text: array
                  - cell [ref=e1378]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e1379]: MachineImages contains constraints regarding allowed values for machine images in the Shoot specification.
                - row [ref=e1380]:
                  - cell [ref=e1381]:
                    - code [ref=e1382]: machineTypes
                    - emphasis [ref=e1383]:
                      - link "MachineType" [ref=e1384] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#machinetype"
                      - text: array
                  - cell [ref=e1385]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e1386]: MachineTypes contains constraints regarding allowed values for machine types in the 'workers' block in the Shoot specification.
                - row [ref=e1387]:
                  - cell [ref=e1388]:
                    - code [ref=e1389]: providerConfig
                    - emphasis [ref=e1390]:
                      - link "RawExtension" [ref=e1391] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#rawextension-runtime-pkg
                  - cell [ref=e1392]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e1393]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e1394]: ProviderConfig contains provider-specific configuration for the profile.
                - row [ref=e1395]:
                  - cell [ref=e1396]:
                    - code [ref=e1397]: regions
                    - emphasis [ref=e1398]:
                      - link "Region" [ref=e1399] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#region"
                      - text: array
                  - cell [ref=e1400]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e1401]: Regions contains constraints regarding allowed values for regions and zones.
                - row [ref=e1402]:
                  - cell [ref=e1403]:
                    - code [ref=e1404]: seedSelector
                    - emphasis [ref=e1405]:
                      - link "SeedSelector" [ref=e1406] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#seedselector"
                  - cell [ref=e1407]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e1408]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e1409]: "SeedSelector contains an optional list of labels on `Seed` resources that marks those seeds whose shoots may use this provider profile.An empty list means that all seeds of the same provider type are supported.This is useful for environments that are of the same type (like openstack) but may have different \"instances\"/landscapes.Optionally a list of possible providers can be added to enable cross-provider scheduling. By default, the providertype of the seed must match the shoot's provider."
                - row [ref=e1410]:
                  - cell [ref=e1411]:
                    - code [ref=e1412]: type
                    - emphasis [ref=e1413]: string
                  - cell [ref=e1414]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e1415]: Type is the name of the provider.
                - row [ref=e1416]:
                  - cell [ref=e1417]:
                    - code [ref=e1418]: volumeTypes
                    - emphasis [ref=e1419]:
                      - link "VolumeType" [ref=e1420] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#volumetype"
                      - text: array
                  - cell [ref=e1421]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e1422]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e1423]: VolumeTypes contains constraints regarding allowed values for volume types in the 'workers' block in the Shoot specification.
                - row [ref=e1424]:
                  - cell [ref=e1425]:
                    - code [ref=e1426]: bastion
                    - emphasis [ref=e1427]:
                      - link "Bastion" [ref=e1428] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#bastion"
                  - cell [ref=e1429]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e1430]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e1431]: Bastion contains the machine and image properties
                - row [ref=e1432]:
                  - cell [ref=e1433]:
                    - code [ref=e1434]: limits
                    - emphasis [ref=e1435]:
                      - link "Limits" [ref=e1436] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#limits"
                  - cell [ref=e1437]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e1438]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e1439]: Limits configures operational limits for Shoot clusters using this CloudProfile.See https://github.com/gardener/gardener/blob/master/docs/usage/shoot/shoot_limits.md.
                - row [ref=e1440]:
                  - cell [ref=e1441]:
                    - code [ref=e1442]: machineCapabilities
                    - emphasis [ref=e1443]:
                      - link "CapabilityDefinition" [ref=e1444] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#capabilitydefinition"
                      - text: array
                  - cell [ref=e1445]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e1446]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e1447]: MachineCapabilities contains the definition of all possible capabilities in the CloudProfile.Only capabilities and values defined here can be used to describe MachineImages and MachineTypes.The order of values for a given capability is relevant. The most important value is listed first.During maintenance upgrades, the image that matches most capabilities will be selected.
                - row [ref=e1448]:
                  - cell [ref=e1449]:
                    - code [ref=e1450]: controlPlane
                    - emphasis [ref=e1451]:
                      - link "CloudProfileControlPlane" [ref=e1452] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#cloudprofilecontrolplane"
                  - cell [ref=e1453]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e1454]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e1455]: ControlPlane holds settings that control what control-plane-related features shootsusing this CloudProfile may configure.
            - heading "CloudProfileStatus" [level=3] [ref=e1456]
            - paragraph [ref=e1457]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e1458]: "Appears on:"
              - link "CloudProfile" [ref=e1459] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#cloudprofile"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e1460]: CloudProfileStatus contains the status of the cloud profile.
            - table [ref=e1461]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e1462]:
                - row [ref=e1463]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e1464]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e1465]
              - rowgroup [ref=e1466]:
                - row [ref=e1467]:
                  - cell [ref=e1468]:
                    - code [ref=e1469]: kubernetes
                    - emphasis [ref=e1470]:
                      - link "KubernetesStatus" [ref=e1471] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#kubernetesstatus"
                  - cell [ref=e1472]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e1473]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e1474]: Kubernetes contains the status information for kubernetes.
                - row [ref=e1475]:
                  - cell [ref=e1476]:
                    - code [ref=e1477]: machineImages
                    - emphasis [ref=e1478]:
                      - link "MachineImageStatus" [ref=e1479] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#machineimagestatus"
                      - text: array
                  - cell [ref=e1480]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e1481]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e1482]: MachineImages contains the statuses of the machine image versions.
            - heading "ClusterAutoscaler" [level=3] [ref=e1483]
            - paragraph [ref=e1484]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e1485]: "Appears on:"
              - link "Kubernetes" [ref=e1486] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#kubernetes"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e1487]: ClusterAutoscaler contains the configuration flags for the Kubernetes cluster autoscaler.
            - table [ref=e1488]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e1489]:
                - row [ref=e1490]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e1491]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e1492]
              - rowgroup [ref=e1493]:
                - row [ref=e1494]:
                  - cell [ref=e1495]:
                    - code [ref=e1496]: scaleDownDelayAfterAdd
                    - emphasis [ref=e1497]:
                      - link "Duration" [ref=e1498] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#duration-v1-meta
                  - cell [ref=e1499]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e1500]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e1501]: "ScaleDownDelayAfterAdd defines how long after scale up that scale down evaluation resumes (default: 1 hour)."
                - row [ref=e1502]:
                  - cell [ref=e1503]:
                    - code [ref=e1504]: scaleDownDelayAfterDelete
                    - emphasis [ref=e1505]:
                      - link "Duration" [ref=e1506] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#duration-v1-meta
                  - cell [ref=e1507]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e1508]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e1509]: "ScaleDownDelayAfterDelete how long after node deletion that scale down evaluation resumes, defaults to scanInterval (default: 0 secs)."
                - row [ref=e1510]:
                  - cell [ref=e1511]:
                    - code [ref=e1512]: scaleDownDelayAfterFailure
                    - emphasis [ref=e1513]:
                      - link "Duration" [ref=e1514] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#duration-v1-meta
                  - cell [ref=e1515]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e1516]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e1517]: "ScaleDownDelayAfterFailure how long after scale down failure that scale down evaluation resumes (default: 3 mins)."
                - row [ref=e1518]:
                  - cell [ref=e1519]:
                    - code [ref=e1520]: scaleDownUnneededTime
                    - emphasis [ref=e1521]:
                      - link "Duration" [ref=e1522] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#duration-v1-meta
                  - cell [ref=e1523]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e1524]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e1525]: "ScaleDownUnneededTime defines how long a node should be unneeded before it is eligible for scale down (default: 30 mins)."
                - row [ref=e1526]:
                  - cell [ref=e1527]:
                    - code [ref=e1528]: scaleDownUtilizationThreshold
                    - emphasis [ref=e1529]: float
                  - cell [ref=e1530]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e1531]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e1532]: "ScaleDownUtilizationThreshold defines the threshold in fraction (0.0 - 1.0) under which a node is being removed (default: 0.5)."
                - row [ref=e1533]:
                  - cell [ref=e1534]:
                    - code [ref=e1535]: scanInterval
                    - emphasis [ref=e1536]:
                      - link "Duration" [ref=e1537] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#duration-v1-meta
                  - cell [ref=e1538]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e1539]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e1540]: "ScanInterval how often cluster is reevaluated for scale up or down (default: 10 secs)."
                - row [ref=e1541]:
                  - cell [ref=e1542]:
                    - code [ref=e1543]: expander
                    - emphasis [ref=e1544]:
                      - link "ExpanderMode" [ref=e1545] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#expandermode"
                  - cell [ref=e1546]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e1547]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e1548]: "Expander defines the algorithm to use during scale up (default: least-waste).See: https://github.com/gardener/autoscaler/blob/machine-controller-manager-provider/cluster-autoscaler/FAQ.md#what-are-expanders."
                - row [ref=e1549]:
                  - cell [ref=e1550]:
                    - code [ref=e1551]: maxNodeProvisionTime
                    - emphasis [ref=e1552]:
                      - link "Duration" [ref=e1553] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#duration-v1-meta
                  - cell [ref=e1554]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e1555]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e1556]: "MaxNodeProvisionTime defines how long CA waits for node to be provisioned (default: 20 mins)."
                - row [ref=e1557]:
                  - cell [ref=e1558]:
                    - code [ref=e1559]: maxGracefulTerminationSeconds
                    - emphasis [ref=e1560]: integer
                  - cell [ref=e1561]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e1562]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e1563]: "MaxGracefulTerminationSeconds is the number of seconds CA waits for pod termination when trying to scale down a node (default: 600)."
                - row [ref=e1564]:
                  - cell [ref=e1565]:
                    - code [ref=e1566]: ignoreTaints
                    - emphasis [ref=e1567]: string array
                  - cell [ref=e1568]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e1569]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e1570]: "IgnoreTaints specifies a list of taint keys to ignore in node templates when considering to scale a node group.Deprecated: Ignore taints are deprecated and treated as startup taints"
                - row [ref=e1571]:
                  - cell [ref=e1572]:
                    - code [ref=e1573]: newPodScaleUpDelay
                    - emphasis [ref=e1574]:
                      - link "Duration" [ref=e1575] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#duration-v1-meta
                  - cell [ref=e1576]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e1577]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e1578]: "NewPodScaleUpDelay specifies how long CA should ignore newly created pods before they have to be considered for scale-up (default: 0s)."
                - row [ref=e1579]:
                  - cell [ref=e1580]:
                    - code [ref=e1581]: maxEmptyBulkDelete
                    - emphasis [ref=e1582]: integer
                  - cell [ref=e1583]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e1584]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e1585]: "MaxEmptyBulkDelete specifies the maximum number of empty nodes that can be deleted at the same time (default: MaxScaleDownParallelism when that is set).Deprecated: This field is deprecated. Setting this field will be forbidden starting from Kubernetes 1.33 and will be removed once gardener drops support for kubernetes v1.32.This cluster-autoscaler field is deprecated upstream, use --max-scale-down-parallelism instead."
                - row [ref=e1586]:
                  - cell [ref=e1587]:
                    - code [ref=e1588]: ignoreDaemonsetsUtilization
                    - emphasis [ref=e1589]: boolean
                  - cell [ref=e1590]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e1591]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e1592]: "IgnoreDaemonsetsUtilization allows CA to ignore DaemonSet pods when calculating resource utilization for scaling down (default: false)."
                - row [ref=e1593]:
                  - cell [ref=e1594]:
                    - code [ref=e1595]: verbosity
                    - emphasis [ref=e1596]: integer
                  - cell [ref=e1597]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e1598]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e1599]: "Verbosity allows CA to modify its log level (default: 2)."
                - row [ref=e1600]:
                  - cell [ref=e1601]:
                    - code [ref=e1602]: startupTaints
                    - emphasis [ref=e1603]: string array
                  - cell [ref=e1604]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e1605]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e1606]: StartupTaints specifies a list of taint keys to ignore in node templates when considering to scale a node group.Cluster Autoscaler treats nodes tainted with startup taints as unready, but taken into account during scale up logic, assuming they will become ready shortly.
                - row [ref=e1607]:
                  - cell [ref=e1608]:
                    - code [ref=e1609]: statusTaints
                    - emphasis [ref=e1610]: string array
                  - cell [ref=e1611]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e1612]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e1613]: StatusTaints specifies a list of taint keys to ignore in node templates when considering to scale a node group.Cluster Autoscaler internally treats nodes tainted with status taints as ready, but filtered out during scale up logic.
                - row [ref=e1614]:
                  - cell [ref=e1615]:
                    - code [ref=e1616]: maxScaleDownParallelism
                    - emphasis [ref=e1617]: integer
                  - cell [ref=e1618]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e1619]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e1620]: "MaxScaleDownParallelism specifies the maximum number of nodes (both empty and needing drain) that can be deleted in parallel.Default: 10 or MaxEmptyBulkDelete when that is set"
                - row [ref=e1621]:
                  - cell [ref=e1622]:
                    - code [ref=e1623]: maxDrainParallelism
                    - emphasis [ref=e1624]: integer
                  - cell [ref=e1625]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e1626]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e1627]: "MaxDrainParallelism specifies the maximum number of nodes needing drain, that can be drained and deleted in parallel.Default: 1"
                - row [ref=e1628]:
                  - cell [ref=e1629]:
                    - code [ref=e1630]: initialNodeGroupBackoffDuration
                    - emphasis [ref=e1631]:
                      - link "Duration" [ref=e1632] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#duration-v1-meta
                  - cell [ref=e1633]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e1634]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e1635]: "InitialNodeGroupBackoffDuration is the duration of first backoff after a new node failed to start (default: 5m)."
                - row [ref=e1636]:
                  - cell [ref=e1637]:
                    - code [ref=e1638]: maxNodeGroupBackoffDuration
                    - emphasis [ref=e1639]:
                      - link "Duration" [ref=e1640] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#duration-v1-meta
                  - cell [ref=e1641]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e1642]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e1643]: "MaxNodeGroupBackoffDuration is the maximum backoff duration for a NodeGroup after new nodes failed to start (default: 30m)."
                - row [ref=e1644]:
                  - cell [ref=e1645]:
                    - code [ref=e1646]: nodeGroupBackoffResetTimeout
                    - emphasis [ref=e1647]:
                      - link "Duration" [ref=e1648] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#duration-v1-meta
                  - cell [ref=e1649]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e1650]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e1651]: "NodeGroupBackoffResetTimeout is the time after last failed scale-up when the backoff duration is reset (default: 3h)."
                - row [ref=e1652]:
                  - cell [ref=e1653]:
                    - code [ref=e1654]: emitPerNodeGroupMetrics
                    - emphasis [ref=e1655]: boolean
                  - cell [ref=e1656]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e1657]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e1658]: "EmitPerNodeGroupMetrics emits additional per node group metrics (default: false)."
                - row [ref=e1659]:
                  - cell [ref=e1660]:
                    - code [ref=e1661]: maxBinpackingTime
                    - emphasis [ref=e1662]:
                      - link "Duration" [ref=e1663] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#duration-v1-meta
                  - cell [ref=e1664]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e1665]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e1666]: "MaxBinpackingTime is the maximum time spent on binpacking for a single scale-up.If binpacking is limited by this, scale-up continues with the already calculated scale-up options (default: 5m)."
                - row [ref=e1667]:
                  - cell [ref=e1668]:
                    - code [ref=e1669]: autoscaling
                    - emphasis [ref=e1670]:
                      - link "ControlPlaneAutoscaling" [ref=e1671] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#controlplaneautoscaling"
                  - cell [ref=e1672]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e1673]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e1674]: Autoscaling contains auto-scaling configuration options for the cluster-autoscaler.
            - heading "ClusterAutoscalerOptions" [level=3] [ref=e1675]
            - paragraph [ref=e1676]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e1677]: "Appears on:"
              - link "Worker" [ref=e1678] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#worker"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e1679]: ClusterAutoscalerOptions contains the cluster autoscaler configurations for a worker pool.
            - table [ref=e1680]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e1681]:
                - row [ref=e1682]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e1683]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e1684]
              - rowgroup [ref=e1685]:
                - row [ref=e1686]:
                  - cell [ref=e1687]:
                    - code [ref=e1688]: scaleDownUtilizationThreshold
                    - emphasis [ref=e1689]: float
                  - cell [ref=e1690]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e1691]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e1692]: ScaleDownUtilizationThreshold defines the threshold in fraction (0.0 - 1.0) under which a node is being removed.
                - row [ref=e1693]:
                  - cell [ref=e1694]:
                    - code [ref=e1695]: scaleDownGpuUtilizationThreshold
                    - emphasis [ref=e1696]: float
                  - cell [ref=e1697]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e1698]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e1699]: ScaleDownGpuUtilizationThreshold defines the threshold in fraction (0.0 - 1.0) of gpu resources under which a node is being removed.
                - row [ref=e1700]:
                  - cell [ref=e1701]:
                    - code [ref=e1702]: scaleDownUnneededTime
                    - emphasis [ref=e1703]:
                      - link "Duration" [ref=e1704] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#duration-v1-meta
                  - cell [ref=e1705]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e1706]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e1707]: ScaleDownUnneededTime defines how long a node should be unneeded before it is eligible for scale down.
                - row [ref=e1708]:
                  - cell [ref=e1709]:
                    - code [ref=e1710]: scaleDownUnreadyTime
                    - emphasis [ref=e1711]:
                      - link "Duration" [ref=e1712] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#duration-v1-meta
                  - cell [ref=e1713]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e1714]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e1715]: ScaleDownUnreadyTime defines how long an unready node should be unneeded before it is eligible for scale down.
                - row [ref=e1716]:
                  - cell [ref=e1717]:
                    - code [ref=e1718]: maxNodeProvisionTime
                    - emphasis [ref=e1719]:
                      - link "Duration" [ref=e1720] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#duration-v1-meta
                  - cell [ref=e1721]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e1722]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e1723]: MaxNodeProvisionTime defines how long CA waits for node to be provisioned.
            - heading "ClusterType" [level=3] [ref=e1724]
            - paragraph [ref=e1725]:
              - emphasis [ref=e1726]: "Underlying type: string"
            - paragraph [ref=e1727]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e1728]: "Appears on:"
              - link "ControllerResource" [ref=e1729] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#controllerresource"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e1730]: ClusterType defines the type of cluster.
            - heading "Condition" [level=3] [ref=e1731]
            - paragraph [ref=e1732]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e1733]: "Appears on:"
              - link "ControllerInstallationStatus" [ref=e1734] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#controllerinstallationstatus"
              - text: ","
              - link "LiveMigration" [ref=e1735] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#livemigration"
              - text: ","
              - link "ProjectStatus" [ref=e1736] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#projectstatus"
              - text: ","
              - link "SeedStatus" [ref=e1737] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#seedstatus"
              - text: ","
              - link "ShootStatus" [ref=e1738] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#shootstatus"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e1739]: Condition holds the information about the state of a resource based on health checks.
            - table [ref=e1740]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e1741]:
                - row [ref=e1742]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e1743]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e1744]
              - rowgroup [ref=e1745]:
                - row [ref=e1746]:
                  - cell [ref=e1747]:
                    - code [ref=e1748]: type
                    - emphasis [ref=e1749]:
                      - link "ConditionType" [ref=e1750] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#conditiontype"
                  - cell [ref=e1751]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e1752]: Type of the condition.
                - row [ref=e1753]:
                  - cell [ref=e1754]:
                    - code [ref=e1755]: status
                    - emphasis [ref=e1756]:
                      - link "ConditionStatus" [ref=e1757] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#conditionstatus"
                  - cell [ref=e1758]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e1759]: Status of the condition, one of True, False, Unknown.
                - row [ref=e1760]:
                  - cell [ref=e1761]:
                    - code [ref=e1762]: lastTransitionTime
                    - emphasis [ref=e1763]:
                      - link "Time" [ref=e1764] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#time-v1-meta
                  - cell [ref=e1765]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e1766]: Last time the condition transitioned from one status to another.
                - row [ref=e1767]:
                  - cell [ref=e1768]:
                    - code [ref=e1769]: lastUpdateTime
                    - emphasis [ref=e1770]:
                      - link "Time" [ref=e1771] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#time-v1-meta
                  - cell [ref=e1772]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e1773]: Last time the condition was updated.
                - row [ref=e1774]:
                  - cell [ref=e1775]:
                    - code [ref=e1776]: reason
                    - emphasis [ref=e1777]: string
                  - cell [ref=e1778]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e1779]: The reason for the condition's last transition.
                - row [ref=e1780]:
                  - cell [ref=e1781]:
                    - code [ref=e1782]: message
                    - emphasis [ref=e1783]: string
                  - cell [ref=e1784]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e1785]: A human readable message indicating details about the transition.
                - row [ref=e1786]:
                  - cell [ref=e1787]:
                    - code [ref=e1788]: codes
                    - emphasis [ref=e1789]:
                      - link "ErrorCode" [ref=e1790] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#errorcode"
                      - text: array
                  - cell [ref=e1791]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e1792]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e1793]: Well-defined error codes in case the condition reports a problem.
            - heading "ConditionStatus" [level=3] [ref=e1794]
            - paragraph [ref=e1795]:
              - emphasis [ref=e1796]: "Underlying type: string"
            - paragraph [ref=e1797]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e1798]: "Appears on:"
              - link "Condition" [ref=e1799] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#condition"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e1800]: ConditionStatus is the status of a condition.
            - heading "ConditionType" [level=3] [ref=e1801]
            - paragraph [ref=e1802]:
              - emphasis [ref=e1803]: "Underlying type: string"
            - paragraph [ref=e1804]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e1805]: "Appears on:"
              - link "Condition" [ref=e1806] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#condition"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e1807]: ConditionType is a string alias.
            - heading "ContainerRuntime" [level=3] [ref=e1808]
            - paragraph [ref=e1809]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e1810]: "Appears on:"
              - link "CRI" [ref=e1811] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#cri"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e1812]: ContainerRuntime contains information about worker's available container runtime
            - table [ref=e1813]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e1814]:
                - row [ref=e1815]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e1816]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e1817]
              - rowgroup [ref=e1818]:
                - row [ref=e1819]:
                  - cell [ref=e1820]:
                    - code [ref=e1821]: type
                    - emphasis [ref=e1822]: string
                  - cell [ref=e1823]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e1824]: Type is the type of the Container Runtime.
                - row [ref=e1825]:
                  - cell [ref=e1826]:
                    - code [ref=e1827]: providerConfig
                    - emphasis [ref=e1828]:
                      - link "RawExtension" [ref=e1829] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#rawextension-runtime-pkg
                  - cell [ref=e1830]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e1831]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e1832]: ProviderConfig is the configuration passed to container runtime resource.
            - heading "ControlPlane" [level=3] [ref=e1833]
            - paragraph [ref=e1834]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e1835]: "Appears on:"
              - link "ShootSpec" [ref=e1836] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#shootspec"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e1837]: ControlPlane holds information about the general settings for the control plane of a shoot.
            - table [ref=e1838]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e1839]:
                - row [ref=e1840]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e1841]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e1842]
              - rowgroup [ref=e1843]:
                - row [ref=e1844]:
                  - cell [ref=e1845]:
                    - code [ref=e1846]: highAvailability
                    - emphasis [ref=e1847]:
                      - link "HighAvailability" [ref=e1848] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#highavailability"
                  - cell [ref=e1849]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e1850]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e1851]: HighAvailability holds the configuration settings for high availability of thecontrol plane of a shoot.
                - row [ref=e1852]:
                  - cell [ref=e1853]:
                    - code [ref=e1854]: zones
                    - emphasis [ref=e1855]: string array
                  - cell [ref=e1856]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e1857]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e1858]: Zones is a list of availability zones in which the control plane components should be placed.Requires the referenced CloudProfile to have spec.controlPlane.allowZonePinning set to true.This field is immutable once set.
            - heading "ControlPlaneAutoscaling" [level=3] [ref=e1859]
            - paragraph [ref=e1860]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e1861]: "Appears on:"
              - link "ClusterAutoscaler" [ref=e1862] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#clusterautoscaler"
              - text: ","
              - link "ETCDConfig" [ref=e1863] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#etcdconfig"
              - text: ","
              - link "KubeAPIServerConfig" [ref=e1864] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#kubeapiserverconfig"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e1865]: ControlPlaneAutoscaling contains auto-scaling configuration options for control-plane components.
            - heading "ControllerDeployment" [level=3] [ref=e1866]
            - paragraph [ref=e1867]: ControllerDeployment contains information about how this controller is deployed.
            - table [ref=e1868]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e1869]:
                - row [ref=e1870]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e1871]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e1872]
              - rowgroup [ref=e1873]:
                - row [ref=e1874]:
                  - cell [ref=e1875]:
                    - code [ref=e1876]: metadata
                    - emphasis [ref=e1877]:
                      - link "ObjectMeta" [ref=e1878] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#objectmeta-v1-meta
                  - cell [ref=e1879]:
                    - text: Refer to the Kubernetes API documentation for the fields of the
                    - code [ref=e1880]: metadata
                    - text: field.
                - row [ref=e1881]:
                  - cell [ref=e1882]:
                    - code [ref=e1883]: type
                    - emphasis [ref=e1884]: string
                  - cell [ref=e1885]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e1886]: Type is the deployment type.
                - row [ref=e1887]:
                  - cell [ref=e1888]:
                    - code [ref=e1889]: providerConfig
                    - emphasis [ref=e1890]:
                      - link "RawExtension" [ref=e1891] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#rawextension-runtime-pkg
                  - cell [ref=e1892]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e1893]: ProviderConfig contains type-specific configuration. It contains assets that deploy the controller.
                - row [ref=e1894]:
                  - cell [ref=e1895]:
                    - code [ref=e1896]: injectGardenKubeconfig
                    - emphasis [ref=e1897]: boolean
                  - cell [ref=e1898]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e1899]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e1900]: InjectGardenKubeconfig controls whether a kubeconfig to the garden cluster should be injected into workloadresources.
            - heading "ControllerDeploymentPolicy" [level=3] [ref=e1901]
            - paragraph [ref=e1902]:
              - emphasis [ref=e1903]: "Underlying type: string"
            - paragraph [ref=e1904]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e1905]: "Appears on:"
              - link "ControllerRegistrationDeployment" [ref=e1906] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#controllerregistrationdeployment"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e1907]: ControllerDeploymentPolicy is a string alias.
            - heading "ControllerInstallation" [level=3] [ref=e1908]
            - paragraph [ref=e1909]: ControllerInstallation represents an installation request for an external controller.
            - table [ref=e1910]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e1911]:
                - row [ref=e1912]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e1913]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e1914]
              - rowgroup [ref=e1915]:
                - row [ref=e1916]:
                  - cell [ref=e1917]:
                    - code [ref=e1918]: metadata
                    - emphasis [ref=e1919]:
                      - link "ObjectMeta" [ref=e1920] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#objectmeta-v1-meta
                  - cell [ref=e1921]:
                    - text: Refer to the Kubernetes API documentation for the fields of the
                    - code [ref=e1922]: metadata
                    - text: field.
                - row [ref=e1923]:
                  - cell [ref=e1924]:
                    - code [ref=e1925]: spec
                    - emphasis [ref=e1926]:
                      - link "ControllerInstallationSpec" [ref=e1927] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#controllerinstallationspec"
                  - cell [ref=e1928]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e1929]: Spec contains the specification of this installation.If the object's deletion timestamp is set, this field is immutable.
                - row [ref=e1930]:
                  - cell [ref=e1931]:
                    - code [ref=e1932]: status
                    - emphasis [ref=e1933]:
                      - link "ControllerInstallationStatus" [ref=e1934] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#controllerinstallationstatus"
                  - cell [ref=e1935]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e1936]: Status contains the status of this installation.
            - heading "ControllerInstallationSpec" [level=3] [ref=e1937]
            - paragraph [ref=e1938]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e1939]: "Appears on:"
              - link "ControllerInstallation" [ref=e1940] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#controllerinstallation"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e1941]: ControllerInstallationSpec is the specification of a ControllerInstallation.
            - table [ref=e1942]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e1943]:
                - row [ref=e1944]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e1945]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e1946]
              - rowgroup [ref=e1947]:
                - row [ref=e1948]:
                  - cell [ref=e1949]:
                    - code [ref=e1950]: registrationRef
                    - emphasis [ref=e1951]:
                      - link "ObjectReference" [ref=e1952] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#objectreference-v1-core
                  - cell [ref=e1953]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e1954]: RegistrationRef is used to reference a ControllerRegistration resource.The name field of the RegistrationRef is immutable.
                - row [ref=e1955]:
                  - cell [ref=e1956]:
                    - code [ref=e1957]: seedRef
                    - emphasis [ref=e1958]:
                      - link "ObjectReference" [ref=e1959] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#objectreference-v1-core
                  - cell [ref=e1960]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e1961]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e1962]: SeedRef is used to reference a Seed resource. The name field of the SeedRef is immutable.
                - row [ref=e1963]:
                  - cell [ref=e1964]:
                    - code [ref=e1965]: shootRef
                    - emphasis [ref=e1966]:
                      - link "ObjectReference" [ref=e1967] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#objectreference-v1-core
                  - cell [ref=e1968]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e1969]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e1970]: ShootRef is used to reference a Shoot resource. The name and namespace fields of the ShootRef are immutable.
                - row [ref=e1971]:
                  - cell [ref=e1972]:
                    - code [ref=e1973]: deploymentRef
                    - emphasis [ref=e1974]:
                      - link "ObjectReference" [ref=e1975] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#objectreference-v1-core
                  - cell [ref=e1976]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e1977]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e1978]: DeploymentRef is used to reference a ControllerDeployment resource.
                - row [ref=e1979]:
                  - cell [ref=e1980]:
                    - code [ref=e1981]: resourceRefs
                    - emphasis [ref=e1982]:
                      - link "ObjectReference" [ref=e1983] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#objectreference-v1-core
                      - text: array
                  - cell [ref=e1984]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e1985]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e1986]: ResourceRefs is used to reference Resource resources.
            - heading "ControllerInstallationStatus" [level=3] [ref=e1987]
            - paragraph [ref=e1988]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e1989]: "Appears on:"
              - link "ControllerInstallation" [ref=e1990] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#controllerinstallation"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e1991]: ControllerInstallationStatus is the status of a ControllerInstallation.
            - table [ref=e1992]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e1993]:
                - row [ref=e1994]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e1995]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e1996]
              - rowgroup [ref=e1997]:
                - row [ref=e1998]:
                  - cell [ref=e1999]:
                    - code [ref=e2000]: conditions
                    - emphasis [ref=e2001]:
                      - link "Condition" [ref=e2002] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#condition"
                      - text: array
                  - cell [ref=e2003]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e2004]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e2005]: Conditions represents the latest available observations of a ControllerInstallations's current state.
                - row [ref=e2006]:
                  - cell [ref=e2007]:
                    - code [ref=e2008]: providerStatus
                    - emphasis [ref=e2009]:
                      - link "RawExtension" [ref=e2010] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#rawextension-runtime-pkg
                  - cell [ref=e2011]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e2012]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e2013]: ProviderStatus contains type-specific status.
            - heading "ControllerRegistration" [level=3] [ref=e2014]
            - paragraph [ref=e2015]: ControllerRegistration represents a registration of an external controller.
            - table [ref=e2016]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e2017]:
                - row [ref=e2018]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e2019]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e2020]
              - rowgroup [ref=e2021]:
                - row [ref=e2022]:
                  - cell [ref=e2023]:
                    - code [ref=e2024]: metadata
                    - emphasis [ref=e2025]:
                      - link "ObjectMeta" [ref=e2026] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#objectmeta-v1-meta
                  - cell [ref=e2027]:
                    - text: Refer to the Kubernetes API documentation for the fields of the
                    - code [ref=e2028]: metadata
                    - text: field.
                - row [ref=e2029]:
                  - cell [ref=e2030]:
                    - code [ref=e2031]: spec
                    - emphasis [ref=e2032]:
                      - link "ControllerRegistrationSpec" [ref=e2033] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#controllerregistrationspec"
                  - cell [ref=e2034]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e2035]: Spec contains the specification of this registration.If the object's deletion timestamp is set, this field is immutable.
            - heading "ControllerRegistrationDeployment" [level=3] [ref=e2036]
            - paragraph [ref=e2037]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e2038]: "Appears on:"
              - link "ControllerRegistrationSpec" [ref=e2039] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#controllerregistrationspec"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e2040]: ControllerRegistrationDeployment contains information for how this controller is deployed.
            - table [ref=e2041]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e2042]:
                - row [ref=e2043]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e2044]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e2045]
              - rowgroup [ref=e2046]:
                - row [ref=e2047]:
                  - cell [ref=e2048]:
                    - code [ref=e2049]: policy
                    - emphasis [ref=e2050]:
                      - link "ControllerDeploymentPolicy" [ref=e2051] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#controllerdeploymentpolicy"
                  - cell [ref=e2052]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e2053]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e2054]: Policy controls how the controller is deployed. It defaults to 'OnDemand'.
                - row [ref=e2055]:
                  - cell [ref=e2056]:
                    - code [ref=e2057]: seedSelector
                    - emphasis [ref=e2058]:
                      - link "LabelSelector" [ref=e2059] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#labelselector-v1-meta
                  - cell [ref=e2060]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e2061]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e2062]: SeedSelector contains an optional label selector for seeds. Only if the labels match then this controller will beconsidered for a deployment.An empty list means that all seeds are selected.
                - row [ref=e2063]:
                  - cell [ref=e2064]:
                    - code [ref=e2065]: deploymentRefs
                    - emphasis [ref=e2066]:
                      - link "DeploymentRef" [ref=e2067] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#deploymentref"
                      - text: array
                  - cell [ref=e2068]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e2069]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e2070]: "DeploymentRefs holds references to `ControllerDeployments`. Only one element is supported currently."
            - heading "ControllerRegistrationSpec" [level=3] [ref=e2071]
            - paragraph [ref=e2072]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e2073]: "Appears on:"
              - link "ControllerRegistration" [ref=e2074] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#controllerregistration"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e2075]: ControllerRegistrationSpec is the specification of a ControllerRegistration.
            - table [ref=e2076]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e2077]:
                - row [ref=e2078]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e2079]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e2080]
              - rowgroup [ref=e2081]:
                - row [ref=e2082]:
                  - cell [ref=e2083]:
                    - code [ref=e2084]: resources
                    - emphasis [ref=e2085]:
                      - link "ControllerResource" [ref=e2086] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#controllerresource"
                      - text: array
                  - cell [ref=e2087]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e2088]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e2089]: Resources is a list of combinations of kinds (DNSProvider, Infrastructure, Generic, ...) and their actual types(aws-route53, gcp, auditlog, ...).
                - row [ref=e2090]:
                  - cell [ref=e2091]:
                    - code [ref=e2092]: deployment
                    - emphasis [ref=e2093]:
                      - link "ControllerRegistrationDeployment" [ref=e2094] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#controllerregistrationdeployment"
                  - cell [ref=e2095]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e2096]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e2097]: Deployment contains information for how this controller is deployed.
            - heading "ControllerResource" [level=3] [ref=e2098]
            - paragraph [ref=e2099]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e2100]: "Appears on:"
              - link "ControllerRegistrationSpec" [ref=e2101] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#controllerregistrationspec"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e2102]: ControllerResource is a combination of a kind (DNSProvider, Infrastructure, Generic, ...) and the actual type for this kind (aws-route53, gcp, auditlog, ...).
            - table [ref=e2103]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e2104]:
                - row [ref=e2105]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e2106]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e2107]
              - rowgroup [ref=e2108]:
                - row [ref=e2109]:
                  - cell [ref=e2110]:
                    - code [ref=e2111]: kind
                    - emphasis [ref=e2112]: string
                  - cell [ref=e2113]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e2114]: Kind is the resource kind, for example "OperatingSystemConfig".
                - row [ref=e2115]:
                  - cell [ref=e2116]:
                    - code [ref=e2117]: type
                    - emphasis [ref=e2118]: string
                  - cell [ref=e2119]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e2120]: Type is the resource type, for example "coreos" or "ubuntu".
                - row [ref=e2121]:
                  - cell [ref=e2122]:
                    - code [ref=e2123]: reconcileTimeout
                    - emphasis [ref=e2124]:
                      - link "Duration" [ref=e2125] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#duration-v1-meta
                  - cell [ref=e2126]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e2127]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e2128]: ReconcileTimeout defines how long Gardener should wait for the resource reconciliation.This field is defaulted to 3m0s when kind is "Extension".
                - row [ref=e2129]:
                  - cell [ref=e2130]:
                    - code [ref=e2131]: primary
                    - emphasis [ref=e2132]: boolean
                  - cell [ref=e2133]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e2134]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e2135]: Primary determines if the controller backed by this ControllerRegistration is responsible for the extensionresource's lifecycle. This field defaults to true. There must be exactly one primary controller for this kind/typecombination. This field is immutable.
                - row [ref=e2136]:
                  - cell [ref=e2137]:
                    - code [ref=e2138]: lifecycle
                    - emphasis [ref=e2139]:
                      - link "ControllerResourceLifecycle" [ref=e2140] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#controllerresourcelifecycle"
                  - cell [ref=e2141]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e2142]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e2143]: "Lifecycle defines a strategy that determines when different operations on a ControllerResource should be performed.This field is defaulted in the following way when kind is \"Extension\". Reconcile: \"AfterKubeAPIServer\" Delete: \"BeforeKubeAPIServer\" Migrate: \"BeforeKubeAPIServer\""
                - row [ref=e2144]:
                  - cell [ref=e2145]:
                    - code [ref=e2146]: workerlessSupported
                    - emphasis [ref=e2147]: boolean
                  - cell [ref=e2148]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e2149]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e2150]: WorkerlessSupported specifies whether this ControllerResource supports Workerless Shoot clusters.This field is only relevant when kind is "Extension".
                - row [ref=e2151]:
                  - cell [ref=e2152]:
                    - code [ref=e2153]: autoEnable
                    - emphasis [ref=e2154]:
                      - link "ClusterType" [ref=e2155] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#clustertype"
                      - text: array
                  - cell [ref=e2156]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e2157]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e2158]: AutoEnable determines if this resource is automatically enabled for shoot or seed clusters, or both.This field can only be set for resources of kind "Extension".
                - row [ref=e2159]:
                  - cell [ref=e2160]:
                    - code [ref=e2161]: clusterCompatibility
                    - emphasis [ref=e2162]:
                      - link "ClusterType" [ref=e2163] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#clustertype"
                      - text: array
                  - cell [ref=e2164]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e2165]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e2166]: ClusterCompatibility defines the compatibility of this resource with different cluster types.If compatibility is not specified, it will be defaulted to 'shoot'.This field can only be set for resources of kind "Extension".
                - row [ref=e2167]:
                  - cell [ref=e2168]:
                    - code [ref=e2169]: continuousEndpointUpdate
                    - emphasis [ref=e2170]: boolean
                  - cell [ref=e2171]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e2172]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e2173]: ContinuousEndpointUpdate indicates whether the extension requires the SelfHostedShootExposure endpoints to be continuouslyupdated with healthy/valid control plane endpoints.
            - heading "ControllerResourceLifecycle" [level=3] [ref=e2174]
            - paragraph [ref=e2175]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e2176]: "Appears on:"
              - link "ControllerResource" [ref=e2177] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#controllerresource"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e2178]: ControllerResourceLifecycle defines the lifecycle of a controller resource.
            - table [ref=e2179]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e2180]:
                - row [ref=e2181]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e2182]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e2183]
              - rowgroup [ref=e2184]:
                - row [ref=e2185]:
                  - cell [ref=e2186]:
                    - code [ref=e2187]: reconcile
                    - emphasis [ref=e2188]:
                      - link "ControllerResourceLifecycleStrategy" [ref=e2189] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#controllerresourcelifecyclestrategy"
                  - cell [ref=e2190]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e2191]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e2192]: Reconcile defines the strategy during reconciliation.
                - row [ref=e2193]:
                  - cell [ref=e2194]:
                    - code [ref=e2195]: delete
                    - emphasis [ref=e2196]:
                      - link "ControllerResourceLifecycleStrategy" [ref=e2197] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#controllerresourcelifecyclestrategy"
                  - cell [ref=e2198]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e2199]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e2200]: Delete defines the strategy during deletion.
                - row [ref=e2201]:
                  - cell [ref=e2202]:
                    - code [ref=e2203]: migrate
                    - emphasis [ref=e2204]:
                      - link "ControllerResourceLifecycleStrategy" [ref=e2205] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#controllerresourcelifecyclestrategy"
                  - cell [ref=e2206]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e2207]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e2208]: Migrate defines the strategy during migration.
            - heading "ControllerResourceLifecycleStrategy" [level=3] [ref=e2209]
            - paragraph [ref=e2210]:
              - emphasis [ref=e2211]: "Underlying type: string"
            - paragraph [ref=e2212]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e2213]: "Appears on:"
