              - link "ControllerResourceLifecycle" [ref=e2214] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#controllerresourcelifecycle"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e2215]: ControllerResourceLifecycleStrategy is a string alias.
            - heading "CoreDNS" [level=3] [ref=e2216]
            - paragraph [ref=e2217]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e2218]: "Appears on:"
              - link "SystemComponents" [ref=e2219] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#systemcomponents"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e2220]: CoreDNS contains the settings of the Core DNS components running in the data plane of the Shoot cluster.
            - table [ref=e2221]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e2222]:
                - row [ref=e2223]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e2224]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e2225]
              - rowgroup [ref=e2226]:
                - row [ref=e2227]:
                  - cell [ref=e2228]:
                    - code [ref=e2229]: autoscaling
                    - emphasis [ref=e2230]:
                      - link "CoreDNSAutoscaling" [ref=e2231] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#corednsautoscaling"
                  - cell [ref=e2232]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e2233]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e2234]: Autoscaling contains the settings related to autoscaling of the Core DNS components running in the data plane of the Shoot cluster.
                - row [ref=e2235]:
                  - cell [ref=e2236]:
                    - code [ref=e2237]: rewriting
                    - emphasis [ref=e2238]:
                      - link "CoreDNSRewriting" [ref=e2239] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#corednsrewriting"
                  - cell [ref=e2240]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e2241]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e2242]: Rewriting contains the setting related to rewriting of requests, which are obviously incorrect due to the unnecessary application of the search path.
            - heading "CoreDNSAutoscaling" [level=3] [ref=e2243]
            - paragraph [ref=e2244]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e2245]: "Appears on:"
              - link "CoreDNS" [ref=e2246] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#coredns"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e2247]: CoreDNSAutoscaling contains the settings related to autoscaling of the Core DNS components running in the data plane of the Shoot cluster.
            - table [ref=e2248]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e2249]:
                - row [ref=e2250]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e2251]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e2252]
              - rowgroup [ref=e2253]:
                - row [ref=e2254]:
                  - cell [ref=e2255]:
                    - code [ref=e2256]: mode
                    - emphasis [ref=e2257]:
                      - link "CoreDNSAutoscalingMode" [ref=e2258] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#corednsautoscalingmode"
                  - cell [ref=e2259]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e2260]: "The mode of the autoscaling to be used for the Core DNS components running in the data plane of the Shoot cluster.Supported values are `horizontal` and `cluster-proportional`."
            - heading "CoreDNSAutoscalingMode" [level=3] [ref=e2261]
            - paragraph [ref=e2262]:
              - emphasis [ref=e2263]: "Underlying type: string"
            - paragraph [ref=e2264]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e2265]: "Appears on:"
              - link "CoreDNSAutoscaling" [ref=e2266] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#corednsautoscaling"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e2267]: CoreDNSAutoscalingMode is a type alias for the Core DNS autoscaling mode string.
            - heading "CoreDNSRewriting" [level=3] [ref=e2268]
            - paragraph [ref=e2269]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e2270]: "Appears on:"
              - link "CoreDNS" [ref=e2271] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#coredns"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e2272]: CoreDNSRewriting contains the setting related to rewriting requests, which are obviously incorrect due to the unnecessary application of the search path.
            - table [ref=e2273]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e2274]:
                - row [ref=e2275]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e2276]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e2277]
              - rowgroup [ref=e2278]:
                - row [ref=e2279]:
                  - cell [ref=e2280]:
                    - code [ref=e2281]: commonSuffixes
                    - emphasis [ref=e2282]: string array
                  - cell [ref=e2283]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e2284]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e2285]: CommonSuffixes are expected to be the suffix of a fully qualified domain name. Each suffix should contain at least one or two dots ('.') to prevent accidental clashes.
            - heading "CredentialsRotationPhase" [level=3] [ref=e2286]
            - paragraph [ref=e2287]:
              - emphasis [ref=e2288]: "Underlying type: string"
            - paragraph [ref=e2289]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e2290]: "Appears on:"
              - link "CARotation" [ref=e2291] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#carotation"
              - text: ","
              - link "ETCDEncryptionKeyRotation" [ref=e2292] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#etcdencryptionkeyrotation"
              - text: ","
              - link "ServiceAccountKeyRotation" [ref=e2293] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#serviceaccountkeyrotation"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e2294]: CredentialsRotationPhase is a string alias.
            - heading "DNS" [level=3] [ref=e2295]
            - paragraph [ref=e2296]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e2297]: "Appears on:"
              - link "ShootSpec" [ref=e2298] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#shootspec"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e2299]: DNS holds information about the provider, the hosted zone id and the domain.
            - table [ref=e2300]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e2301]:
                - row [ref=e2302]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e2303]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e2304]
              - rowgroup [ref=e2305]:
                - row [ref=e2306]:
                  - cell [ref=e2307]:
                    - code [ref=e2308]: domain
                    - emphasis [ref=e2309]: string
                  - cell [ref=e2310]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e2311]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e2312]: Domain is the external available domain of the Shoot cluster. This domain will be written into thekubeconfig that is handed out to end-users. This field is immutable.
                - row [ref=e2313]:
                  - cell [ref=e2314]:
                    - code [ref=e2315]: providers
                    - emphasis [ref=e2316]:
                      - link "DNSProvider" [ref=e2317] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#dnsprovider"
                      - text: array
                  - cell [ref=e2318]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e2319]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e2320]: "Providers is a list of DNS providers that shall be enabled for this shoot cluster. Only relevant ifnot a default domain is used.Deprecated: Configuring multiple DNS providers is deprecated and will be forbidden in a future release.Please use the DNS extension provider config (e.g. shoot-dns-service) for additional providers."
            - heading "DNSExposure" [level=3] [ref=e2321]
            - paragraph [ref=e2322]:
              - emphasis [ref=e2323]:
                - text: "Underlying type:"
                - 'link "struct{}" [ref=e2324] [cursor=pointer]':
                  - /url: "#struct{}"
            - paragraph [ref=e2325]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e2326]: "Appears on:"
              - link "Exposure" [ref=e2327] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#exposure"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e2328]: DNSExposure specifies that this shoot will be exposed by DNS. There is no specific configuration currently, for future extendability.
            - heading "DNSIncludeExclude" [level=3] [ref=e2329]
            - paragraph [ref=e2330]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e2331]: "Appears on:"
              - link "DNSProvider" [ref=e2332] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#dnsprovider"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e2333]: DNSIncludeExclude contains information about which domains shall be included/excluded.
            - table [ref=e2334]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e2335]:
                - row [ref=e2336]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e2337]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e2338]
              - rowgroup [ref=e2339]:
                - row [ref=e2340]:
                  - cell [ref=e2341]:
                    - code [ref=e2342]: include
                    - emphasis [ref=e2343]: string array
                  - cell [ref=e2344]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e2345]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e2346]: Include is a list of domains that shall be included.
                - row [ref=e2347]:
                  - cell [ref=e2348]:
                    - code [ref=e2349]: exclude
                    - emphasis [ref=e2350]: string array
                  - cell [ref=e2351]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e2352]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e2353]: Exclude is a list of domains that shall be excluded.
            - heading "DNSProvider" [level=3] [ref=e2354]
            - paragraph [ref=e2355]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e2356]: "Appears on:"
              - link "DNS" [ref=e2357] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#dns"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e2358]: DNSProvider contains information about a DNS provider.
            - table [ref=e2359]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e2360]:
                - row [ref=e2361]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e2362]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e2363]
              - rowgroup [ref=e2364]:
                - row [ref=e2365]:
                  - cell [ref=e2366]:
                    - code [ref=e2367]: domains
                    - emphasis [ref=e2368]:
                      - link "DNSIncludeExclude" [ref=e2369] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#dnsincludeexclude"
                  - cell [ref=e2370]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e2371]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e2372]: "Domains contains information about which domains shall be included/excluded for this provider.Deprecated: This field is deprecated and will be removed in a future release.Please use the DNS extension provider config (e.g. shoot-dns-service) for additional configuration."
                - row [ref=e2373]:
                  - cell [ref=e2374]:
                    - code [ref=e2375]: primary
                    - emphasis [ref=e2376]: boolean
                  - cell [ref=e2377]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e2378]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e2379]: "Primary indicates that this DNSProvider is used for shoot related domains.Deprecated: This field is deprecated and will be removed in a future release.Please use the DNS extension provider config (e.g. shoot-dns-service) for additional and non-primary providers."
                - row [ref=e2380]:
                  - cell [ref=e2381]:
                    - code [ref=e2382]: secretName
                    - emphasis [ref=e2383]: string
                  - cell [ref=e2384]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e2385]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e2386]: "SecretName is a name of a secret containing credentials for the stated domain and theprovider. When not specified, the Gardener will use the cloud provider credentials referencedby the Shoot and try to find respective credentials there (primary provider only). Specifying this field may overridethis behavior, i.e. forcing the Gardener to only look into the given secret.Deprecated: This field is deprecated and will be forbidden starting from Kubernetes 1.35. Please use `CredentialsRef` instead.Until removed, this field is synced with the `CredentialsRef` field when it refers to a secret."
                - row [ref=e2387]:
                  - cell [ref=e2388]:
                    - code [ref=e2389]: type
                    - emphasis [ref=e2390]: string
                  - cell [ref=e2391]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e2392]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e2393]: Type is the DNS provider type.
                - row [ref=e2394]:
                  - cell [ref=e2395]:
                    - code [ref=e2396]: zones
                    - emphasis [ref=e2397]:
                      - link "DNSIncludeExclude" [ref=e2398] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#dnsincludeexclude"
                  - cell [ref=e2399]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e2400]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e2401]: "Zones contains information about which hosted zones shall be included/excluded for this provider.Deprecated: This field is deprecated and will be removed in a future release.Please use the DNS extension provider config (e.g. shoot-dns-service) for additional configuration."
                - row [ref=e2402]:
                  - cell [ref=e2403]:
                    - code [ref=e2404]: credentialsRef
                    - emphasis [ref=e2405]:
                      - link "CrossVersionObjectReference" [ref=e2406] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#crossversionobjectreference-v1-autoscaling
                  - cell [ref=e2407]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e2408]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e2409]: CredentialsRef is a reference to a resource providing credentials for the DNS provider.Supported resources are Secret and WorkloadIdentity.
            - heading "DataVolume" [level=3] [ref=e2410]
            - paragraph [ref=e2411]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e2412]: "Appears on:"
              - link "Worker" [ref=e2413] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#worker"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e2414]: DataVolume contains information about a data volume.
            - table [ref=e2415]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e2416]:
                - row [ref=e2417]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e2418]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e2419]
              - rowgroup [ref=e2420]:
                - row [ref=e2421]:
                  - cell [ref=e2422]:
                    - code [ref=e2423]: name
                    - emphasis [ref=e2424]: string
                  - cell [ref=e2425]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e2426]: Name of the volume to make it referenceable.
                - row [ref=e2427]:
                  - cell [ref=e2428]:
                    - code [ref=e2429]: type
                    - emphasis [ref=e2430]: string
                  - cell [ref=e2431]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e2432]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e2433]: Type is the type of the volume.
                - row [ref=e2434]:
                  - cell [ref=e2435]:
                    - code [ref=e2436]: size
                    - emphasis [ref=e2437]: string
                  - cell [ref=e2438]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e2439]: VolumeSize is the size of the volume.
                - row [ref=e2440]:
                  - cell [ref=e2441]:
                    - code [ref=e2442]: encrypted
                    - emphasis [ref=e2443]: boolean
                  - cell [ref=e2444]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e2445]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e2446]: Encrypted determines if the volume should be encrypted.
            - heading "DeploymentRef" [level=3] [ref=e2447]
            - paragraph [ref=e2448]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e2449]: "Appears on:"
              - link "ControllerRegistrationDeployment" [ref=e2450] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#controllerregistrationdeployment"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e2451]: "DeploymentRef contains information about `ControllerDeployment` references."
            - table [ref=e2452]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e2453]:
                - row [ref=e2454]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e2455]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e2456]
              - rowgroup [ref=e2457]:
                - row [ref=e2458]:
                  - cell [ref=e2459]:
                    - code [ref=e2460]: name
                    - emphasis [ref=e2461]: string
                  - cell [ref=e2462]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e2463]: "Name is the name of the `ControllerDeployment` that is being referred to."
            - heading "DualApprovalForDeletion" [level=3] [ref=e2464]
            - paragraph [ref=e2465]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e2466]: "Appears on:"
              - link "ProjectSpec" [ref=e2467] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#projectspec"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e2468]: DualApprovalForDeletion contains configuration for the dual approval concept for resource deletion.
            - table [ref=e2469]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e2470]:
                - row [ref=e2471]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e2472]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e2473]
              - rowgroup [ref=e2474]:
                - row [ref=e2475]:
                  - cell [ref=e2476]:
                    - code [ref=e2477]: resource
                    - emphasis [ref=e2478]: string
                  - cell [ref=e2479]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e2480]: Resource is the name of the resource this applies to.
                - row [ref=e2481]:
                  - cell [ref=e2482]:
                    - code [ref=e2483]: selector
                    - emphasis [ref=e2484]:
                      - link "LabelSelector" [ref=e2485] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#labelselector-v1-meta
                  - cell [ref=e2486]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e2487]: Selector is the label selector for the resources.
                - row [ref=e2488]:
                  - cell [ref=e2489]:
                    - code [ref=e2490]: includeServiceAccounts
                    - emphasis [ref=e2491]: boolean
                  - cell [ref=e2492]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e2493]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e2494]: IncludeServiceAccounts specifies whether the concept also applies when deletion is triggered by ServiceAccounts.Defaults to true.
            - heading "ETCD" [level=3] [ref=e2495]
            - paragraph [ref=e2496]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e2497]: "Appears on:"
              - link "Kubernetes" [ref=e2498] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#kubernetes"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e2499]: ETCD contains configuration for etcds of the shoot cluster.
            - table [ref=e2500]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e2501]:
                - row [ref=e2502]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e2503]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e2504]
              - rowgroup [ref=e2505]:
                - row [ref=e2506]:
                  - cell [ref=e2507]:
                    - code [ref=e2508]: main
                    - emphasis [ref=e2509]:
                      - link "ETCDConfig" [ref=e2510] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#etcdconfig"
                  - cell [ref=e2511]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e2512]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e2513]: Main contains configuration for the main etcd.
                - row [ref=e2514]:
                  - cell [ref=e2515]:
                    - code [ref=e2516]: events
                    - emphasis [ref=e2517]:
                      - link "ETCDConfig" [ref=e2518] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#etcdconfig"
                  - cell [ref=e2519]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e2520]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e2521]: Events contains configuration for the events etcd.
            - heading "ETCDConfig" [level=3] [ref=e2522]
            - paragraph [ref=e2523]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e2524]: "Appears on:"
              - link "ETCD" [ref=e2525] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#etcd"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e2526]: ETCDConfig contains etcd configuration.
            - table [ref=e2527]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e2528]:
                - row [ref=e2529]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e2530]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e2531]
              - rowgroup [ref=e2532]:
                - row [ref=e2533]:
                  - cell [ref=e2534]:
                    - code [ref=e2535]: autoscaling
                    - emphasis [ref=e2536]:
                      - link "ControlPlaneAutoscaling" [ref=e2537] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#controlplaneautoscaling"
                  - cell [ref=e2538]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e2539]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e2540]: Autoscaling contains auto-scaling configuration options for etcd.
            - heading "ETCDEncryptionKeyRotation" [level=3] [ref=e2541]
            - paragraph [ref=e2542]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e2543]: "Appears on:"
              - link "ShootCredentialsRotation" [ref=e2544] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#shootcredentialsrotation"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e2545]: ETCDEncryptionKeyRotation contains information about the ETCD encryption key credential rotation.
            - table [ref=e2546]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e2547]:
                - row [ref=e2548]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e2549]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e2550]
              - rowgroup [ref=e2551]:
                - row [ref=e2552]:
                  - cell [ref=e2553]:
                    - code [ref=e2554]: phase
                    - emphasis [ref=e2555]:
                      - link "CredentialsRotationPhase" [ref=e2556] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#credentialsrotationphase"
                  - cell [ref=e2557]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e2558]: Phase describes the phase of the ETCD encryption key credential rotation.
                - row [ref=e2559]:
                  - cell [ref=e2560]:
                    - code [ref=e2561]: lastCompletionTime
                    - emphasis [ref=e2562]:
                      - link "Time" [ref=e2563] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#time-v1-meta
                  - cell [ref=e2564]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e2565]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e2566]: LastCompletionTime is the most recent time when the ETCD encryption key credential rotation was successfullycompleted.
                - row [ref=e2567]:
                  - cell [ref=e2568]:
                    - code [ref=e2569]: lastInitiationTime
                    - emphasis [ref=e2570]:
                      - link "Time" [ref=e2571] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#time-v1-meta
                  - cell [ref=e2572]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e2573]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e2574]: LastInitiationTime is the most recent time when the ETCD encryption key credential rotation was initiated.
                - row [ref=e2575]:
                  - cell [ref=e2576]:
                    - code [ref=e2577]: lastInitiationFinishedTime
                    - emphasis [ref=e2578]:
                      - link "Time" [ref=e2579] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#time-v1-meta
                  - cell [ref=e2580]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e2581]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e2582]: LastInitiationFinishedTime is the recent time when the ETCD encryption key credential rotation initiation wascompleted.
                - row [ref=e2583]:
                  - cell [ref=e2584]:
                    - code [ref=e2585]: lastCompletionTriggeredTime
                    - emphasis [ref=e2586]:
                      - link "Time" [ref=e2587] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#time-v1-meta
                  - cell [ref=e2588]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e2589]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e2590]: LastCompletionTriggeredTime is the recent time when the ETCD encryption key credential rotation completion wastriggered.
                - row [ref=e2591]:
                  - cell [ref=e2592]:
                    - code [ref=e2593]: autoCompleteAfterPrepared
                    - emphasis [ref=e2594]: boolean
                  - cell [ref=e2595]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e2596]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e2597]: "AutoCompleteAfterPrepared indicates whether the current ETCD encryption key rotation should be auto completed after the preparation phase has finished.Such rotation can be triggered by the `rotate-etcd-encryption-key` annotation.This field is needed while we support two types of key rotations: two-operation and single operation rotation.Deprecated: This field will be removed in a future release. The field will be no longer needed withthe removal `rotate-etcd-encryption-key-start` & `rotate-etcd-encryption-key-complete` annotations."
            - heading "EncryptionAtRest" [level=3] [ref=e2598]
            - paragraph [ref=e2599]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e2600]: "Appears on:"
              - link "ShootCredentials" [ref=e2601] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#shootcredentials"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e2602]: EncryptionAtRest contains information about Shoot data encryption at rest.
            - table [ref=e2603]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e2604]:
                - row [ref=e2605]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e2606]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e2607]
              - rowgroup [ref=e2608]:
                - row [ref=e2609]:
                  - cell [ref=e2610]:
                    - code [ref=e2611]: resources
                    - emphasis [ref=e2612]: string array
                  - cell [ref=e2613]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e2614]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e2615]: Resources is the list of resources in the Shoot which are currently encrypted.Secrets are encrypted by default and are not part of the list.See https://github.com/gardener/gardener/blob/master/docs/usage/security/etcd_encryption_config.md for more details.
                - row [ref=e2616]:
                  - cell [ref=e2617]:
                    - code [ref=e2618]: provider
                    - emphasis [ref=e2619]:
                      - link "EncryptionProviderStatus" [ref=e2620] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#encryptionproviderstatus"
                  - cell [ref=e2621]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e2622]: Provider contains information about Shoot encryption provider.
            - heading "EncryptionConfig" [level=3] [ref=e2623]
            - paragraph [ref=e2624]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e2625]: "Appears on:"
              - link "KubeAPIServerConfig" [ref=e2626] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#kubeapiserverconfig"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e2627]: EncryptionConfig contains customizable encryption configuration of the API server.
            - table [ref=e2628]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e2629]:
                - row [ref=e2630]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e2631]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e2632]
              - rowgroup [ref=e2633]:
                - row [ref=e2634]:
                  - cell [ref=e2635]:
                    - code [ref=e2636]: resources
                    - emphasis [ref=e2637]: string array
                  - cell [ref=e2638]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e2639]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e2640]: Resources contains the list of resources that shall be encrypted in addition to secrets.Each item is a Kubernetes resource name in plural (resource or resource.group) that should be encrypted.Wildcards are not supported for now.See https://github.com/gardener/gardener/blob/master/docs/usage/security/etcd_encryption_config.md for more details.
                - row [ref=e2641]:
                  - cell [ref=e2642]:
                    - code [ref=e2643]: provider
                    - emphasis [ref=e2644]:
                      - link "EncryptionProvider" [ref=e2645] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#encryptionprovider"
                  - cell [ref=e2646]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e2647]: Provider contains information about the encryption provider.
            - heading "EncryptionProvider" [level=3] [ref=e2648]
            - paragraph [ref=e2649]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e2650]: "Appears on:"
              - link "EncryptionConfig" [ref=e2651] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#encryptionconfig"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e2652]: EncryptionProvider contains information about the encryption provider.
            - table [ref=e2653]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e2654]:
                - row [ref=e2655]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e2656]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e2657]
              - rowgroup [ref=e2658]:
                - row [ref=e2659]:
                  - cell [ref=e2660]:
                    - code [ref=e2661]: type
                    - emphasis [ref=e2662]:
                      - link "EncryptionProviderType" [ref=e2663] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#encryptionprovidertype"
                  - cell [ref=e2664]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e2665]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e2666]: "Type contains the type of the encryption provider.Supported types: - \"aescbc\" - \"aesgcm\" - \"secretbox\"Defaults to aescbc."
            - heading "EncryptionProviderStatus" [level=3] [ref=e2667]
            - paragraph [ref=e2668]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e2669]: "Appears on:"
              - link "EncryptionAtRest" [ref=e2670] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#encryptionatrest"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e2671]: EncryptionProviderStatus contains information about Shoot encryption provider.
            - table [ref=e2672]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e2673]:
                - row [ref=e2674]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e2675]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e2676]
              - rowgroup [ref=e2677]:
                - row [ref=e2678]:
                  - cell [ref=e2679]:
                    - code [ref=e2680]: type
                    - emphasis [ref=e2681]:
                      - link "EncryptionProviderType" [ref=e2682] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#encryptionprovidertype"
                  - cell [ref=e2683]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e2684]: Type is the used encryption provider type.
            - heading "EncryptionProviderType" [level=3] [ref=e2685]
            - paragraph [ref=e2686]:
              - emphasis [ref=e2687]: "Underlying type: string"
            - paragraph [ref=e2688]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e2689]: "Appears on:"
              - link "EncryptionProvider" [ref=e2690] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#encryptionprovider"
              - text: ","
              - link "EncryptionProviderStatus" [ref=e2691] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#encryptionproviderstatus"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e2692]: EncryptionProviderType is a type alias for the encryption provider type string.
            - heading "ErrorCode" [level=3] [ref=e2693]
            - paragraph [ref=e2694]:
              - emphasis [ref=e2695]: "Underlying type: string"
            - paragraph [ref=e2696]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e2697]: "Appears on:"
              - link "Condition" [ref=e2698] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#condition"
              - text: ","
              - link "LastError" [ref=e2699] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#lasterror"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e2700]: ErrorCode is a string alias.
            - heading "ExpanderMode" [level=3] [ref=e2701]
            - paragraph [ref=e2702]:
              - emphasis [ref=e2703]: "Underlying type: string"
            - paragraph [ref=e2704]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e2705]: "Appears on:"
              - link "ClusterAutoscaler" [ref=e2706] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#clusterautoscaler"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e2707]: ExpanderMode is type used for Expander values
            - heading "ExpirableVersion" [level=3] [ref=e2708]
            - paragraph [ref=e2709]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e2710]: "Appears on:"
              - link "KubernetesSettings" [ref=e2711] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#kubernetessettings"
              - text: ","
              - link "MachineImageVersion" [ref=e2712] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#machineimageversion"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e2713]: ExpirableVersion contains a version with associated lifecycle information.
            - table [ref=e2714]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e2715]:
                - row [ref=e2716]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e2717]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e2718]
              - rowgroup [ref=e2719]:
                - row [ref=e2720]:
                  - cell [ref=e2721]:
                    - code [ref=e2722]: version
                    - emphasis [ref=e2723]: string
                  - cell [ref=e2724]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e2725]: Version is the version identifier.
                - row [ref=e2726]:
                  - cell [ref=e2727]:
                    - code [ref=e2728]: expirationDate
                    - emphasis [ref=e2729]:
                      - link "Time" [ref=e2730] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#time-v1-meta
                  - cell [ref=e2731]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e2732]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e2733]: "ExpirationDate defines the time at which this version expires.Deprecated: Is replaced by Lifecycle; mutually exclusive with it."
                - row [ref=e2734]:
                  - cell [ref=e2735]:
                    - code [ref=e2736]: classification
                    - emphasis [ref=e2737]:
                      - link "VersionClassification" [ref=e2738] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#versionclassification"
                  - cell [ref=e2739]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e2740]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e2741]: "Classification defines the state of a version (preview, supported, deprecated).Deprecated: Is replaced by Lifecycle. mutually exclusive with it."
                - row [ref=e2742]:
                  - cell [ref=e2743]:
                    - code [ref=e2744]: lifecycle
                    - emphasis [ref=e2745]:
                      - link "LifecycleStage" [ref=e2746] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#lifecyclestage"
                      - text: array
                  - cell [ref=e2747]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e2748]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e2749]: Lifecycle defines the lifecycle stages for this version.Mutually exclusive with Classification and ExpirationDate.This can only be used when the VersionClassificationLifecycle feature gate is enabled.
            - heading "ExpirableVersionStatus" [level=3] [ref=e2750]
            - paragraph [ref=e2751]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e2752]: "Appears on:"
              - link "KubernetesStatus" [ref=e2753] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#kubernetesstatus"
              - text: ","
              - link "MachineImageStatus" [ref=e2754] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#machineimagestatus"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e2755]: ExpirableVersionStatus defines the current status of an expirable version.
            - table [ref=e2756]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e2757]:
                - row [ref=e2758]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e2759]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e2760]
              - rowgroup [ref=e2761]:
                - row [ref=e2762]:
                  - cell [ref=e2763]:
                    - code [ref=e2764]: version
                    - emphasis [ref=e2765]: string
                  - cell [ref=e2766]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e2767]: Version is the version identifier.
                - row [ref=e2768]:
                  - cell [ref=e2769]:
                    - code [ref=e2770]: classification
                    - emphasis [ref=e2771]:
                      - link "VersionClassification" [ref=e2772] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#versionclassification"
                  - cell [ref=e2773]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e2774]: Classification reflects the current state in the classification lifecycle.
            - heading "Exposure" [level=3] [ref=e2775]
            - paragraph [ref=e2776]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e2777]: "Appears on:"
              - link "WorkerControlPlane" [ref=e2778] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#workercontrolplane"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e2779]: "Exposure holds the exposure configuration for the shoot (either `extension` or `dns` or omitted/empty)."
            - table [ref=e2780]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e2781]:
                - row [ref=e2782]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e2783]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e2784]
              - rowgroup [ref=e2785]:
                - row [ref=e2786]:
                  - cell [ref=e2787]:
                    - code [ref=e2788]: extension
                    - emphasis [ref=e2789]:
                      - link "ExtensionExposure" [ref=e2790] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#extensionexposure"
                  - cell [ref=e2791]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e2792]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e2793]: Extension holds the type and provider config of the exposure extension.Mutually exclusive with DNS.
                - row [ref=e2794]:
                  - cell [ref=e2795]:
                    - code [ref=e2796]: dns
                    - emphasis [ref=e2797]:
                      - link "DNSExposure" [ref=e2798] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#dnsexposure"
                  - cell [ref=e2799]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e2800]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e2801]: DNS specifies that this shoot will be exposed by DNS.Mutually exclusive with Extension.
            - heading "ExposureClass" [level=3] [ref=e2802]
            - paragraph [ref=e2803]: ExposureClass represents a control plane endpoint exposure strategy.
            - table [ref=e2804]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e2805]:
                - row [ref=e2806]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e2807]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e2808]
              - rowgroup [ref=e2809]:
                - row [ref=e2810]:
                  - cell [ref=e2811]:
                    - code [ref=e2812]: metadata
                    - emphasis [ref=e2813]:
                      - link "ObjectMeta" [ref=e2814] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#objectmeta-v1-meta
                  - cell [ref=e2815]:
                    - text: Refer to the Kubernetes API documentation for the fields of the
                    - code [ref=e2816]: metadata
                    - text: field.
                - row [ref=e2817]:
                  - cell [ref=e2818]:
                    - code [ref=e2819]: handler
                    - emphasis [ref=e2820]: string
                  - cell [ref=e2821]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e2822]: Handler is the name of the handler which applies the control plane endpoint exposure strategy.This field is immutable.
                - row [ref=e2823]:
                  - cell [ref=e2824]:
                    - code [ref=e2825]: scheduling
                    - emphasis [ref=e2826]:
                      - link "ExposureClassScheduling" [ref=e2827] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#exposureclassscheduling"
                  - cell [ref=e2828]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e2829]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e2830]: Scheduling holds information how to select applicable Seed's for ExposureClass usage.This field is immutable.
            - heading "ExposureClassScheduling" [level=3] [ref=e2831]
            - paragraph [ref=e2832]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e2833]: "Appears on:"
              - link "ExposureClass" [ref=e2834] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#exposureclass"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e2835]: ExposureClassScheduling holds information to select applicable Seed's for ExposureClass usage.
            - table [ref=e2836]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e2837]:
                - row [ref=e2838]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e2839]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e2840]
              - rowgroup [ref=e2841]:
                - row [ref=e2842]:
                  - cell [ref=e2843]:
                    - code [ref=e2844]: seedSelector
                    - emphasis [ref=e2845]:
                      - link "SeedSelector" [ref=e2846] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#seedselector"
                  - cell [ref=e2847]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e2848]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e2849]: SeedSelector is an optional label selector for Seed's which are suitable to use the ExposureClass.
                - row [ref=e2850]:
                  - cell [ref=e2851]:
                    - code [ref=e2852]: tolerations
                    - emphasis [ref=e2853]:
                      - link "Toleration" [ref=e2854] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#toleration"
                      - text: array
                  - cell [ref=e2855]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e2856]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e2857]: Tolerations contains the tolerations for taints on Seed clusters.
            - heading "Extension" [level=3] [ref=e2858]
            - paragraph [ref=e2859]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e2860]: "Appears on:"
              - link "SeedSpec" [ref=e2861] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#seedspec"
              - text: ","
              - link "ShootSpec" [ref=e2862] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#shootspec"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e2863]: Extension contains type and provider information for extensions.
            - table [ref=e2864]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e2865]:
                - row [ref=e2866]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e2867]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e2868]
              - rowgroup [ref=e2869]:
                - row [ref=e2870]:
                  - cell [ref=e2871]:
                    - code [ref=e2872]: type
                    - emphasis [ref=e2873]: string
                  - cell [ref=e2874]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e2875]: Type is the type of the extension resource.
                - row [ref=e2876]:
                  - cell [ref=e2877]:
                    - code [ref=e2878]: providerConfig
                    - emphasis [ref=e2879]:
                      - link "RawExtension" [ref=e2880] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#rawextension-runtime-pkg
                  - cell [ref=e2881]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e2882]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e2883]: ProviderConfig is the configuration passed to extension resource.
                - row [ref=e2884]:
                  - cell [ref=e2885]:
                    - code [ref=e2886]: disabled
                    - emphasis [ref=e2887]: boolean
                  - cell [ref=e2888]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e2889]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e2890]: Disabled allows to disable extensions that were marked as 'automatically enabled' by Gardener administrators.
            - heading "ExtensionExposure" [level=3] [ref=e2891]
            - paragraph [ref=e2892]:
              - emphasis [ref=e2893]:
                - text: "Underlying type:"
                - 'link "struct{Type *string \"json:\"type,omitempty\" protobuf:\"bytes,1,opt,name=type\"\"; ProviderConfig *k8s.io/apimachinery/pkg/runtime.RawExtension \"json:\"providerConfig,omitempty\" protobuf:\"bytes,2,opt,name=providerConfig\"\"}" [ref=e2894] [cursor=pointer]':
                  - /url: "#struct{type-*string-"
            - paragraph [ref=e2895]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e2896]: "Appears on:"
              - link "Exposure" [ref=e2897] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#exposure"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e2898]: ExtensionExposure holds the type and provider config of the exposure extension.
            - heading "ExtensionResourceState" [level=3] [ref=e2899]
            - paragraph [ref=e2900]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e2901]: "Appears on:"
              - link "ShootStateSpec" [ref=e2902] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#shootstatespec"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e2903]: ExtensionResourceState contains the kind of the extension custom resource and its last observed state in the Shoot's namespace on the Seed cluster.
            - table [ref=e2904]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e2905]:
                - row [ref=e2906]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e2907]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e2908]
              - rowgroup [ref=e2909]:
                - row [ref=e2910]:
                  - cell [ref=e2911]:
                    - code [ref=e2912]: kind
                    - emphasis [ref=e2913]: string
                  - cell [ref=e2914]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e2915]: Kind (type) of the extension custom resource
                - row [ref=e2916]:
                  - cell [ref=e2917]:
                    - code [ref=e2918]: name
                    - emphasis [ref=e2919]: string
                  - cell [ref=e2920]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e2921]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e2922]: Name of the extension custom resource
                - row [ref=e2923]:
                  - cell [ref=e2924]:
                    - code [ref=e2925]: purpose
                    - emphasis [ref=e2926]: string
                  - cell [ref=e2927]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e2928]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e2929]: Purpose of the extension custom resource
                - row [ref=e2930]:
                  - cell [ref=e2931]:
                    - code [ref=e2932]: state
                    - emphasis [ref=e2933]:
                      - link "RawExtension" [ref=e2934] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#rawextension-runtime-pkg
                  - cell [ref=e2935]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e2936]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e2937]: State of the extension resource
                - row [ref=e2938]:
                  - cell [ref=e2939]:
                    - code [ref=e2940]: resources
                    - emphasis [ref=e2941]:
                      - link "NamedResourceReference" [ref=e2942] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#namedresourcereference"
                      - text: array
                  - cell [ref=e2943]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e2944]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e2945]: Resources holds a list of named resource references that can be referred to in the state by their names.
            - heading "FailureTolerance" [level=3] [ref=e2946]
            - paragraph [ref=e2947]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e2948]: "Appears on:"
              - link "HighAvailability" [ref=e2949] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#highavailability"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e2950]: FailureTolerance describes information about failure tolerance level of a highly available resource.
            - table [ref=e2951]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e2952]:
                - row [ref=e2953]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e2954]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e2955]
              - rowgroup [ref=e2956]:
                - row [ref=e2957]:
                  - cell [ref=e2958]:
                    - code [ref=e2959]: type
                    - emphasis [ref=e2960]:
                      - link "FailureToleranceType" [ref=e2961] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#failuretolerancetype"
                  - cell [ref=e2962]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e2963]: Type specifies the type of failure that the highly available resource can tolerate
            - heading "FailureToleranceType" [level=3] [ref=e2964]
            - paragraph [ref=e2965]:
              - emphasis [ref=e2966]: "Underlying type: string"
            - paragraph [ref=e2967]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e2968]: "Appears on:"
              - link "FailureTolerance" [ref=e2969] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#failuretolerance"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e2970]: FailureToleranceType specifies the type of failure that a highly available shoot control plane that can tolerate.
            - heading "Gardener" [level=3] [ref=e2971]
            - paragraph [ref=e2972]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e2973]: "Appears on:"
              - link "SeedStatus" [ref=e2974] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#seedstatus"
              - text: ","
              - link "ShootStatus" [ref=e2975] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#shootstatus"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e2976]: Gardener holds the information about the Gardener version that operated a resource.
            - table [ref=e2977]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e2978]:
                - row [ref=e2979]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e2980]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e2981]
              - rowgroup [ref=e2982]:
                - row [ref=e2983]:
                  - cell [ref=e2984]:
                    - code [ref=e2985]: id
                    - emphasis [ref=e2986]: string
                  - cell [ref=e2987]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e2988]: ID is the container id of the Gardener which last acted on a resource.
                - row [ref=e2989]:
                  - cell [ref=e2990]:
                    - code [ref=e2991]: name
                    - emphasis [ref=e2992]: string
                  - cell [ref=e2993]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e2994]: Name is the hostname (pod name) of the Gardener which last acted on a resource.
                - row [ref=e2995]:
                  - cell [ref=e2996]:
                    - code [ref=e2997]: version
                    - emphasis [ref=e2998]: string
                  - cell [ref=e2999]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e3000]: Version is the version of the Gardener which last acted on a resource.
            - heading "GardenerResourceData" [level=3] [ref=e3001]
            - paragraph [ref=e3002]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e3003]: "Appears on:"
              - link "ShootStateSpec" [ref=e3004] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#shootstatespec"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e3005]: GardenerResourceData holds the data which is used to generate resources, deployed in the Shoot's control plane.
            - table [ref=e3006]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e3007]:
                - row [ref=e3008]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e3009]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e3010]
              - rowgroup [ref=e3011]:
                - row [ref=e3012]:
                  - cell [ref=e3013]:
                    - code [ref=e3014]: name
                    - emphasis [ref=e3015]: string
                  - cell [ref=e3016]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e3017]: Name of the object required to generate resources
                - row [ref=e3018]:
                  - cell [ref=e3019]:
                    - code [ref=e3020]: type
                    - emphasis [ref=e3021]: string
                  - cell [ref=e3022]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e3023]: Type of the object
                - row [ref=e3024]:
                  - cell [ref=e3025]:
                    - code [ref=e3026]: data
                    - emphasis [ref=e3027]:
                      - link "RawExtension" [ref=e3028] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#rawextension-runtime-pkg
                  - cell [ref=e3029]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e3030]: Data contains the payload required to generate resources
                - row [ref=e3031]:
                  - cell [ref=e3032]:
                    - code [ref=e3033]: labels
                    - emphasis [ref=e3034]: object (keys:string, values:string)
                  - cell [ref=e3035]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e3036]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e3037]: Labels are labels of the object
            - heading "HelmControllerDeployment" [level=3] [ref=e3038]
            - paragraph [ref=e3039]: HelmControllerDeployment configures how an extension controller is deployed using helm. This is the legacy structure that used to be defined in gardenlet's ControllerInstallation controller for ControllerDeployment's with type=helm. While this is not a proper API type, we need to define the structure in the API package so that we can convert it to the internal API version in the new representation.
            - table [ref=e3040]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e3041]:
                - row [ref=e3042]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e3043]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e3044]
              - rowgroup [ref=e3045]:
                - row [ref=e3046]:
                  - cell [ref=e3047]:
                    - code [ref=e3048]: chart
                    - emphasis [ref=e3049]: integer array
                  - cell [ref=e3050]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e3051]: Chart is a Helm chart tarball.
                - row [ref=e3052]:
                  - cell [ref=e3053]:
                    - code [ref=e3054]: values
                    - emphasis [ref=e3055]:
                      - link "JSON" [ref=e3056] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#json-v1-apiextensions-k8s-io
                  - cell [ref=e3057]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e3058]: Values is a map of values for the given chart.
                - row [ref=e3059]:
                  - cell [ref=e3060]:
                    - code [ref=e3061]: ociRepository
                    - emphasis [ref=e3062]:
                      - link "OCIRepository" [ref=e3063] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#ocirepository"
                  - cell [ref=e3064]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e3065]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e3066]: OCIRepository defines where to pull the chart.
            - heading "Hibernation" [level=3] [ref=e3067]
            - paragraph [ref=e3068]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e3069]: "Appears on:"
              - link "ShootSpec" [ref=e3070] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#shootspec"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e3071]: Hibernation contains information whether the Shoot is suspended or not.
            - table [ref=e3072]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e3073]:
                - row [ref=e3074]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e3075]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e3076]
              - rowgroup [ref=e3077]:
                - row [ref=e3078]:
                  - cell [ref=e3079]:
                    - code [ref=e3080]: enabled
                    - emphasis [ref=e3081]: boolean
                  - cell [ref=e3082]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e3083]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e3084]: Enabled specifies whether the Shoot needs to be hibernated or not. If it is true, the Shoot's desired state is to be hibernated.If it is false or nil, the Shoot's desired state is to be awakened.
                - row [ref=e3085]:
                  - cell [ref=e3086]:
                    - code [ref=e3087]: schedules
                    - emphasis [ref=e3088]:
                      - link "HibernationSchedule" [ref=e3089] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#hibernationschedule"
                      - text: array
                  - cell [ref=e3090]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e3091]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e3092]: Schedules determine the hibernation schedules.
            - heading "HibernationSchedule" [level=3] [ref=e3093]
            - paragraph [ref=e3094]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e3095]: "Appears on:"
              - link "Hibernation" [ref=e3096] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#hibernation"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e3097]: HibernationSchedule determines the hibernation schedule of a Shoot. A Shoot will be regularly hibernated at each start time and will be woken up at each end time. Start or End can be omitted, though at least one of each has to be specified.
            - table [ref=e3098]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e3099]:
                - row [ref=e3100]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e3101]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e3102]
              - rowgroup [ref=e3103]:
                - row [ref=e3104]:
                  - cell [ref=e3105]:
                    - code [ref=e3106]: start
                    - emphasis [ref=e3107]: string
                  - cell [ref=e3108]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e3109]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e3110]: Start is a Cron spec at which time a Shoot will be hibernated.
                - row [ref=e3111]:
                  - cell [ref=e3112]:
                    - code [ref=e3113]: end
                    - emphasis [ref=e3114]: string
                  - cell [ref=e3115]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e3116]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e3117]: End is a Cron spec at which time a Shoot will be woken up.
                - row [ref=e3118]:
                  - cell [ref=e3119]:
                    - code [ref=e3120]: location
                    - emphasis [ref=e3121]: string
                  - cell [ref=e3122]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e3123]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e3124]: Location is the time location in which both start and shall be evaluated.
            - heading "HighAvailability" [level=3] [ref=e3125]
            - paragraph [ref=e3126]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e3127]: "Appears on:"
              - link "ControlPlane" [ref=e3128] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#controlplane"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e3129]: HighAvailability specifies the configuration settings for high availability for a resource. Typical usages could be to configure HA for shoot control plane or for seed system components.
            - table [ref=e3130]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e3131]:
                - row [ref=e3132]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e3133]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e3134]
              - rowgroup [ref=e3135]:
                - row [ref=e3136]:
                  - cell [ref=e3137]:
                    - code [ref=e3138]: failureTolerance
                    - emphasis [ref=e3139]:
                      - link "FailureTolerance" [ref=e3140] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#failuretolerance"
                  - cell [ref=e3141]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e3142]: FailureTolerance holds information about failure tolerance level of a highly available resource.
            - heading "HorizontalPodAutoscalerConfig" [level=3] [ref=e3143]
            - paragraph [ref=e3144]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e3145]: "Appears on:"
              - link "KubeControllerManagerConfig" [ref=e3146] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#kubecontrollermanagerconfig"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e3147]: "HorizontalPodAutoscalerConfig contains horizontal pod autoscaler configuration settings for the kube-controller-manager. Note: Descriptions were taken from the Kubernetes documentation."
            - table [ref=e3148]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e3149]:
                - row [ref=e3150]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e3151]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e3152]
              - rowgroup [ref=e3153]:
                - row [ref=e3154]:
                  - cell [ref=e3155]:
                    - code [ref=e3156]: cpuInitializationPeriod
                    - emphasis [ref=e3157]:
                      - link "Duration" [ref=e3158] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#duration-v1-meta
                  - cell [ref=e3159]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e3160]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e3161]: The period after which a ready pod transition is considered to be the first.
                - row [ref=e3162]:
                  - cell [ref=e3163]:
                    - code [ref=e3164]: downscaleStabilization
                    - emphasis [ref=e3165]:
                      - link "Duration" [ref=e3166] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#duration-v1-meta
                  - cell [ref=e3167]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e3168]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e3169]: The configurable window at which the controller will choose the highest recommendation for autoscaling.
                - row [ref=e3170]:
                  - cell [ref=e3171]:
                    - code [ref=e3172]: initialReadinessDelay
                    - emphasis [ref=e3173]:
                      - link "Duration" [ref=e3174] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#duration-v1-meta
                  - cell [ref=e3175]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e3176]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e3177]: The configurable period at which the horizontal pod autoscaler considers a Pod “not yet ready” given that it’s unready and it has transitioned to unready during that time.
                - row [ref=e3178]:
                  - cell [ref=e3179]:
                    - code [ref=e3180]: syncPeriod
                    - emphasis [ref=e3181]:
                      - link "Duration" [ref=e3182] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#duration-v1-meta
                  - cell [ref=e3183]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e3184]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e3185]: The period for syncing the number of pods in horizontal pod autoscaler.
                - row [ref=e3186]:
                  - cell [ref=e3187]:
                    - code [ref=e3188]: tolerance
                    - emphasis [ref=e3189]: float
                  - cell [ref=e3190]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e3191]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e3192]: The minimum change (from 1.0) in the desired-to-actual metrics ratio for the horizontal pod autoscaler to consider scaling.
            - heading "IPFamily" [level=3] [ref=e3193]
            - paragraph [ref=e3194]:
              - emphasis [ref=e3195]: "Underlying type: string"
            - paragraph [ref=e3196]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e3197]: "Appears on:"
              - link "Networking" [ref=e3198] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#networking"
              - text: ","
              - link "SeedNetworks" [ref=e3199] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#seednetworks"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e3200]: IPFamily is a type for specifying an IP protocol version to use in Gardener clusters.
            - heading "ImagePullCredentialsVerificationPolicy" [level=3] [ref=e3201]
            - paragraph [ref=e3202]:
              - emphasis [ref=e3203]: "Underlying type: string"
            - paragraph [ref=e3204]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e3205]: "Appears on:"
              - link "KubeletConfig" [ref=e3206] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#kubeletconfig"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e3207]: ImagePullCredentialsVerificationPolicy is an enum for the policy that determines how credentials must be verified before a pod is allowed to use an image that is already present on the node.
            - heading "InPlaceUpdates" [level=3] [ref=e3208]
            - paragraph [ref=e3209]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e3210]: "Appears on:"
              - link "MachineImageVersion" [ref=e3211] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#machineimageversion"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e3212]: InPlaceUpdates contains the configuration for in-place updates for a machine image version.
            - table [ref=e3213]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e3214]:
                - row [ref=e3215]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e3216]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e3217]
              - rowgroup [ref=e3218]:
                - row [ref=e3219]:
                  - cell [ref=e3220]:
                    - code [ref=e3221]: supported
                    - emphasis [ref=e3222]: boolean
                  - cell [ref=e3223]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e3224]: Supported indicates whether in-place updates are supported for this machine image version.
                - row [ref=e3225]:
                  - cell [ref=e3226]:
                    - code [ref=e3227]: minVersionForUpdate
                    - emphasis [ref=e3228]: string
                  - cell [ref=e3229]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e3230]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e3231]: MinVersionForInPlaceUpdate specifies the minimum supported version from which an in-place update to this machine image version can be performed.
            - heading "InPlaceUpdatesStatus" [level=3] [ref=e3232]
            - paragraph [ref=e3233]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e3234]: "Appears on:"
              - link "ShootStatus" [ref=e3235] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#shootstatus"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e3236]: InPlaceUpdatesStatus contains information about in-place updates for the Shoot workers.
            - table [ref=e3237]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e3238]:
                - row [ref=e3239]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e3240]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e3241]
              - rowgroup [ref=e3242]:
                - row [ref=e3243]:
                  - cell [ref=e3244]:
                    - code [ref=e3245]: pendingWorkerUpdates
                    - emphasis [ref=e3246]:
                      - link "PendingWorkerUpdates" [ref=e3247] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#pendingworkerupdates"
                  - cell [ref=e3248]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e3249]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e3250]: PendingWorkerUpdates contains information about worker pools pending in-place updates.
            - heading "Ingress" [level=3] [ref=e3251]
            - paragraph [ref=e3252]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e3253]: "Appears on:"
              - link "SeedSpec" [ref=e3254] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#seedspec"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e3255]: Ingress configures the Ingress specific settings of the cluster
            - table [ref=e3256]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e3257]:
                - row [ref=e3258]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e3259]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e3260]
              - rowgroup [ref=e3261]:
                - row [ref=e3262]:
                  - cell [ref=e3263]:
                    - code [ref=e3264]: domain
                    - emphasis [ref=e3265]: string
                  - cell [ref=e3266]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e3267]: Domain specifies the IngressDomain of the cluster pointing to the ingress controller endpoint. It will be usedto construct ingress URLs for system applications running in Shoot/Garden clusters. Once set this field is immutable.
                - row [ref=e3268]:
                  - cell [ref=e3269]:
                    - code [ref=e3270]: controller
                    - emphasis [ref=e3271]:
                      - link "IngressController" [ref=e3272] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#ingresscontroller"
                  - cell [ref=e3273]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e3274]: Controller configures a Gardener managed Ingress Controller listening on the ingressDomain
            - heading "IngressController" [level=3] [ref=e3275]
            - paragraph [ref=e3276]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e3277]: "Appears on:"
              - link "Ingress" [ref=e3278] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#ingress"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e3279]: IngressController enables a Gardener managed Ingress Controller listening on the ingressDomain
            - table [ref=e3280]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e3281]:
                - row [ref=e3282]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e3283]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e3284]
              - rowgroup [ref=e3285]:
                - row [ref=e3286]:
                  - cell [ref=e3287]:
                    - code [ref=e3288]: kind
                    - emphasis [ref=e3289]: string
                  - cell [ref=e3290]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e3291]: "Kind defines which kind of IngressController to use. At the moment only `nginx` is supported"
                - row [ref=e3292]:
                  - cell [ref=e3293]:
                    - code [ref=e3294]: providerConfig
                    - emphasis [ref=e3295]:
                      - link "RawExtension" [ref=e3296] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#rawextension-runtime-pkg
                  - cell [ref=e3297]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e3298]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e3299]: ProviderConfig specifies infrastructure specific configuration for the ingressController
            - heading "InternalSecret" [level=3] [ref=e3300]
            - paragraph [ref=e3301]: InternalSecret holds secret data of a certain type. The total bytes of the values in the Data field must be less than MaxSecretSize bytes.
            - table [ref=e3302]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e3303]:
                - row [ref=e3304]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e3305]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e3306]
              - rowgroup [ref=e3307]:
                - row [ref=e3308]:
                  - cell [ref=e3309]:
                    - code [ref=e3310]: metadata
                    - emphasis [ref=e3311]:
                      - link "ObjectMeta" [ref=e3312] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#objectmeta-v1-meta
                  - cell [ref=e3313]:
                    - text: Refer to the Kubernetes API documentation for the fields of the
                    - code [ref=e3314]: metadata
                    - text: field.
                - row [ref=e3315]:
                  - cell [ref=e3316]:
                    - code [ref=e3317]: immutable
                    - emphasis [ref=e3318]: boolean
                  - cell [ref=e3319]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e3320]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e3321]: Immutable, if set to true, ensures that data stored in the Secret cannotbe updated (only object metadata can be modified).If not set to true, the field can be modified at any time.Defaulted to nil.
                - row [ref=e3322]:
                  - cell [ref=e3323]:
                    - code [ref=e3324]: data
                    - emphasis [ref=e3325]: object (keys:string, values:integer array)
                  - cell [ref=e3326]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e3327]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e3328]: Data contains the secret data. Each key must consist of alphanumericcharacters, '-', '_' or '.'. The serialized form of the secret data is abase64 encoded string, representing the arbitrary (possibly non-string)data value here. Described in https://tools.ietf.org/html/rfc4648#section-4
                - row [ref=e3329]:
                  - cell [ref=e3330]:
                    - code [ref=e3331]: stringData
                    - emphasis [ref=e3332]: object (keys:string, values:string)
                  - cell [ref=e3333]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e3334]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e3335]: stringData allows specifying non-binary secret data in string form.It is provided as a write-only input field for convenience.All keys and values are merged into the data field on write, overwriting any existing values.The stringData field is never output when reading from the API.
                - row [ref=e3336]:
                  - cell [ref=e3337]:
                    - code [ref=e3338]: type
                    - emphasis [ref=e3339]:
                      - link "SecretType" [ref=e3340] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#secrettype-v1-core
                  - cell [ref=e3341]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e3342]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e3343]: "Used to facilitate programmatic handling of secret data.More info: https://kubernetes.io/docs/concepts/configuration/secret/#secret-types"
            - heading "KubeAPIServerConfig" [level=3] [ref=e3344]
            - paragraph [ref=e3345]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e3346]: "Appears on:"
              - link "Kubernetes" [ref=e3347] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#kubernetes"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e3348]: KubeAPIServerConfig contains configuration settings for the kube-apiserver.
            - table [ref=e3349]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e3350]:
                - row [ref=e3351]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e3352]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e3353]
              - rowgroup [ref=e3354]:
                - row [ref=e3355]:
                  - cell [ref=e3356]:
                    - code [ref=e3357]: featureGates
                    - emphasis [ref=e3358]: object (keys:string, values:boolean)
                  - cell [ref=e3359]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e3360]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e3361]: FeatureGates contains information about enabled feature gates.
                - row [ref=e3362]:
                  - cell [ref=e3363]:
                    - code [ref=e3364]: admissionPlugins
                    - emphasis [ref=e3365]:
                      - link "AdmissionPlugin" [ref=e3366] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#admissionplugin"
                      - text: array
                  - cell [ref=e3367]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e3368]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e3369]: AdmissionPlugins contains the list of user-defined admission plugins (additional to those managed by Gardener), and, if desired, the correspondingconfiguration.
                - row [ref=e3370]:
                  - cell [ref=e3371]:
                    - code [ref=e3372]: apiAudiences
                    - emphasis [ref=e3373]: string array
                  - cell [ref=e3374]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e3375]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e3376]: APIAudiences are the identifiers of the API. The service account token authenticator willvalidate that tokens used against the API are bound to at least one of these audiences.Defaults to ["kubernetes"].
                - row [ref=e3377]:
                  - cell [ref=e3378]:
                    - code [ref=e3379]: auditConfig
                    - emphasis [ref=e3380]:
                      - link "AuditConfig" [ref=e3381] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#auditconfig"
                  - cell [ref=e3382]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e3383]: (Optional)
