                    - paragraph [ref=e3384]: AuditConfig contains configuration settings for the audit of the kube-apiserver.
                - row [ref=e3385]:
                  - cell [ref=e3386]:
                    - code [ref=e3387]: runtimeConfig
                    - emphasis [ref=e3388]: object (keys:string, values:boolean)
                  - cell [ref=e3389]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e3390]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e3391]: RuntimeConfig contains information about enabled or disabled APIs.
                - row [ref=e3392]:
                  - cell [ref=e3393]:
                    - code [ref=e3394]: serviceAccountConfig
                    - emphasis [ref=e3395]:
                      - link "ServiceAccountConfig" [ref=e3396] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#serviceaccountconfig"
                  - cell [ref=e3397]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e3398]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e3399]: ServiceAccountConfig contains configuration settings for the service account handlingof the kube-apiserver.
                - row [ref=e3400]:
                  - cell [ref=e3401]:
                    - code [ref=e3402]: watchCacheSizes
                    - emphasis [ref=e3403]:
                      - link "WatchCacheSizes" [ref=e3404] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#watchcachesizes"
                  - cell [ref=e3405]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e3406]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e3407]: "WatchCacheSizes contains configuration of the API server's watch cache sizes.Configuring these flags might be useful for large-scale Shoot clusters with a lot of parallel update requestsand a lot of watching controllers (e.g. large ManagedSeed clusters). When the API server's watch cache'scapacity is too small to cope with the amount of update requests and watchers for a particular resource, itmight happen that controller watches are permanently stopped with `too old resource version` errors.Starting from kubernetes v1.19, the API server's watch cache size is adapted dynamically and setting the watchcache size flags will have no effect, except when setting it to 0 (which disables the watch cache)."
                - row [ref=e3408]:
                  - cell [ref=e3409]:
                    - code [ref=e3410]: requests
                    - emphasis [ref=e3411]:
                      - link "APIServerRequests" [ref=e3412] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#apiserverrequests"
                  - cell [ref=e3413]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e3414]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e3415]: Requests contains configuration for request-specific settings for the kube-apiserver.
                - row [ref=e3416]:
                  - cell [ref=e3417]:
                    - code [ref=e3418]: enableAnonymousAuthentication
                    - emphasis [ref=e3419]: boolean
                  - cell [ref=e3420]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e3421]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e3422]: "EnableAnonymousAuthentication defines whether anonymous requests to the secure portof the API server should be allowed (flag `--anonymous-auth`).See: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/command-line-tools-reference/kube-apiserver/Deprecated: This field is deprecated and will be removed after support for Kubernetes v1.34 is dropped.This field is forbidden for clusters with Kubernetes version >= 1.35.Please use anonymous authentication configuration instead."
                - row [ref=e3423]:
                  - cell [ref=e3424]:
                    - code [ref=e3425]: eventTTL
                    - emphasis [ref=e3426]:
                      - link "Duration" [ref=e3427] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#duration-v1-meta
                  - cell [ref=e3428]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e3429]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e3430]: EventTTL controls the amount of time to retain events.Defaults to 1h.
                - row [ref=e3431]:
                  - cell [ref=e3432]:
                    - code [ref=e3433]: logging
                    - emphasis [ref=e3434]:
                      - link "APIServerLogging" [ref=e3435] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#apiserverlogging"
                  - cell [ref=e3436]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e3437]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e3438]: Logging contains configuration for the log level and HTTP access logs.
                - row [ref=e3439]:
                  - cell [ref=e3440]:
                    - code [ref=e3441]: defaultNotReadyTolerationSeconds
                    - emphasis [ref=e3442]: integer
                  - cell [ref=e3443]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e3444]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e3445]: "DefaultNotReadyTolerationSeconds indicates the tolerationSeconds of the toleration for notReady:NoExecutethat is added by default to every pod that does not already have such a toleration (flag `--default-not-ready-toleration-seconds`).The field has effect only when the `DefaultTolerationSeconds` admission plugin is enabled.Defaults to 300."
                - row [ref=e3446]:
                  - cell [ref=e3447]:
                    - code [ref=e3448]: defaultUnreachableTolerationSeconds
                    - emphasis [ref=e3449]: integer
                  - cell [ref=e3450]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e3451]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e3452]: "DefaultUnreachableTolerationSeconds indicates the tolerationSeconds of the toleration for unreachable:NoExecutethat is added by default to every pod that does not already have such a toleration (flag `--default-unreachable-toleration-seconds`).The field has effect only when the `DefaultTolerationSeconds` admission plugin is enabled.Defaults to 300."
                - row [ref=e3453]:
                  - cell [ref=e3454]:
                    - code [ref=e3455]: encryptionConfig
                    - emphasis [ref=e3456]:
                      - link "EncryptionConfig" [ref=e3457] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#encryptionconfig"
                  - cell [ref=e3458]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e3459]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e3460]: EncryptionConfig contains customizable encryption configuration of the Kube API server.
                - row [ref=e3461]:
                  - cell [ref=e3462]:
                    - code [ref=e3463]: structuredAuthentication
                    - emphasis [ref=e3464]:
                      - link "StructuredAuthentication" [ref=e3465] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#structuredauthentication"
                  - cell [ref=e3466]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e3467]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e3468]: StructuredAuthentication contains configuration settings for structured authentication for the kube-apiserver.
                - row [ref=e3469]:
                  - cell [ref=e3470]:
                    - code [ref=e3471]: structuredAuthorization
                    - emphasis [ref=e3472]:
                      - link "StructuredAuthorization" [ref=e3473] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#structuredauthorization"
                  - cell [ref=e3474]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e3475]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e3476]: StructuredAuthorization contains configuration settings for structured authorization for the kube-apiserver.
                - row [ref=e3477]:
                  - cell [ref=e3478]:
                    - code [ref=e3479]: autoscaling
                    - emphasis [ref=e3480]:
                      - link "ControlPlaneAutoscaling" [ref=e3481] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#controlplaneautoscaling"
                  - cell [ref=e3482]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e3483]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e3484]: Autoscaling contains auto-scaling configuration options for the kube-apiserver.
                - row [ref=e3485]:
                  - cell [ref=e3486]:
                    - code [ref=e3487]: tlsMinVersion
                    - emphasis [ref=e3488]: string
                  - cell [ref=e3489]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e3490]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e3491]: "TLSMinVersion is the minimum TLS version accepted by the kube-apiserver.Supported values: VersionTLS12, VersionTLS13."
            - heading "KubeControllerManagerConfig" [level=3] [ref=e3492]
            - paragraph [ref=e3493]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e3494]: "Appears on:"
              - link "Kubernetes" [ref=e3495] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#kubernetes"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e3496]: KubeControllerManagerConfig contains configuration settings for the kube-controller-manager.
            - table [ref=e3497]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e3498]:
                - row [ref=e3499]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e3500]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e3501]
              - rowgroup [ref=e3502]:
                - row [ref=e3503]:
                  - cell [ref=e3504]:
                    - code [ref=e3505]: featureGates
                    - emphasis [ref=e3506]: object (keys:string, values:boolean)
                  - cell [ref=e3507]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e3508]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e3509]: FeatureGates contains information about enabled feature gates.
                - row [ref=e3510]:
                  - cell [ref=e3511]:
                    - code [ref=e3512]: horizontalPodAutoscaler
                    - emphasis [ref=e3513]:
                      - link "HorizontalPodAutoscalerConfig" [ref=e3514] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#horizontalpodautoscalerconfig"
                  - cell [ref=e3515]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e3516]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e3517]: HorizontalPodAutoscalerConfig contains horizontal pod autoscaler configuration settings for the kube-controller-manager.
                - row [ref=e3518]:
                  - cell [ref=e3519]:
                    - code [ref=e3520]: nodeCIDRMaskSize
                    - emphasis [ref=e3521]: integer
                  - cell [ref=e3522]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e3523]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e3524]: NodeCIDRMaskSize defines the mask size for node cidr in cluster (default is 24). This field is immutable.
                - row [ref=e3525]:
                  - cell [ref=e3526]:
                    - code [ref=e3527]: podEvictionTimeout
                    - emphasis [ref=e3528]:
                      - link "Duration" [ref=e3529] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#duration-v1-meta
                  - cell [ref=e3530]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e3531]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e3532]: "PodEvictionTimeout defines the grace period for deleting pods on failed nodes. Defaults to 2m.Deprecated: The corresponding kube-controller-manager flag `--pod-eviction-timeout` is deprecatedin favor of the kube-apiserver flags `--default-not-ready-toleration-seconds` and `--default-unreachable-toleration-seconds`.The `--pod-eviction-timeout` flag does not have effect when the taint based eviction is enabled. The taintbased eviction is beta (enabled by default) since Kubernetes 1.13 and GA since Kubernetes 1.18. Hence,instead of setting this field, set the `spec.kubernetes.kubeAPIServer.defaultNotReadyTolerationSeconds` and`spec.kubernetes.kubeAPIServer.defaultUnreachableTolerationSeconds`. Setting this field is forbidden startingfrom Kubernetes 1.33."
                - row [ref=e3533]:
                  - cell [ref=e3534]:
                    - code [ref=e3535]: nodeMonitorGracePeriod
                    - emphasis [ref=e3536]:
                      - link "Duration" [ref=e3537] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#duration-v1-meta
                  - cell [ref=e3538]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e3539]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e3540]: NodeMonitorGracePeriod defines the grace period before an unresponsive node is marked unhealthy.
                - row [ref=e3541]:
                  - cell [ref=e3542]:
                    - code [ref=e3543]: nodeCIDRMaskSizeIPv6
                    - emphasis [ref=e3544]: integer
                  - cell [ref=e3545]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e3546]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e3547]: NodeCIDRMaskSizeIPv6 defines the mask size for node cidr in cluster (default is 64). This field is immutable.
            - heading "KubeProxyConfig" [level=3] [ref=e3548]
            - paragraph [ref=e3549]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e3550]: "Appears on:"
              - link "Kubernetes" [ref=e3551] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#kubernetes"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e3552]: KubeProxyConfig contains configuration settings for the kube-proxy.
            - table [ref=e3553]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e3554]:
                - row [ref=e3555]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e3556]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e3557]
              - rowgroup [ref=e3558]:
                - row [ref=e3559]:
                  - cell [ref=e3560]:
                    - code [ref=e3561]: featureGates
                    - emphasis [ref=e3562]: object (keys:string, values:boolean)
                  - cell [ref=e3563]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e3564]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e3565]: FeatureGates contains information about enabled feature gates.
                - row [ref=e3566]:
                  - cell [ref=e3567]:
                    - code [ref=e3568]: mode
                    - emphasis [ref=e3569]:
                      - link "ProxyMode" [ref=e3570] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#proxymode"
                  - cell [ref=e3571]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e3572]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e3573]: Mode specifies which proxy mode to use.defaults to IPTables.
                - row [ref=e3574]:
                  - cell [ref=e3575]:
                    - code [ref=e3576]: enabled
                    - emphasis [ref=e3577]: boolean
                  - cell [ref=e3578]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e3579]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e3580]: Enabled indicates whether kube-proxy should be deployed or not.Depending on the networking extensions switching kube-proxy off might be rejected. Consulting the respective documentation of the used networking extension is recommended before using this field.defaults to true if not specified.
            - heading "KubeSchedulerConfig" [level=3] [ref=e3581]
            - paragraph [ref=e3582]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e3583]: "Appears on:"
              - link "Kubernetes" [ref=e3584] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#kubernetes"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e3585]: KubeSchedulerConfig contains configuration settings for the kube-scheduler.
            - table [ref=e3586]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e3587]:
                - row [ref=e3588]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e3589]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e3590]
              - rowgroup [ref=e3591]:
                - row [ref=e3592]:
                  - cell [ref=e3593]:
                    - code [ref=e3594]: featureGates
                    - emphasis [ref=e3595]: object (keys:string, values:boolean)
                  - cell [ref=e3596]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e3597]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e3598]: FeatureGates contains information about enabled feature gates.
                - row [ref=e3599]:
                  - cell [ref=e3600]:
                    - code [ref=e3601]: kubeMaxPDVols
                    - emphasis [ref=e3602]: string
                  - cell [ref=e3603]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e3604]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e3605]: "KubeMaxPDVols is not respected anymore by kube-scheduler.The maximum number of attached volumes is configured by the CSI driver.More information can be found at https://kubernetes.io/docs/concepts/storage/storage-limits/#custom-limits.Deprecated: This field is deprecated. Using this field will be forbidden starting from Kubernetes 1.35."
                - row [ref=e3606]:
                  - cell [ref=e3607]:
                    - code [ref=e3608]: profile
                    - emphasis [ref=e3609]:
                      - link "SchedulingProfile" [ref=e3610] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#schedulingprofile"
                  - cell [ref=e3611]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e3612]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e3613]: Profile configures the scheduling profile for the cluster.If not specified, the used profile is "balanced" (provides the default kube-scheduler behavior).
            - heading "KubeletConfig" [level=3] [ref=e3614]
            - paragraph [ref=e3615]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e3616]: "Appears on:"
              - link "Kubernetes" [ref=e3617] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#kubernetes"
              - text: ","
              - link "WorkerKubernetes" [ref=e3618] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#workerkubernetes"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e3619]: KubeletConfig contains configuration settings for the kubelet.
            - table [ref=e3620]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e3621]:
                - row [ref=e3622]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e3623]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e3624]
              - rowgroup [ref=e3625]:
                - row [ref=e3626]:
                  - cell [ref=e3627]:
                    - code [ref=e3628]: featureGates
                    - emphasis [ref=e3629]: object (keys:string, values:boolean)
                  - cell [ref=e3630]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e3631]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e3632]: FeatureGates contains information about enabled feature gates.
                - row [ref=e3633]:
                  - cell [ref=e3634]:
                    - code [ref=e3635]: cpuCFSQuota
                    - emphasis [ref=e3636]: boolean
                  - cell [ref=e3637]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e3638]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e3639]: CPUCFSQuota allows you to disable/enable CPU throttling for Pods.
                - row [ref=e3640]:
                  - cell [ref=e3641]:
                    - code [ref=e3642]: cpuManagerPolicy
                    - emphasis [ref=e3643]: string
                  - cell [ref=e3644]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e3645]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e3646]: "CPUManagerPolicy allows to set alternative CPU management policies (default: none)."
                - row [ref=e3647]:
                  - cell [ref=e3648]:
                    - code [ref=e3649]: evictionHard
                    - emphasis [ref=e3650]:
                      - link "KubeletConfigEviction" [ref=e3651] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#kubeletconfigeviction"
                  - cell [ref=e3652]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e3653]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e3654]: "EvictionHard describes a set of eviction thresholds (e.g. memory.available<1Gi) that if met would trigger a Pod eviction.Default: memory.available: \"100Mi/1Gi/5%\" nodefs.available: \"5%\" nodefs.inodesFree: \"5%\" imagefs.available: \"5%\" imagefs.inodesFree: \"5%\""
                - row [ref=e3655]:
                  - cell [ref=e3656]:
                    - code [ref=e3657]: evictionMaxPodGracePeriod
                    - emphasis [ref=e3658]: integer
                  - cell [ref=e3659]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e3660]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e3661]: "EvictionMaxPodGracePeriod describes the maximum allowed grace period (in seconds) to use when terminating pods in response to a soft eviction threshold being met.Default: 90"
                - row [ref=e3662]:
                  - cell [ref=e3663]:
                    - code [ref=e3664]: evictionMinimumReclaim
                    - emphasis [ref=e3665]:
                      - link "KubeletConfigEvictionMinimumReclaim" [ref=e3666] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#kubeletconfigevictionminimumreclaim"
                  - cell [ref=e3667]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e3668]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e3669]: "EvictionMinimumReclaim configures the amount of resources below the configured eviction threshold that the kubelet attempts to reclaim whenever the kubelet observes resource pressure.Default: 0 for each resource"
                - row [ref=e3670]:
                  - cell [ref=e3671]:
                    - code [ref=e3672]: evictionPressureTransitionPeriod
                    - emphasis [ref=e3673]:
                      - link "Duration" [ref=e3674] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#duration-v1-meta
                  - cell [ref=e3675]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e3676]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e3677]: "EvictionPressureTransitionPeriod is the duration for which the kubelet has to wait before transitioning out of an eviction pressure condition.Default: 4m0s"
                - row [ref=e3678]:
                  - cell [ref=e3679]:
                    - code [ref=e3680]: evictionSoft
                    - emphasis [ref=e3681]:
                      - link "KubeletConfigEviction" [ref=e3682] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#kubeletconfigeviction"
                  - cell [ref=e3683]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e3684]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e3685]: "EvictionSoft describes a set of eviction thresholds (e.g. memory.available<1.5Gi) that if met over a corresponding grace period would trigger a Pod eviction.Default: memory.available: \"200Mi/1.5Gi/10%\" nodefs.available: \"10%\" nodefs.inodesFree: \"10%\" imagefs.available: \"10%\" imagefs.inodesFree: \"10%\""
                - row [ref=e3686]:
                  - cell [ref=e3687]:
                    - code [ref=e3688]: evictionSoftGracePeriod
                    - emphasis [ref=e3689]:
                      - link "KubeletConfigEvictionSoftGracePeriod" [ref=e3690] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#kubeletconfigevictionsoftgraceperiod"
                  - cell [ref=e3691]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e3692]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e3693]: "EvictionSoftGracePeriod describes a set of eviction grace periods (e.g. memory.available=1m30s) that correspond to how long a soft eviction threshold must hold before triggering a Pod eviction.Default: memory.available: 1m30s nodefs.available: 1m30s nodefs.inodesFree: 1m30s imagefs.available: 1m30s imagefs.inodesFree: 1m30s"
                - row [ref=e3694]:
                  - cell [ref=e3695]:
                    - code [ref=e3696]: maxPods
                    - emphasis [ref=e3697]: integer
                  - cell [ref=e3698]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e3699]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e3700]: "MaxPods is the maximum number of Pods that are allowed by the Kubelet.Default: 110"
                - row [ref=e3701]:
                  - cell [ref=e3702]:
                    - code [ref=e3703]: podPidsLimit
                    - emphasis [ref=e3704]: integer
                  - cell [ref=e3705]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e3706]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e3707]: PodPIDsLimit is the maximum number of process IDs per pod allowed by the kubelet.
                - row [ref=e3708]:
                  - cell [ref=e3709]:
                    - code [ref=e3710]: failSwapOn
                    - emphasis [ref=e3711]: boolean
                  - cell [ref=e3712]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e3713]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e3714]: FailSwapOn makes the Kubelet fail to start if swap is enabled on the node. (default true).
                - row [ref=e3715]:
                  - cell [ref=e3716]:
                    - code [ref=e3717]: kubeReserved
                    - emphasis [ref=e3718]:
                      - link "KubeletConfigReserved" [ref=e3719] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#kubeletconfigreserved"
                  - cell [ref=e3720]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e3721]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e3722]: "KubeReserved is the configuration for resources reserved for kubernetes node components (mainly kubelet and container runtime).When updating these values, be aware that cgroup resizes may not succeed on active worker nodes. Look for the NodeAllocatableEnforced event to determine if the configuration was applied.Default: cpu=80m,memory=1Gi,pid=20k"
                - row [ref=e3723]:
                  - cell [ref=e3724]:
                    - code [ref=e3725]: imageGCHighThresholdPercent
                    - emphasis [ref=e3726]: integer
                  - cell [ref=e3727]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e3728]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e3729]: "ImageGCHighThresholdPercent describes the percent of the disk usage which triggers image garbage collection.Default: 50"
                - row [ref=e3730]:
                  - cell [ref=e3731]:
                    - code [ref=e3732]: imageGCLowThresholdPercent
                    - emphasis [ref=e3733]: integer
                  - cell [ref=e3734]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e3735]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e3736]: "ImageGCLowThresholdPercent describes the percent of the disk to which garbage collection attempts to free.Default: 40"
                - row [ref=e3737]:
                  - cell [ref=e3738]:
                    - code [ref=e3739]: serializeImagePulls
                    - emphasis [ref=e3740]: boolean
                  - cell [ref=e3741]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e3742]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e3743]: "SerializeImagePulls describes whether the images are pulled one at a time.Default: true"
                - row [ref=e3744]:
                  - cell [ref=e3745]:
                    - code [ref=e3746]: registryPullQPS
                    - emphasis [ref=e3747]: integer
                  - cell [ref=e3748]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e3749]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e3750]: "RegistryPullQPS is the limit of registry pulls per second. The value must not be a negative number.Setting it to 0 means no limit.Default: 5"
                - row [ref=e3751]:
                  - cell [ref=e3752]:
                    - code [ref=e3753]: registryBurst
                    - emphasis [ref=e3754]: integer
                  - cell [ref=e3755]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e3756]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e3757]: "RegistryBurst is the maximum size of bursty pulls, temporarily allows pulls to burst to this number,while still not exceeding registryPullQPS. The value must not be a negative number.Only used if registryPullQPS is greater than 0.Default: 10"
                - row [ref=e3758]:
                  - cell [ref=e3759]:
                    - code [ref=e3760]: seccompDefault
                    - emphasis [ref=e3761]: boolean
                  - cell [ref=e3762]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e3763]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e3764]: "SeccompDefault enables the use of `RuntimeDefault` as the default seccomp profile for all workloads."
                - row [ref=e3765]:
                  - cell [ref=e3766]:
                    - code [ref=e3767]: containerLogMaxSize
                    - emphasis [ref=e3768]:
                      - link "Quantity" [ref=e3769] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#quantity-resource-api
                  - cell [ref=e3770]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e3771]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e3772]: "A quantity defines the maximum size of the container log file before it is rotated. For example: \"5Mi\" or \"256Ki\".Default: 100Mi"
                - row [ref=e3773]:
                  - cell [ref=e3774]:
                    - code [ref=e3775]: containerLogMaxFiles
                    - emphasis [ref=e3776]: integer
                  - cell [ref=e3777]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e3778]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e3779]: Maximum number of container log files that can be present for a container.
                - row [ref=e3780]:
                  - cell [ref=e3781]:
                    - code [ref=e3782]: protectKernelDefaults
                    - emphasis [ref=e3783]: boolean
                  - cell [ref=e3784]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e3785]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e3786]: ProtectKernelDefaults ensures that the kernel tunables are equal to the kubelet defaults.Defaults to true.
                - row [ref=e3787]:
                  - cell [ref=e3788]:
                    - code [ref=e3789]: streamingConnectionIdleTimeout
                    - emphasis [ref=e3790]:
                      - link "Duration" [ref=e3791] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#duration-v1-meta
                  - cell [ref=e3792]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e3793]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e3794]: "StreamingConnectionIdleTimeout is the maximum time a streaming connection can be idle before the connection is automatically closed.This field cannot be set lower than \"30s\" or greater than \"4h\".Default: \"5m\"."
                - row [ref=e3795]:
                  - cell [ref=e3796]:
                    - code [ref=e3797]: memorySwap
                    - emphasis [ref=e3798]:
                      - link "MemorySwapConfiguration" [ref=e3799] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#memoryswapconfiguration"
                  - cell [ref=e3800]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e3801]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e3802]: MemorySwap configures swap memory available to container workloads.
                - row [ref=e3803]:
                  - cell [ref=e3804]:
                    - code [ref=e3805]: maxParallelImagePulls
                    - emphasis [ref=e3806]: integer
                  - cell [ref=e3807]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e3808]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e3809]: "MaxParallelImagePulls describes the maximum number of image pulls in parallel. The value must be a positive number.This field cannot be set if SerializeImagePulls (pull one image at a time) is set to true.Setting it to nil means no limit.Default: nil"
                - row [ref=e3810]:
                  - cell [ref=e3811]:
                    - code [ref=e3812]: imageMinimumGCAge
                    - emphasis [ref=e3813]:
                      - link "Duration" [ref=e3814] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#duration-v1-meta
                  - cell [ref=e3815]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e3816]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e3817]: "ImageMinimumGCAge is the minimum age of an unused image before it can be garbage collected.Default: 2m0s"
                - row [ref=e3818]:
                  - cell [ref=e3819]:
                    - code [ref=e3820]: imageMaximumGCAge
                    - emphasis [ref=e3821]:
                      - link "Duration" [ref=e3822] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#duration-v1-meta
                  - cell [ref=e3823]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e3824]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e3825]: "ImageMaximumGCAge is the maximum age of an unused image before it can be garbage collected.Default: 0s"
                - row [ref=e3826]:
                  - cell [ref=e3827]:
                    - code [ref=e3828]: singleProcessOOMKill
                    - emphasis [ref=e3829]: boolean
                  - cell [ref=e3830]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e3831]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e3832]: "SingleProcessOOMKill, if true, will prevent the `memory.oom.group` flag from being set for containercgroups in cgroups v2. This causes processes in the container to be OOM killed individually instead ofas a group. It means that if true, the behavior aligns with the behavior of cgroups v1."
                - row [ref=e3833]:
                  - cell [ref=e3834]:
                    - code [ref=e3835]: imagePullCredentialsVerificationPolicy
                    - emphasis [ref=e3836]:
                      - link "ImagePullCredentialsVerificationPolicy" [ref=e3837] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#imagepullcredentialsverificationpolicy"
                  - cell [ref=e3838]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e3839]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e3840]: "ImagePullCredentialsVerificationPolicy determines how credentials should be verified when pulling images thatalready exist on the node. It corresponds to the kubelet's `imagePullCredentialsVerificationPolicy` field and is onlyeffective for Kubernetes versions >= 1.35. May be one of {\"NeverVerify\", \"NeverVerifyPreloadedImages\",\"NeverVerifyAllowlistedImages\", \"AlwaysVerify\"}. Defaults to \"NeverVerifyPreloadedImages\" (the kubelet default)."
                - row [ref=e3841]:
                  - cell [ref=e3842]:
                    - code [ref=e3843]: preloadedImagesVerificationAllowlist
                    - emphasis [ref=e3844]: string array
                  - cell [ref=e3845]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e3846]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e3847]: "PreloadedImagesVerificationAllowlist specifies a list of images that are exempted from credentialre-verification for the \"NeverVerifyAllowlistedImages\" ImagePullCredentialsVerificationPolicy. The list accepts afull path segment wildcard suffix \"/*\". Only image specs without an image tag or digest must be used. Itcorresponds to the kubelet's `preloadedImagesVerificationAllowlist` field and is only effective for Kubernetes versions>= 1.35."
            - heading "KubeletConfigEviction" [level=3] [ref=e3848]
            - paragraph [ref=e3849]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e3850]: "Appears on:"
              - link "KubeletConfig" [ref=e3851] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#kubeletconfig"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e3852]: KubeletConfigEviction contains kubelet eviction thresholds supporting either a resource.Quantity or a percentage based value.
            - table [ref=e3853]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e3854]:
                - row [ref=e3855]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e3856]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e3857]
              - rowgroup [ref=e3858]:
                - row [ref=e3859]:
                  - cell [ref=e3860]:
                    - code [ref=e3861]: memoryAvailable
                    - emphasis [ref=e3862]: string
                  - cell [ref=e3863]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e3864]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e3865]: MemoryAvailable is the threshold for the free memory on the host server.
                - row [ref=e3866]:
                  - cell [ref=e3867]:
                    - code [ref=e3868]: imageFSAvailable
                    - emphasis [ref=e3869]: string
                  - cell [ref=e3870]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e3871]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e3872]: ImageFSAvailable is the threshold for the free disk space in the imagefs filesystem (docker images and container writable layers).
                - row [ref=e3873]:
                  - cell [ref=e3874]:
                    - code [ref=e3875]: imageFSInodesFree
                    - emphasis [ref=e3876]: string
                  - cell [ref=e3877]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e3878]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e3879]: ImageFSInodesFree is the threshold for the available inodes in the imagefs filesystem.
                - row [ref=e3880]:
                  - cell [ref=e3881]:
                    - code [ref=e3882]: nodeFSAvailable
                    - emphasis [ref=e3883]: string
                  - cell [ref=e3884]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e3885]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e3886]: NodeFSAvailable is the threshold for the free disk space in the nodefs filesystem (docker volumes, logs, etc).
                - row [ref=e3887]:
                  - cell [ref=e3888]:
                    - code [ref=e3889]: nodeFSInodesFree
                    - emphasis [ref=e3890]: string
                  - cell [ref=e3891]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e3892]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e3893]: NodeFSInodesFree is the threshold for the available inodes in the nodefs filesystem.
            - heading "KubeletConfigEvictionMinimumReclaim" [level=3] [ref=e3894]
            - paragraph [ref=e3895]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e3896]: "Appears on:"
              - link "KubeletConfig" [ref=e3897] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#kubeletconfig"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e3898]: KubeletConfigEvictionMinimumReclaim contains configuration for the kubelet eviction minimum reclaim.
            - table [ref=e3899]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e3900]:
                - row [ref=e3901]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e3902]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e3903]
              - rowgroup [ref=e3904]:
                - row [ref=e3905]:
                  - cell [ref=e3906]:
                    - code [ref=e3907]: memoryAvailable
                    - emphasis [ref=e3908]:
                      - link "Quantity" [ref=e3909] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#quantity-resource-api
                  - cell [ref=e3910]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e3911]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e3912]: MemoryAvailable is the threshold for the memory reclaim on the host server.
                - row [ref=e3913]:
                  - cell [ref=e3914]:
                    - code [ref=e3915]: imageFSAvailable
                    - emphasis [ref=e3916]:
                      - link "Quantity" [ref=e3917] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#quantity-resource-api
                  - cell [ref=e3918]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e3919]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e3920]: ImageFSAvailable is the threshold for the disk space reclaim in the imagefs filesystem (docker images and container writable layers).
                - row [ref=e3921]:
                  - cell [ref=e3922]:
                    - code [ref=e3923]: imageFSInodesFree
                    - emphasis [ref=e3924]:
                      - link "Quantity" [ref=e3925] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#quantity-resource-api
                  - cell [ref=e3926]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e3927]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e3928]: ImageFSInodesFree is the threshold for the inodes reclaim in the imagefs filesystem.
                - row [ref=e3929]:
                  - cell [ref=e3930]:
                    - code [ref=e3931]: nodeFSAvailable
                    - emphasis [ref=e3932]:
                      - link "Quantity" [ref=e3933] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#quantity-resource-api
                  - cell [ref=e3934]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e3935]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e3936]: NodeFSAvailable is the threshold for the disk space reclaim in the nodefs filesystem (docker volumes, logs, etc).
                - row [ref=e3937]:
                  - cell [ref=e3938]:
                    - code [ref=e3939]: nodeFSInodesFree
                    - emphasis [ref=e3940]:
                      - link "Quantity" [ref=e3941] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#quantity-resource-api
                  - cell [ref=e3942]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e3943]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e3944]: NodeFSInodesFree is the threshold for the inodes reclaim in the nodefs filesystem.
            - heading "KubeletConfigEvictionSoftGracePeriod" [level=3] [ref=e3945]
            - paragraph [ref=e3946]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e3947]: "Appears on:"
              - link "KubeletConfig" [ref=e3948] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#kubeletconfig"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e3949]: KubeletConfigEvictionSoftGracePeriod contains grace periods for kubelet eviction thresholds.
            - table [ref=e3950]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e3951]:
                - row [ref=e3952]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e3953]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e3954]
              - rowgroup [ref=e3955]:
                - row [ref=e3956]:
                  - cell [ref=e3957]:
                    - code [ref=e3958]: memoryAvailable
                    - emphasis [ref=e3959]:
                      - link "Duration" [ref=e3960] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#duration-v1-meta
                  - cell [ref=e3961]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e3962]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e3963]: MemoryAvailable is the grace period for the MemoryAvailable eviction threshold.
                - row [ref=e3964]:
                  - cell [ref=e3965]:
                    - code [ref=e3966]: imageFSAvailable
                    - emphasis [ref=e3967]:
                      - link "Duration" [ref=e3968] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#duration-v1-meta
                  - cell [ref=e3969]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e3970]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e3971]: ImageFSAvailable is the grace period for the ImageFSAvailable eviction threshold.
                - row [ref=e3972]:
                  - cell [ref=e3973]:
                    - code [ref=e3974]: imageFSInodesFree
                    - emphasis [ref=e3975]:
                      - link "Duration" [ref=e3976] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#duration-v1-meta
                  - cell [ref=e3977]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e3978]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e3979]: ImageFSInodesFree is the grace period for the ImageFSInodesFree eviction threshold.
                - row [ref=e3980]:
                  - cell [ref=e3981]:
                    - code [ref=e3982]: nodeFSAvailable
                    - emphasis [ref=e3983]:
                      - link "Duration" [ref=e3984] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#duration-v1-meta
                  - cell [ref=e3985]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e3986]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e3987]: NodeFSAvailable is the grace period for the NodeFSAvailable eviction threshold.
                - row [ref=e3988]:
                  - cell [ref=e3989]:
                    - code [ref=e3990]: nodeFSInodesFree
                    - emphasis [ref=e3991]:
                      - link "Duration" [ref=e3992] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#duration-v1-meta
                  - cell [ref=e3993]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e3994]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e3995]: NodeFSInodesFree is the grace period for the NodeFSInodesFree eviction threshold.

            - heading "Kubernetes" [level=3] [ref=e4039]

              - text: (

              - link "ShootSpec" [ref=e4042] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#shootspec"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e4043]: Kubernetes contains the version and configuration variables for the Shoot control plane.
            - table [ref=e4044]:

                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e4047]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e4048]

                  - cell [ref=e4055]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e4056]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e4057]: ClusterAutoscaler contains the configuration flags for the Kubernetes cluster autoscaler.
                - row [ref=e4058]:
                  - cell [ref=e4059]:

                    - emphasis [ref=e4072]: (Optional)

                - row [ref=e4074]:
                  - cell [ref=e4075]:

                    - emphasis [ref=e4077]:
                      - link "KubeSchedulerConfig" [ref=e4078] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#kubeschedulerconfig"
                  - cell [ref=e4079]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e4080]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e4081]: KubeScheduler contains configuration settings for the kube-scheduler.
                - row [ref=e4082]:

                    - paragraph [ref=e4089]: KubeProxy contains configuration settings for the kube-proxy.

                    - paragraph [ref=e4097]: Kubelet contains configuration settings for the kubelet.
                - row [ref=e4098]:
                  - cell [ref=e4099]:
                    - code [ref=e4100]: version
                    - emphasis [ref=e4101]: string
                  - 'cell "(Optional) Version is the semantic Kubernetes version to use for the Shoot cluster. Defaults to the highest supported minor and patch version given in the referenced cloud profile. The version can be omitted completely or partially specified, e.g. `.`." [ref=e4102]':
                    - emphasis [ref=e4103]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e4104]:
                      - text: "Version is the semantic Kubernetes version to use for the Shoot cluster.Defaults to the highest supported minor and patch version given in the referenced cloud profile.The version can be omitted completely or partially specified, e.g. `"
                      - generic [ref=e4105]: ".`."

                  - cell [ref=e4111]:

                    - paragraph [ref=e4113]: VerticalPodAutoscaler contains the configuration flags for the Kubernetes vertical pod autoscaler.

                      - link "ETCD" [ref=e4118] [cursor=pointer]:

                  - cell [ref=e4119]:

            - heading "KubernetesConfig" [level=3] [ref=e4122]
            - paragraph [ref=e4123]:
              - text: (

              - text: ","

              - text: ","
              - link "KubeletConfig" [ref=e4129] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#kubeletconfig"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e4130]: KubernetesConfig contains common configuration fields for the control plane components. This is a legacy type that should not be used in new API fields or resources. Instead of embedding this type, consider using inline map for feature gates definitions.
            - table [ref=e4131]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e4132]:

                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e4134]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e4135]

                    - emphasis [ref=e4140]: object (keys:string, values:boolean)
                  - cell [ref=e4141]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e4142]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e4143]: FeatureGates contains information about enabled feature gates.
            - heading "KubernetesDashboard" [level=3] [ref=e4144]

                - /url: "#addons"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e4148]: KubernetesDashboard describes configuration values for the kubernetes-dashboard addon.
            - table [ref=e4149]:

                - row [ref=e4151]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e4152]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e4153]
              - rowgroup [ref=e4154]:
                - row [ref=e4155]:
                  - cell [ref=e4156]:

                    - emphasis [ref=e4158]: boolean
                  - cell [ref=e4159]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e4160]: Enabled indicates whether the addon is enabled or not.
                - row [ref=e4161]:
                  - cell [ref=e4162]:
                    - code [ref=e4163]: authenticationMode
                    - emphasis [ref=e4164]: string
                  - cell [ref=e4165]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e4166]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e4167]: AuthenticationMode defines the authentication mode for the kubernetes-dashboard.
            - heading "KubernetesSettings" [level=3] [ref=e4168]
            - paragraph [ref=e4169]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e4170]: "Appears on:"
              - link "CloudProfileSpec" [ref=e4171] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#cloudprofilespec"
              - text: ","
              - link "NamespacedCloudProfileSpec" [ref=e4172] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#namespacedcloudprofilespec"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e4173]: KubernetesSettings contains constraints regarding allowed values of the 'kubernetes' block in the Shoot specification.
            - table [ref=e4174]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e4175]:
                - row [ref=e4176]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e4177]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e4178]
              - rowgroup [ref=e4179]:
                - row [ref=e4180]:
                  - cell [ref=e4181]:

                      - link "ExpirableVersion" [ref=e4184] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#expirableversion"
                      - text: array
                  - cell [ref=e4185]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e4186]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e4187]: Versions is the list of allowed Kubernetes versions with optional expiration dates for Shoot clusters.
            - heading "KubernetesStatus" [level=3] [ref=e4188]
            - paragraph [ref=e4189]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e4190]: "Appears on:"
              - link "CloudProfileStatus" [ref=e4191] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#cloudprofilestatus"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e4192]: KubernetesStatus contains the status information for kubernetes.
            - table [ref=e4193]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e4194]:
                - row [ref=e4195]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e4196]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e4197]
              - rowgroup [ref=e4198]:
                - row [ref=e4199]:
                  - cell [ref=e4200]:
                    - code [ref=e4201]: versions
                    - emphasis [ref=e4202]:
                      - link "ExpirableVersionStatus" [ref=e4203] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#expirableversionstatus"
                      - text: array
                  - cell [ref=e4204]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e4205]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e4206]: Versions contains the statuses of the kubernetes versions.
            - heading "LastError" [level=3] [ref=e4207]
            - paragraph [ref=e4208]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e4209]: "Appears on:"
              - link "BackupBucketStatus" [ref=e4210] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#backupbucketstatus"
              - text: ","
              - link "BackupEntryStatus" [ref=e4211] [cursor=pointer]:

              - text: ","
              - link "ShootStatus" [ref=e4212] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#shootstatus"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e4213]: LastError indicates the last occurred error for an operation on a resource.
            - table [ref=e4214]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e4215]:
                - row [ref=e4216]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e4217]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e4218]

                - row [ref=e4220]:
                  - cell [ref=e4221]:
                    - code [ref=e4222]: description
                    - emphasis [ref=e4223]: string
                  - cell [ref=e4224]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e4225]: A human readable message indicating details about the last error.
                - row [ref=e4226]:
                  - cell [ref=e4227]:
                    - code [ref=e4228]: taskID
                    - emphasis [ref=e4229]: string
                  - cell [ref=e4230]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e4231]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e4232]: ID of the task which caused this last error

                  - cell [ref=e4234]:

                    - emphasis [ref=e4236]:
                      - link "ErrorCode" [ref=e4237] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#errorcode"
                      - text: array
                  - cell [ref=e4238]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e4239]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e4240]: Well-defined error codes of the last error(s).
                - row [ref=e4241]:
                  - cell [ref=e4242]:
                    - code [ref=e4243]: lastUpdateTime
                    - emphasis [ref=e4244]:
                      - link "Time" [ref=e4245] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#time-v1-meta
                  - cell [ref=e4246]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e4247]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e4248]: Last time the error was reported
            - heading "LastMaintenance" [level=3] [ref=e4249]
            - paragraph [ref=e4250]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e4251]: "Appears on:"
              - link "ShootStatus" [ref=e4252] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#shootstatus"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e4253]: LastMaintenance holds information about a maintenance operation on the Shoot.
            - table [ref=e4254]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e4255]:
                - row [ref=e4256]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e4257]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e4258]
              - rowgroup [ref=e4259]:
                - row [ref=e4260]:
                  - cell [ref=e4261]:
                    - code [ref=e4262]: description
                    - emphasis [ref=e4263]: string
                  - cell [ref=e4264]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e4265]: A human-readable message containing details about the operations performed in the last maintenance.
                - row [ref=e4266]:
                  - cell [ref=e4267]:
                    - code [ref=e4268]: triggeredTime
                    - emphasis [ref=e4269]:
                      - link "Time" [ref=e4270] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#time-v1-meta
                  - cell [ref=e4271]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e4272]: TriggeredTime is the time when maintenance was triggered.
                - row [ref=e4273]:
                  - cell [ref=e4274]:
                    - code [ref=e4275]: state
                    - emphasis [ref=e4276]:
                      - link "LastOperationState" [ref=e4277] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#lastoperationstate"
                  - cell [ref=e4278]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e4279]: Status of the last maintenance operation, one of Processing, Succeeded, Error.
                - row [ref=e4280]:
                  - cell [ref=e4281]:
                    - code [ref=e4282]: failureReason
                    - emphasis [ref=e4283]: string
                  - cell [ref=e4284]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e4285]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e4286]: FailureReason holds the information about the last maintenance operation failure reason.
            - heading "LastOperation" [level=3] [ref=e4287]
            - paragraph [ref=e4288]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e4289]: "Appears on:"
              - link "BackupBucketStatus" [ref=e4290] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#backupbucketstatus"
              - text: ","
              - link "BackupEntryStatus" [ref=e4291] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#backupentrystatus"
              - text: ","
              - link "SeedStatus" [ref=e4292] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#seedstatus"
              - text: ","
              - link "ShootStatus" [ref=e4293] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#shootstatus"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e4294]: LastOperation indicates the type and the state of the last operation, along with a description message and a progress indicator.
            - table [ref=e4295]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e4296]:
                - row [ref=e4297]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e4298]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e4299]
              - rowgroup [ref=e4300]:
                - row [ref=e4301]:
                  - cell [ref=e4302]:
                    - code [ref=e4303]: description
                    - emphasis [ref=e4304]: string
                  - cell [ref=e4305]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e4306]: A human readable message indicating details about the last operation.
                - row [ref=e4307]:
                  - cell [ref=e4308]:
                    - code [ref=e4309]: lastUpdateTime
                    - emphasis [ref=e4310]:
                      - link "Time" [ref=e4311] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#time-v1-meta
                  - cell [ref=e4312]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e4313]: Last time the operation state transitioned from one to another.
                - row [ref=e4314]:
                  - cell [ref=e4315]:
                    - code [ref=e4316]: progress
                    - emphasis [ref=e4317]: integer
                  - cell [ref=e4318]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e4319]: The progress in percentage (0-100) of the last operation.
                - row [ref=e4320]:
                  - cell [ref=e4321]:
                    - code [ref=e4322]: state
                    - emphasis [ref=e4323]:
                      - link "LastOperationState" [ref=e4324] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#lastoperationstate"
                  - cell [ref=e4325]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e4326]: Status of the last operation, one of Aborted, Processing, Succeeded, Error, Failed.
                - row [ref=e4327]:
                  - cell [ref=e4328]:
                    - code [ref=e4329]: type
                    - emphasis [ref=e4330]:
                      - link "LastOperationType" [ref=e4331] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#lastoperationtype"
                  - cell [ref=e4332]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e4333]: Type of the last operation, one of Create, Reconcile, Delete, Migrate, Restore.
            - heading "LastOperationState" [level=3] [ref=e4334]
            - paragraph [ref=e4335]:
              - emphasis [ref=e4336]: "Underlying type: string"
            - paragraph [ref=e4337]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e4338]: "Appears on:"
              - link "LastMaintenance" [ref=e4339] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#lastmaintenance"
              - text: ","
              - link "LastOperation" [ref=e4340] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#lastoperation"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e4341]: LastOperationState is a string alias.
            - heading "LastOperationType" [level=3] [ref=e4342]
            - paragraph [ref=e4343]:
              - emphasis [ref=e4344]: "Underlying type: string"
            - paragraph [ref=e4345]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e4346]: "Appears on:"
              - link "LastOperation" [ref=e4347] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#lastoperation"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e4348]: LastOperationType is a string alias.
            - heading "LifecycleStage" [level=3] [ref=e4349]
            - paragraph [ref=e4350]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e4351]: "Appears on:"
              - link "ExpirableVersion" [ref=e4352] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#expirableversion"
              - text: ","
              - link "MachineImageVersion" [ref=e4353] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#machineimageversion"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e4354]: LifecycleStage describes a stage in the versions lifecycle. Each stage defines the classification of the version (e.g. unavailable, preview, supported, deprecated, expired) and the time at which this classification becomes effective.
            - table [ref=e4355]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e4356]:
                - row [ref=e4357]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e4358]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e4359]
              - rowgroup [ref=e4360]:
                - row [ref=e4361]:
                  - cell [ref=e4362]:
                    - code [ref=e4363]: classification
                    - emphasis [ref=e4364]:
                      - link "VersionClassification" [ref=e4365] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#versionclassification"
                  - cell [ref=e4366]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e4367]: Classification is the category of this lifecycle stage (unavailable, preview, supported, deprecated, expired).
                - row [ref=e4368]:
                  - cell [ref=e4369]:
                    - code [ref=e4370]: startTime
                    - emphasis [ref=e4371]:
                      - link "Time" [ref=e4372] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#time-v1-meta
                  - cell [ref=e4373]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e4374]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e4375]: StartTime defines when this lifecycle stage becomes active.StartTime can be omitted for the first lifecycle stage, implying a start time in the past.
            - heading "Limits" [level=3] [ref=e4376]
            - paragraph [ref=e4377]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e4378]: "Appears on:"
              - link "CloudProfileSpec" [ref=e4379] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#cloudprofilespec"
              - text: ","
              - link "NamespacedCloudProfileSpec" [ref=e4380] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#namespacedcloudprofilespec"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e4381]: Limits configures operational limits for Shoot clusters using this CloudProfile. See https://github.com/gardener/gardener/blob/master/docs/usage/shoot/shoot_limits.md.
            - table [ref=e4382]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e4383]:
                - row [ref=e4384]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e4385]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e4386]
              - rowgroup [ref=e4387]:
                - row [ref=e4388]:
                  - cell [ref=e4389]:
                    - code [ref=e4390]: maxNodesTotal
                    - emphasis [ref=e4391]: integer
                  - cell [ref=e4392]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e4393]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e4394]: MaxNodesTotal configures the maximum node count a Shoot cluster can have during runtime.
            - heading "LiveMigration" [level=3] [ref=e4395]
            - paragraph [ref=e4396]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e4397]: "Appears on:"
              - link "ShootStatus" [ref=e4398] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#shootstatus"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e4399]: LiveMigration contains information about an ongoing live migration of the Shoot control plane.
            - table [ref=e4400]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e4401]:
                - row [ref=e4402]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e4403]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e4404]
              - rowgroup [ref=e4405]:
                - row [ref=e4406]:
                  - cell [ref=e4407]:
                    - code [ref=e4408]: conditions
                    - emphasis [ref=e4409]:
                      - link "Condition" [ref=e4410] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#condition"
                      - text: array
                  - cell [ref=e4411]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e4412]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e4413]: Conditions represents the progress of the live migration, one condition per migration step.
            - heading "LoadBalancerServicesProxyProtocol" [level=3] [ref=e4414]
            - paragraph [ref=e4415]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e4416]: "Appears on:"
              - link "SeedSettingLoadBalancerServices" [ref=e4417] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#seedsettingloadbalancerservices"
              - text: ","
              - link "SeedSettingLoadBalancerServicesZones" [ref=e4418] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#seedsettingloadbalancerserviceszones"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e4419]: LoadBalancerServicesProxyProtocol controls whether ProxyProtocol is (optionally) allowed for the load balancer services.
            - table [ref=e4420]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e4421]:
                - row [ref=e4422]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e4423]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e4424]
              - rowgroup [ref=e4425]:
                - row [ref=e4426]:
                  - cell [ref=e4427]:
                    - code [ref=e4428]: allowed
                    - emphasis [ref=e4429]: boolean
                  - cell [ref=e4430]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e4431]: Allowed controls whether the ProxyProtocol is optionally allowed for the load balancer services.This should only be enabled if the load balancer services are already using ProxyProtocol or will be reconfigured to use it soon.Until the load balancers are configured with ProxyProtocol, enabling this setting may allow clients to spoof their source IP addresses.The option allows a migration from non-ProxyProtocol to ProxyProtocol without downtime (depending on the infrastructure).Defaults to false.
            - heading "Machine" [level=3] [ref=e4432]
            - paragraph [ref=e4433]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e4434]: "Appears on:"
              - link "Worker" [ref=e4435] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#worker"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e4436]: Machine contains information about the machine type and image.
            - table [ref=e4437]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e4438]:
                - row [ref=e4439]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e4440]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e4441]
              - rowgroup [ref=e4442]:
                - row [ref=e4443]:
                  - cell [ref=e4444]:
                    - code [ref=e4445]: type
                    - emphasis [ref=e4446]: string
                  - cell [ref=e4447]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e4448]: Type is the machine type of the worker group.
                - row [ref=e4449]:
                  - cell [ref=e4450]:
                    - code [ref=e4451]: image
                    - emphasis [ref=e4452]:
                      - link "ShootMachineImage" [ref=e4453] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#shootmachineimage"
                  - cell [ref=e4454]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e4455]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e4456]: Image holds information about the machine image to use for all nodes of this pool. It will default to thelatest version of the first image stated in the referenced CloudProfile if no value has been provided.
                - row [ref=e4457]:
                  - cell [ref=e4458]:
                    - code [ref=e4459]: architecture
                    - emphasis [ref=e4460]: string
                  - cell [ref=e4461]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e4462]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e4463]: Architecture is CPU architecture of machines in this worker pool.
            - heading "MachineControllerManagerSettings" [level=3] [ref=e4464]
            - paragraph [ref=e4465]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e4466]: "Appears on:"
              - link "Worker" [ref=e4467] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#worker"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e4468]: MachineControllerManagerSettings contains configurations for different worker-pools. Eg. MachineDrainTimeout, MachineHealthTimeout.
            - table [ref=e4469]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e4470]:
                - row [ref=e4471]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e4472]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e4473]
              - rowgroup [ref=e4474]:
                - row [ref=e4475]:
                  - cell [ref=e4476]:
                    - code [ref=e4477]: machineDrainTimeout
                    - emphasis [ref=e4478]:
                      - link "Duration" [ref=e4479] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#duration-v1-meta
                  - cell [ref=e4480]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e4481]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e4482]: MachineDrainTimeout is the period after which machine is forcefully deleted.
                - row [ref=e4483]:
                  - cell [ref=e4484]:
                    - code [ref=e4485]: machineHealthTimeout
                    - emphasis [ref=e4486]:
                      - link "Duration" [ref=e4487] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#duration-v1-meta
                  - cell [ref=e4488]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e4489]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e4490]: MachineHealthTimeout is the period after which machine is declared failed.
                - row [ref=e4491]:
                  - cell [ref=e4492]:
                    - code [ref=e4493]: machineCreationTimeout
                    - emphasis [ref=e4494]:
                      - link "Duration" [ref=e4495] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#duration-v1-meta
                  - cell [ref=e4496]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e4497]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e4498]: MachineCreationTimeout is the period after which creation of the machine is declared failed.
                - row [ref=e4499]:
                  - cell [ref=e4500]:
                    - code [ref=e4501]: maxEvictRetries
                    - emphasis [ref=e4502]: integer
                  - cell [ref=e4503]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e4504]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e4505]: MaxEvictRetries are the number of eviction retries on a pod after which drain is declared failed, and forceful deletion is triggered.
                - row [ref=e4506]:
                  - cell [ref=e4507]:
                    - code [ref=e4508]: nodeConditions
                    - emphasis [ref=e4509]: string array
                  - cell [ref=e4510]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e4511]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e4512]: NodeConditions are the set of conditions if set to true for the period of MachineHealthTimeout, machine will be declared failed.
                - row [ref=e4513]:
                  - cell [ref=e4514]:
                    - code [ref=e4515]: inPlaceUpdateTimeout
                    - emphasis [ref=e4516]:
                      - link "Duration" [ref=e4517] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#duration-v1-meta
                  - cell [ref=e4518]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e4519]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e4520]: MachineInPlaceUpdateTimeout is the timeout after which in-place update is declared failed.
                - row [ref=e4521]:
                  - cell [ref=e4522]:
                    - code [ref=e4523]: disableHealthTimeout
                    - emphasis [ref=e4524]: boolean
                  - cell [ref=e4525]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e4526]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e4527]: DisableHealthTimeout if set to true, health timeout will be ignored. Leading to machine never being declared failed.This is intended to be used only for in-place updates.
                - row [ref=e4528]:
                  - cell [ref=e4529]:
                    - code [ref=e4530]: machinePreserveTimeout
                    - emphasis [ref=e4531]:
                      - link "Duration" [ref=e4532] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#duration-v1-meta
                  - cell [ref=e4533]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e4534]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e4535]: MachinePreserveTimeout defines the duration after which machine preservation is disabled.If preservation is disabled while the machine is in the Failed phase, the machine transitionsto the Terminating phase. For machines in any other phase, disabling preservation does notalter the current phase, and normal behavior and phase transitions continue as usual.However, the Cluster Autoscaler (CA) may scale down the machine if required.
                - row [ref=e4536]:
                  - cell [ref=e4537]:
                    - code [ref=e4538]: autoPreserveFailedMachineMax
                    - emphasis [ref=e4539]: integer
                  - cell [ref=e4540]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e4541]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e4542]: AutoPreserveFailedMachineMax is the maximum number of machines that can be auto-preserved by MCM for the worker pool.This value is distributed across zones like Minimum and Maximum.
            - heading "MachineImage" [level=3] [ref=e4543]
            - paragraph [ref=e4544]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e4545]: "Appears on:"
              - link "CloudProfileSpec" [ref=e4546] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#cloudprofilespec"
              - text: ","
              - link "NamespacedCloudProfileSpec" [ref=e4547] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#namespacedcloudprofilespec"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e4548]: MachineImage defines the name and multiple versions of the machine image in any environment.
            - table [ref=e4549]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e4550]:
                - row [ref=e4551]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e4552]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e4553]
              - rowgroup [ref=e4554]:
                - row [ref=e4555]:
                  - cell [ref=e4556]:
                    - code [ref=e4557]: name
                    - emphasis [ref=e4558]: string
                  - cell [ref=e4559]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e4560]: Name is the name of the image.
                - row [ref=e4561]:
                  - cell [ref=e4562]:
                    - code [ref=e4563]: versions
                    - emphasis [ref=e4564]:
                      - link "MachineImageVersion" [ref=e4565] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#machineimageversion"
                      - text: array
                  - cell [ref=e4566]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e4567]: Versions contains versions, expiration dates and container runtimes of the machine image
                - row [ref=e4568]:
                  - cell [ref=e4569]:
                    - code [ref=e4570]: updateStrategy
                    - emphasis [ref=e4571]:
                      - link "MachineImageUpdateStrategy" [ref=e4572] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#machineimageupdatestrategy"
                  - cell [ref=e4573]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e4574]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e4575]: "UpdateStrategy is the update strategy to use for the machine image. Possible values are: - patch: update to the latest patch version of the current minor version. - minor: update to the latest minor and patch version. - major: always update to the overall latest version (default)."
            - heading "MachineImageFlavor" [level=3] [ref=e4576]
            - paragraph [ref=e4577]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e4578]: "Appears on:"
              - link "MachineImageVersion" [ref=e4579] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#machineimageversion"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e4580]: MachineImageFlavor is a wrapper for Capabilities. This is a workaround as the Protobuf generator can't handle a slice of maps.
            - heading "MachineImageStatus" [level=3] [ref=e4581]
            - paragraph [ref=e4582]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e4583]: "Appears on:"
              - link "CloudProfileStatus" [ref=e4584] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#cloudprofilestatus"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e4585]: MachineImageStatus contains the status of a machine image and its version classifications.
            - table [ref=e4586]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e4587]:
                - row [ref=e4588]:
