                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e4589]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e4590]
              - rowgroup [ref=e4591]:
                - row [ref=e4592]:
                  - cell [ref=e4593]:
                    - code [ref=e4594]: name
                    - emphasis [ref=e4595]: string
                  - cell [ref=e4596]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e4597]: Name matches the name of the MachineImage the status is represented of.
                - row [ref=e4598]:
                  - cell [ref=e4599]:
                    - code [ref=e4600]: versions
                    - emphasis [ref=e4601]:
                      - link "ExpirableVersionStatus" [ref=e4602] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#expirableversionstatus"
                      - text: array
                  - cell [ref=e4603]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e4604]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e4605]: Versions contains the statuses of the machine image versions.
            - heading "MachineImageUpdateStrategy" [level=3] [ref=e4606]
            - paragraph [ref=e4607]:
              - emphasis [ref=e4608]: "Underlying type: string"
            - paragraph [ref=e4609]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e4610]: "Appears on:"
              - link "MachineImage" [ref=e4611] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#machineimage"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e4612]: MachineImageUpdateStrategy is the update strategy to use for a machine image
            - heading "MachineImageVersion" [level=3] [ref=e4613]
            - paragraph [ref=e4614]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e4615]: "Appears on:"
              - link "MachineImage" [ref=e4616] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#machineimage"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e4617]: MachineImageVersion is an expirable version with list of supported container runtimes and interfaces
            - table [ref=e4618]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e4619]:
                - row [ref=e4620]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e4621]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e4622]
              - rowgroup [ref=e4623]:
                - row [ref=e4624]:
                  - cell [ref=e4625]:
                    - code [ref=e4626]: version
                    - emphasis [ref=e4627]: string
                  - cell [ref=e4628]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e4629]: Version is the version identifier.
                - row [ref=e4630]:
                  - cell [ref=e4631]:
                    - code [ref=e4632]: expirationDate
                    - emphasis [ref=e4633]:
                      - link "Time" [ref=e4634] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#time-v1-meta
                  - cell [ref=e4635]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e4636]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e4637]: "ExpirationDate defines the time at which this version expires.Deprecated: Is replaced by Lifecycle; mutually exclusive with it."
                - row [ref=e4638]:
                  - cell [ref=e4639]:
                    - code [ref=e4640]: classification
                    - emphasis [ref=e4641]:
                      - link "VersionClassification" [ref=e4642] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#versionclassification"
                  - cell [ref=e4643]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e4644]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e4645]: "Classification defines the state of a version (preview, supported, deprecated).Deprecated: Is replaced by Lifecycle. mutually exclusive with it."
                - row [ref=e4646]:
                  - cell [ref=e4647]:
                    - code [ref=e4648]: lifecycle
                    - emphasis [ref=e4649]:
                      - link "LifecycleStage" [ref=e4650] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#lifecyclestage"
                      - text: array
                  - cell [ref=e4651]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e4652]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e4653]: Lifecycle defines the lifecycle stages for this version.Mutually exclusive with Classification and ExpirationDate.This can only be used when the VersionClassificationLifecycle feature gate is enabled.
                - row [ref=e4654]:
                  - cell [ref=e4655]:
                    - code [ref=e4656]: cri
                    - emphasis [ref=e4657]:
                      - link "CRI" [ref=e4658] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#cri"
                      - text: array
                  - cell [ref=e4659]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e4660]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e4661]: CRI list of supported container runtime and interfaces supported by this version
                - row [ref=e4662]:
                  - cell [ref=e4663]:
                    - code [ref=e4664]: architectures
                    - emphasis [ref=e4665]: string array
                  - cell [ref=e4666]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e4667]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e4668]: Architectures is the list of CPU architectures of the machine image in this version.
                - row [ref=e4669]:
                  - cell [ref=e4670]:
                    - code [ref=e4671]: kubeletVersionConstraint
                    - emphasis [ref=e4672]: string
                  - cell [ref=e4673]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e4674]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e4675]: KubeletVersionConstraint is a constraint describing the supported kubelet versions by the machine image in this version.If the field is not specified, it is assumed that the machine image in this version supports all kubelet versions.Examples:- '>= 1.26' - supports only kubelet versions greater than or equal to 1.26- '< 1.26' - supports only kubelet versions less than 1.26
                - row [ref=e4676]:
                  - cell [ref=e4677]:
                    - code [ref=e4678]: inPlaceUpdates
                    - emphasis [ref=e4679]:
                      - link "InPlaceUpdates" [ref=e4680] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#inplaceupdates"
                  - cell [ref=e4681]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e4682]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e4683]: InPlaceUpdates contains the configuration for in-place updates for this machine image version.
                - row [ref=e4684]:
                  - cell [ref=e4685]:
                    - code [ref=e4686]: capabilityFlavors
                    - emphasis [ref=e4687]:
                      - link "MachineImageFlavor" [ref=e4688] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#machineimageflavor"
                      - text: array
                  - cell [ref=e4689]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e4690]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e4691]: CapabilityFlavors is an array of MachineImageFlavor. Each entry represents a combination of capabilities that is provided bythe machine image version.
            - heading "MachineType" [level=3] [ref=e4692]
            - paragraph [ref=e4693]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e4694]: "Appears on:"
              - link "CloudProfileSpec" [ref=e4695] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#cloudprofilespec"
              - text: ","
              - link "NamespacedCloudProfileSpec" [ref=e4696] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#namespacedcloudprofilespec"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e4697]: MachineType contains certain properties of a machine type.
            - table [ref=e4698]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e4699]:
                - row [ref=e4700]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e4701]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e4702]
              - rowgroup [ref=e4703]:
                - row [ref=e4704]:
                  - cell [ref=e4705]:
                    - code [ref=e4706]: cpu
                    - emphasis [ref=e4707]:
                      - link "Quantity" [ref=e4708] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#quantity-resource-api
                  - cell [ref=e4709]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e4710]: CPU is the number of CPUs for this machine type.
                - row [ref=e4711]:
                  - cell [ref=e4712]:
                    - code [ref=e4713]: gpu
                    - emphasis [ref=e4714]:
                      - link "Quantity" [ref=e4715] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#quantity-resource-api
                  - cell [ref=e4716]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e4717]: GPU is the number of GPUs for this machine type.
                - row [ref=e4718]:
                  - cell [ref=e4719]:
                    - code [ref=e4720]: memory
                    - emphasis [ref=e4721]:
                      - link "Quantity" [ref=e4722] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#quantity-resource-api
                  - cell [ref=e4723]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e4724]: Memory is the amount of memory for this machine type.
                - row [ref=e4725]:
                  - cell [ref=e4726]:
                    - code [ref=e4727]: name
                    - emphasis [ref=e4728]: string
                  - cell [ref=e4729]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e4730]: Name is the name of the machine type.
                - row [ref=e4731]:
                  - cell [ref=e4732]:
                    - code [ref=e4733]: storage
                    - emphasis [ref=e4734]:
                      - link "MachineTypeStorage" [ref=e4735] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#machinetypestorage"
                  - cell [ref=e4736]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e4737]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e4738]: Storage is the amount of storage associated with the root volume of this machine type.
                - row [ref=e4739]:
                  - cell [ref=e4740]:
                    - code [ref=e4741]: usable
                    - emphasis [ref=e4742]: boolean
                  - cell [ref=e4743]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e4744]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e4745]: Usable defines if the machine type can be used for shoot clusters.
                - row [ref=e4746]:
                  - cell [ref=e4747]:
                    - code [ref=e4748]: architecture
                    - emphasis [ref=e4749]: string
                  - cell [ref=e4750]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e4751]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e4752]: Architecture is the CPU architecture of this machine type.
                - row [ref=e4753]:
                  - cell [ref=e4754]:
                    - code [ref=e4755]: capabilities
                    - emphasis [ref=e4756]:
                      - link "Capabilities" [ref=e4757] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#capabilities"
                  - cell [ref=e4758]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e4759]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e4760]: Capabilities contains the machine type capabilities.
                - row [ref=e4761]:
                  - cell [ref=e4762]:
                    - code [ref=e4763]: machineControllerManager
                    - emphasis [ref=e4764]:
                      - link "CloudProfileMachineControllerManagerSettings" [ref=e4765] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#cloudprofilemachinecontrollermanagersettings"
                  - cell [ref=e4766]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e4767]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e4768]: MachineControllerManagerSettings contains a subset of the MachineControllerManagerSettings which can be defaulted for a machine type in a CloudProfile.
            - heading "MachineTypeStorage" [level=3] [ref=e4769]
            - paragraph [ref=e4770]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e4771]: "Appears on:"
              - link "MachineType" [ref=e4772] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#machinetype"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e4773]: MachineTypeStorage is the amount of storage associated with the root volume of this machine type.
            - table [ref=e4774]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e4775]:
                - row [ref=e4776]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e4777]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e4778]
              - rowgroup [ref=e4779]:
                - row [ref=e4780]:
                  - cell [ref=e4781]:
                    - code [ref=e4782]: class
                    - emphasis [ref=e4783]: string
                  - cell [ref=e4784]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e4785]: Class is the class of the storage type.
                - row [ref=e4786]:
                  - cell [ref=e4787]:
                    - code [ref=e4788]: size
                    - emphasis [ref=e4789]:
                      - link "Quantity" [ref=e4790] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#quantity-resource-api
                  - cell [ref=e4791]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e4792]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e4793]: StorageSize is the storage size.
                - row [ref=e4794]:
                  - cell [ref=e4795]:
                    - code [ref=e4796]: type
                    - emphasis [ref=e4797]: string
                  - cell [ref=e4798]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e4799]: Type is the type of the storage.
                - row [ref=e4800]:
                  - cell [ref=e4801]:
                    - code [ref=e4802]: minSize
                    - emphasis [ref=e4803]:
                      - link "Quantity" [ref=e4804] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#quantity-resource-api
                  - cell [ref=e4805]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e4806]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e4807]: "MinSize is the minimal supported storage size.This overrides any other common minimum size configuration from `spec.volumeTypes[*].minSize`."
            - heading "MachineUpdateStrategy" [level=3] [ref=e4808]
            - paragraph [ref=e4809]:
              - emphasis [ref=e4810]: "Underlying type: string"
            - paragraph [ref=e4811]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e4812]: "Appears on:"
              - link "Worker" [ref=e4813] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#worker"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e4814]: MachineUpdateStrategy specifies the machine update strategy for the worker pool.
            - heading "Maintenance" [level=3] [ref=e4815]
            - paragraph [ref=e4816]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e4817]: "Appears on:"
              - link "ShootSpec" [ref=e4818] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#shootspec"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e4819]: Maintenance contains information about the time window for maintenance operations and which operations should be performed.
            - table [ref=e4820]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e4821]:
                - row [ref=e4822]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e4823]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e4824]
              - rowgroup [ref=e4825]:
                - row [ref=e4826]:
                  - cell [ref=e4827]:
                    - code [ref=e4828]: autoUpdate
                    - emphasis [ref=e4829]:
                      - link "MaintenanceAutoUpdate" [ref=e4830] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#maintenanceautoupdate"
                  - cell [ref=e4831]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e4832]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e4833]: AutoUpdate contains information about which constraints should be automatically updated.
                - row [ref=e4834]:
                  - cell [ref=e4835]:
                    - code [ref=e4836]: timeWindow
                    - emphasis [ref=e4837]:
                      - link "MaintenanceTimeWindow" [ref=e4838] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#maintenancetimewindow"
                  - cell [ref=e4839]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e4840]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e4841]: TimeWindow contains information about the time window for maintenance operations.
                - row [ref=e4842]:
                  - cell [ref=e4843]:
                    - code [ref=e4844]: confineSpecUpdateRollout
                    - emphasis [ref=e4845]: boolean
                  - cell [ref=e4846]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e4847]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e4848]: ConfineSpecUpdateRollout prevents that changes/updates to the shoot specification will be rolled out immediately.Instead, they are rolled out during the shoot's maintenance time window. There is one exception that will triggeran immediate roll out which is changes to the Spec.Hibernation.Enabled field.
                - row [ref=e4849]:
                  - cell [ref=e4850]:
                    - code [ref=e4851]: autoRotation
                    - emphasis [ref=e4852]:
                      - link "MaintenanceAutoRotation" [ref=e4853] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#maintenanceautorotation"
                  - cell [ref=e4854]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e4855]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e4856]: AutoRotation contains information about which rotations should be automatically performed.
            - heading "MaintenanceAutoRotation" [level=3] [ref=e4857]
            - paragraph [ref=e4858]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e4859]: "Appears on:"
              - link "Maintenance" [ref=e4860] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#maintenance"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e4861]: MaintenanceAutoRotation contains information about which rotations should be automatically performed.
            - table [ref=e4862]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e4863]:
                - row [ref=e4864]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e4865]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e4866]
              - rowgroup [ref=e4867]:
                - row [ref=e4868]:
                  - cell [ref=e4869]:
                    - code [ref=e4870]: credentials
                    - emphasis [ref=e4871]:
                      - link "MaintenanceCredentialsAutoRotation" [ref=e4872] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#maintenancecredentialsautorotation"
                  - cell [ref=e4873]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e4874]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e4875]: Credentials contains information about which credentials should be automatically rotated.
            - heading "MaintenanceAutoUpdate" [level=3] [ref=e4876]
            - paragraph [ref=e4877]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e4878]: "Appears on:"
              - link "Maintenance" [ref=e4879] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#maintenance"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e4880]: MaintenanceAutoUpdate contains information about which constraints should be automatically updated.
            - table [ref=e4881]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e4882]:
                - row [ref=e4883]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e4884]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e4885]
              - rowgroup [ref=e4886]:
                - row [ref=e4887]:
                  - cell [ref=e4888]:
                    - code [ref=e4889]: kubernetesVersion
                    - emphasis [ref=e4890]: boolean
                  - cell [ref=e4891]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e4892]: "KubernetesVersion indicates whether the patch Kubernetes version may be automatically updated (default: true)."
                - row [ref=e4893]:
                  - cell [ref=e4894]:
                    - code [ref=e4895]: machineImageVersion
                    - emphasis [ref=e4896]: boolean
                  - cell [ref=e4897]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e4898]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e4899]: "MachineImageVersion indicates whether the machine image version may be automatically updated (default: true)."
            - heading "MaintenanceCredentialsAutoRotation" [level=3] [ref=e4900]
            - paragraph [ref=e4901]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e4902]: "Appears on:"
              - link "MaintenanceAutoRotation" [ref=e4903] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#maintenanceautorotation"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e4904]: MaintenanceCredentialsAutoRotation contains information about which credentials should be automatically rotated.
            - table [ref=e4905]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e4906]:
                - row [ref=e4907]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e4908]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e4909]
              - rowgroup [ref=e4910]:
                - row [ref=e4911]:
                  - cell [ref=e4912]:
                    - code [ref=e4913]: observability
                    - emphasis [ref=e4914]:
                      - link "MaintenanceRotationConfig" [ref=e4915] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#maintenancerotationconfig"
                  - cell [ref=e4916]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e4917]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e4918]: Observability configures the automatic rotation for the observability credentials.
                - row [ref=e4919]:
                  - cell [ref=e4920]:
                    - code [ref=e4921]: sshKeypair
                    - emphasis [ref=e4922]:
                      - link "MaintenanceRotationConfig" [ref=e4923] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#maintenancerotationconfig"
                  - cell [ref=e4924]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e4925]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e4926]: SSHKeypair configures the automatic rotation for the ssh keypair for worker nodes.
                - row [ref=e4927]:
                  - cell [ref=e4928]:
                    - code [ref=e4929]: etcdEncryptionKey
                    - emphasis [ref=e4930]:
                      - link "MaintenanceRotationConfig" [ref=e4931] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#maintenancerotationconfig"
                  - cell [ref=e4932]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e4933]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e4934]: ETCDEncryptionKey configures the automatic rotation for the etcd encryption key.
            - heading "MaintenanceRotationConfig" [level=3] [ref=e4935]
            - paragraph [ref=e4936]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e4937]: "Appears on:"
              - link "MaintenanceCredentialsAutoRotation" [ref=e4938] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#maintenancecredentialsautorotation"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e4939]: MaintenanceRotationConfig contains configuration for automatic rotation.
            - table [ref=e4940]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e4941]:
                - row [ref=e4942]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e4943]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e4944]
              - rowgroup [ref=e4945]:
                - row [ref=e4946]:
                  - cell [ref=e4947]:
                    - code [ref=e4948]: rotationPeriod
                    - emphasis [ref=e4949]:
                      - link "Duration" [ref=e4950] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#duration-v1-meta
                  - cell [ref=e4951]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e4952]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e4953]: "RotationPeriod is the period between a completed rotation and the start of a new rotation (default: 7d).The allowed rotation period is between 30m and 90d. When set to 0, rotation is disabled."
            - heading "MaintenanceTimeWindow" [level=3] [ref=e4954]
            - paragraph [ref=e4955]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e4956]: "Appears on:"
              - link "Maintenance" [ref=e4957] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#maintenance"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e4958]: MaintenanceTimeWindow contains information about the time window for maintenance operations.
            - table [ref=e4959]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e4960]:
                - row [ref=e4961]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e4962]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e4963]
              - rowgroup [ref=e4964]:
                - row [ref=e4965]:
                  - cell [ref=e4966]:
                    - code [ref=e4967]: begin
                    - emphasis [ref=e4968]: string
                  - cell [ref=e4969]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e4970]: Begin is the beginning of the time window in the format HHMMSS±ZONE, e.g. "220000+0100" or "220000-0500".If not present, a random value will be computed.
                - row [ref=e4971]:
                  - cell [ref=e4972]:
                    - code [ref=e4973]: end
                    - emphasis [ref=e4974]: string
                  - cell [ref=e4975]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e4976]: End is the end of the time window in the format HHMMSS±ZONE, e.g. "220000+0100" or "220000-0500".If not present, the value will be computed based on the "Begin" value.
            - heading "ManualWorkerPoolRollout" [level=3] [ref=e4977]
            - paragraph [ref=e4978]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e4979]: "Appears on:"
              - link "ShootStatus" [ref=e4980] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#shootstatus"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e4981]: ManualWorkerPoolRollout contains information about the worker pool rollout progress that has been initiated via the gardener.cloud/operation=rollout-workers annotation.
            - table [ref=e4982]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e4983]:
                - row [ref=e4984]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e4985]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e4986]
              - rowgroup [ref=e4987]:
                - row [ref=e4988]:
                  - cell [ref=e4989]:
                    - code [ref=e4990]: pendingWorkersRollouts
                    - emphasis [ref=e4991]:
                      - link "PendingWorkersRollout" [ref=e4992] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#pendingworkersrollout"
                      - text: array
                  - cell [ref=e4993]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e4994]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e4995]: PendingWorkersRollouts contains the names of the worker pools that are still pending rollout.
            - heading "MemorySwapConfiguration" [level=3] [ref=e4996]
            - paragraph [ref=e4997]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e4998]: "Appears on:"
              - link "KubeletConfig" [ref=e4999] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#kubeletconfig"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e5000]: "MemorySwapConfiguration contains kubelet swap configuration For more information, please see KEP: 2400-node-swap"
            - table [ref=e5001]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e5002]:
                - row [ref=e5003]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e5004]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e5005]
              - rowgroup [ref=e5006]:
                - row [ref=e5007]:
                  - cell [ref=e5008]:
                    - code [ref=e5009]: swapBehavior
                    - emphasis [ref=e5010]:
                      - link "SwapBehavior" [ref=e5011] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#swapbehavior"
                  - cell [ref=e5012]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e5013]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e5014]: "SwapBehavior configures swap memory available to container workloads. May be one of {\"NoSwap\", \"LimitedSwap\"}defaults to: LimitedSwap"
            - heading "Monitoring" [level=3] [ref=e5015]
            - paragraph [ref=e5016]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e5017]: "Appears on:"
              - link "ShootSpec" [ref=e5018] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#shootspec"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e5019]: Monitoring contains information about the monitoring configuration for the shoot.
            - table [ref=e5020]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e5021]:
                - row [ref=e5022]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e5023]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e5024]
              - rowgroup [ref=e5025]:
                - row [ref=e5026]:
                  - cell [ref=e5027]:
                    - code [ref=e5028]: alerting
                    - emphasis [ref=e5029]:
                      - link "Alerting" [ref=e5030] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#alerting"
                  - cell [ref=e5031]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e5032]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e5033]: Alerting contains information about the alerting configuration for the shoot cluster.
            - heading "NamedResourceReference" [level=3] [ref=e5034]
            - paragraph [ref=e5035]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e5036]: "Appears on:"
              - link "ExtensionResourceState" [ref=e5037] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#extensionresourcestate"
              - text: ","
              - link "SeedSpec" [ref=e5038] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#seedspec"
              - text: ","
              - link "ShootSpec" [ref=e5039] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#shootspec"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e5040]: NamedResourceReference is a named reference to a resource.
            - table [ref=e5041]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e5042]:
                - row [ref=e5043]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e5044]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e5045]
              - rowgroup [ref=e5046]:
                - row [ref=e5047]:
                  - cell [ref=e5048]:
                    - code [ref=e5049]: name
                    - emphasis [ref=e5050]: string
                  - cell [ref=e5051]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e5052]: Name of the resource reference.
                - row [ref=e5053]:
                  - cell [ref=e5054]:
                    - code [ref=e5055]: resourceRef
                    - emphasis [ref=e5056]:
                      - link "CrossVersionObjectReference" [ref=e5057] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#crossversionobjectreference-v1-autoscaling
                  - cell [ref=e5058]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e5059]: ResourceRef is a reference to a resource.
            - heading "NamespacedCloudProfile" [level=3] [ref=e5060]
            - paragraph [ref=e5061]: NamespacedCloudProfile represents certain properties about a provider environment.
            - table [ref=e5062]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e5063]:
                - row [ref=e5064]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e5065]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e5066]
              - rowgroup [ref=e5067]:
                - row [ref=e5068]:
                  - cell [ref=e5069]:
                    - code [ref=e5070]: metadata
                    - emphasis [ref=e5071]:
                      - link "ObjectMeta" [ref=e5072] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#objectmeta-v1-meta
                  - cell [ref=e5073]:
                    - text: Refer to the Kubernetes API documentation for the fields of the
                    - code [ref=e5074]: metadata
                    - text: field.
                - row [ref=e5075]:
                  - cell [ref=e5076]:
                    - code [ref=e5077]: spec
                    - emphasis [ref=e5078]:
                      - link "NamespacedCloudProfileSpec" [ref=e5079] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#namespacedcloudprofilespec"
                  - cell [ref=e5080]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e5081]: Spec defines the provider environment properties.
                - row [ref=e5082]:
                  - cell [ref=e5083]:
                    - code [ref=e5084]: status
                    - emphasis [ref=e5085]:
                      - link "NamespacedCloudProfileStatus" [ref=e5086] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#namespacedcloudprofilestatus"
                  - cell [ref=e5087]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e5088]: Most recently observed status of the NamespacedCloudProfile.
            - heading "NamespacedCloudProfileSpec" [level=3] [ref=e5089]
            - paragraph [ref=e5090]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e5091]: "Appears on:"
              - link "NamespacedCloudProfile" [ref=e5092] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#namespacedcloudprofile"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e5093]: NamespacedCloudProfileSpec is the specification of a NamespacedCloudProfile.
            - table [ref=e5094]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e5095]:
                - row [ref=e5096]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e5097]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e5098]
              - rowgroup [ref=e5099]:
                - row [ref=e5100]:
                  - cell [ref=e5101]:
                    - code [ref=e5102]: caBundle
                    - emphasis [ref=e5103]: string
                  - cell [ref=e5104]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e5105]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e5106]: CABundle is a certificate bundle which will be installed onto every host machine of shoot cluster targeting this profile.
                - row [ref=e5107]:
                  - cell [ref=e5108]:
                    - code [ref=e5109]: kubernetes
                    - emphasis [ref=e5110]:
                      - link "KubernetesSettings" [ref=e5111] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#kubernetessettings"
                  - cell [ref=e5112]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e5113]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e5114]: Kubernetes contains constraints regarding allowed values of the 'kubernetes' block in the Shoot specification.
                - row [ref=e5115]:
                  - cell [ref=e5116]:
                    - code [ref=e5117]: machineImages
                    - emphasis [ref=e5118]:
                      - link "MachineImage" [ref=e5119] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#machineimage"
                      - text: array
                  - cell [ref=e5120]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e5121]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e5122]: MachineImages contains constraints regarding allowed values for machine images in the Shoot specification.
                - row [ref=e5123]:
                  - cell [ref=e5124]:
                    - code [ref=e5125]: machineTypes
                    - emphasis [ref=e5126]:
                      - link "MachineType" [ref=e5127] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#machinetype"
                      - text: array
                  - cell [ref=e5128]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e5129]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e5130]: MachineTypes contains constraints regarding allowed values for machine types in the 'workers' block in the Shoot specification.
                - row [ref=e5131]:
                  - cell [ref=e5132]:
                    - code [ref=e5133]: volumeTypes
                    - emphasis [ref=e5134]:
                      - link "VolumeType" [ref=e5135] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#volumetype"
                      - text: array
                  - cell [ref=e5136]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e5137]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e5138]: VolumeTypes contains constraints regarding allowed values for volume types in the 'workers' block in the Shoot specification.
                - row [ref=e5139]:
                  - cell [ref=e5140]:
                    - code [ref=e5141]: parent
                    - emphasis [ref=e5142]:
                      - link "CloudProfileReference" [ref=e5143] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#cloudprofilereference"
                  - cell [ref=e5144]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e5145]: Parent contains a reference to a CloudProfile it inherits from.
                - row [ref=e5146]:
                  - cell [ref=e5147]:
                    - code [ref=e5148]: providerConfig
                    - emphasis [ref=e5149]:
                      - link "RawExtension" [ref=e5150] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#rawextension-runtime-pkg
                  - cell [ref=e5151]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e5152]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e5153]: ProviderConfig contains provider-specific configuration for the profile.
                - row [ref=e5154]:
                  - cell [ref=e5155]:
                    - code [ref=e5156]: limits
                    - emphasis [ref=e5157]:
                      - link "Limits" [ref=e5158] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#limits"
                  - cell [ref=e5159]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e5160]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e5161]: Limits configures operational limits for Shoot clusters using this NamespacedCloudProfile.Any limits specified here override those set in the parent CloudProfile.See https://github.com/gardener/gardener/blob/master/docs/usage/shoot/shoot_limits.md.
            - heading "NamespacedCloudProfileStatus" [level=3] [ref=e5162]
            - paragraph [ref=e5163]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e5164]: "Appears on:"
              - link "NamespacedCloudProfile" [ref=e5165] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#namespacedcloudprofile"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e5166]: NamespacedCloudProfileStatus holds the most recently observed status of the NamespacedCloudProfile.
            - table [ref=e5167]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e5168]:
                - row [ref=e5169]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e5170]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e5171]
              - rowgroup [ref=e5172]:
                - row [ref=e5173]:
                  - cell [ref=e5174]:
                    - code [ref=e5175]: cloudProfileSpec
                    - emphasis [ref=e5176]:
                      - link "CloudProfileSpec" [ref=e5177] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#cloudprofilespec"
                  - cell [ref=e5178]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e5179]: CloudProfile is the most recently generated CloudProfile of the NamespacedCloudProfile.
                - row [ref=e5180]:
                  - cell [ref=e5181]:
                    - code [ref=e5182]: observedGeneration
                    - emphasis [ref=e5183]: integer
                  - cell [ref=e5184]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e5185]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e5186]: ObservedGeneration is the most recent generation observed for this NamespacedCloudProfile.
            - heading "Networking" [level=3] [ref=e5187]
            - paragraph [ref=e5188]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e5189]: "Appears on:"
              - link "ShootSpec" [ref=e5190] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#shootspec"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e5191]: Networking defines networking parameters for the shoot cluster.
            - table [ref=e5192]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e5193]:
                - row [ref=e5194]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e5195]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e5196]
              - rowgroup [ref=e5197]:
                - row [ref=e5198]:
                  - cell [ref=e5199]:
                    - code [ref=e5200]: type
                    - emphasis [ref=e5201]: string
                  - cell [ref=e5202]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e5203]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e5204]: Type identifies the type of the networking plugin. This field is immutable.
                - row [ref=e5205]:
                  - cell [ref=e5206]:
                    - code [ref=e5207]: providerConfig
                    - emphasis [ref=e5208]:
                      - link "RawExtension" [ref=e5209] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#rawextension-runtime-pkg
                  - cell [ref=e5210]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e5211]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e5212]: ProviderConfig is the configuration passed to network resource.
                - row [ref=e5213]:
                  - cell [ref=e5214]:
                    - code [ref=e5215]: pods
                    - emphasis [ref=e5216]: string
                  - cell [ref=e5217]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e5218]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e5219]: Pods is the CIDR of the pod network. This field is immutable.
                - row [ref=e5220]:
                  - cell [ref=e5221]:
                    - code [ref=e5222]: nodes
                    - emphasis [ref=e5223]: string
                  - cell [ref=e5224]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e5225]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e5226]: Nodes is the CIDR of the entire node network.This field is mutable.
                - row [ref=e5227]:
                  - cell [ref=e5228]:
                    - code [ref=e5229]: services
                    - emphasis [ref=e5230]: string
                  - cell [ref=e5231]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e5232]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e5233]: Services is the CIDR of the service network. This field is immutable.
                - row [ref=e5234]:
                  - cell [ref=e5235]:
                    - code [ref=e5236]: ipFamilies
                    - emphasis [ref=e5237]:
                      - link "IPFamily" [ref=e5238] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#ipfamily"
                      - text: array
                  - cell [ref=e5239]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e5240]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e5241]: IPFamilies specifies the IP protocol versions to use for shoot networking.See https://github.com/gardener/gardener/blob/master/docs/development/ipv6.md.Defaults to ["IPv4"].
            - heading "NetworkingStatus" [level=3] [ref=e5242]
            - paragraph [ref=e5243]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e5244]: "Appears on:"
              - link "ShootStatus" [ref=e5245] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#shootstatus"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e5246]: NetworkingStatus contains information about cluster networking such as CIDRs.
            - table [ref=e5247]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e5248]:
                - row [ref=e5249]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e5250]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e5251]
              - rowgroup [ref=e5252]:
                - row [ref=e5253]:
                  - cell [ref=e5254]:
                    - code [ref=e5255]: pods
                    - emphasis [ref=e5256]: string array
                  - cell [ref=e5257]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e5258]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e5259]: Pods are the CIDRs of the pod network.
                - row [ref=e5260]:
                  - cell [ref=e5261]:
                    - code [ref=e5262]: nodes
                    - emphasis [ref=e5263]: string array
                  - cell [ref=e5264]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e5265]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e5266]: Nodes are the CIDRs of the node network.
                - row [ref=e5267]:
                  - cell [ref=e5268]:
                    - code [ref=e5269]: services
                    - emphasis [ref=e5270]: string array
                  - cell [ref=e5271]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e5272]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e5273]: Services are the CIDRs of the service network.
                - row [ref=e5274]:
                  - cell [ref=e5275]:
                    - code [ref=e5276]: egressCIDRs
                    - emphasis [ref=e5277]: string array
                  - cell [ref=e5278]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e5279]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e5280]: EgressCIDRs is a list of CIDRs used by the shoot as the source IP for egress traffic as reported by the usedInfrastructure extension controller. For certain environments the egress IPs may not be stable in which case theextension controller may opt to not populate this field.
            - heading "NginxIngress" [level=3] [ref=e5281]
            - paragraph [ref=e5282]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e5283]: "Appears on:"
              - link "Addons" [ref=e5284] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#addons"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e5285]: NginxIngress describes configuration values for the nginx-ingress addon.
            - table [ref=e5286]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e5287]:
                - row [ref=e5288]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e5289]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e5290]
              - rowgroup [ref=e5291]:
                - row [ref=e5292]:
                  - cell [ref=e5293]:
                    - code [ref=e5294]: enabled
                    - emphasis [ref=e5295]: boolean
                  - cell [ref=e5296]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e5297]: Enabled indicates whether the addon is enabled or not.
                - row [ref=e5298]:
                  - cell [ref=e5299]:
                    - code [ref=e5300]: loadBalancerSourceRanges
                    - emphasis [ref=e5301]: string array
                  - cell [ref=e5302]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e5303]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e5304]: LoadBalancerSourceRanges is list of allowed IP sources for NginxIngress
                - row [ref=e5305]:
                  - cell [ref=e5306]:
                    - code [ref=e5307]: config
                    - emphasis [ref=e5308]: object (keys:string, values:string)
                  - cell [ref=e5309]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e5310]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e5311]: Config contains custom configuration for the nginx-ingress-controller configuration.See https://github.com/kubernetes/ingress-nginx/blob/master/docs/user-guide/nginx-configuration/configmap.md#configuration-options
                - row [ref=e5312]:
                  - cell [ref=e5313]:
                    - code [ref=e5314]: externalTrafficPolicy
                    - emphasis [ref=e5315]:
                      - link "ServiceExternalTrafficPolicy" [ref=e5316] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#serviceexternaltrafficpolicy-v1-core
                  - cell [ref=e5317]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e5318]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e5319]: "ExternalTrafficPolicy controls the `.spec.externalTrafficPolicy` value of the load balancer `Service`exposing the nginx-ingress. Defaults to `Cluster`."
            - heading "NodeLocalDNS" [level=3] [ref=e5320]
            - paragraph [ref=e5321]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e5322]: "Appears on:"
              - link "SystemComponents" [ref=e5323] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#systemcomponents"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e5324]: NodeLocalDNS contains the settings of the node local DNS components running in the data plane of the Shoot cluster.
            - table [ref=e5325]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e5326]:
                - row [ref=e5327]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e5328]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e5329]
              - rowgroup [ref=e5330]:
                - row [ref=e5331]:
                  - cell [ref=e5332]:
                    - code [ref=e5333]: enabled
                    - emphasis [ref=e5334]: boolean
                  - cell [ref=e5335]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e5336]: Enabled indicates whether node local DNS is enabled or not.
                - row [ref=e5337]:
                  - cell [ref=e5338]:
                    - code [ref=e5339]: forceTCPToClusterDNS
                    - emphasis [ref=e5340]: boolean
                  - cell [ref=e5341]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e5342]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e5343]: ForceTCPToClusterDNS indicates whether the connection from the node local DNS to the cluster DNS (Core DNS) will be forced to TCP or not.Default, if unspecified, is to enforce TCP.
                - row [ref=e5344]:
                  - cell [ref=e5345]:
                    - code [ref=e5346]: forceTCPToUpstreamDNS
                    - emphasis [ref=e5347]: boolean
                  - cell [ref=e5348]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e5349]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e5350]: ForceTCPToUpstreamDNS indicates whether the connection from the node local DNS to the upstream DNS (infrastructure DNS) will be forced to TCP or not.Default, if unspecified, is to enforce TCP.
                - row [ref=e5351]:
                  - cell [ref=e5352]:
                    - code [ref=e5353]: disableForwardToUpstreamDNS
                    - emphasis [ref=e5354]: boolean
                  - cell [ref=e5355]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e5356]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e5357]: DisableForwardToUpstreamDNS indicates whether requests from node local DNS to upstream DNS should be disabled.Default, if unspecified, is to forward requests for external domains to upstream DNS
            - heading "OCIRepository" [level=3] [ref=e5358]
            - paragraph [ref=e5359]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e5360]: "Appears on:"
              - link "HelmControllerDeployment" [ref=e5361] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#helmcontrollerdeployment"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e5362]: OCIRepository configures where to pull an OCI Artifact, that could contain for example a Helm Chart.
            - table [ref=e5363]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e5364]:
                - row [ref=e5365]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e5366]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e5367]
              - rowgroup [ref=e5368]:
                - row [ref=e5369]:
                  - cell [ref=e5370]:
                    - code [ref=e5371]: ref
                    - emphasis [ref=e5372]: string
                  - cell [ref=e5373]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e5374]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e5375]: Ref is the full artifact Ref and takes precedence over all other fields.
                - row [ref=e5376]:
                  - cell [ref=e5377]:
                    - code [ref=e5378]: repository
                    - emphasis [ref=e5379]: string
                  - cell [ref=e5380]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e5381]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e5382]: Repository is a reference to an OCI artifact repository.
                - row [ref=e5383]:
                  - cell [ref=e5384]:
                    - code [ref=e5385]: tag
                    - emphasis [ref=e5386]: string
                  - cell [ref=e5387]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e5388]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e5389]: Tag is the image tag to pull.
                - row [ref=e5390]:
                  - cell [ref=e5391]:
                    - code [ref=e5392]: digest
                    - emphasis [ref=e5393]: string
                  - cell [ref=e5394]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e5395]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e5396]: Digest of the image to pull, takes precedence over tag.
                - row [ref=e5397]:
                  - cell [ref=e5398]:
                    - code [ref=e5399]: pullSecretRef
                    - emphasis [ref=e5400]:
                      - link "LocalObjectReference" [ref=e5401] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#localobjectreference-v1-core
                  - cell [ref=e5402]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e5403]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e5404]: "PullSecretRef is a reference to a secret containing the pull secret.The secret must be of type `kubernetes.io/dockerconfigjson` and must be located in the `garden` namespace."
                - row [ref=e5405]:
                  - cell [ref=e5406]:
                    - code [ref=e5407]: caBundleSecretRef
                    - emphasis [ref=e5408]:
                      - link "LocalObjectReference" [ref=e5409] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#localobjectreference-v1-core
                  - cell [ref=e5410]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e5411]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e5412]: "CABundleSecretRef is a reference to a secret containing a PEM-encoded certificate authority bundle.The CA bundle is used to verify the TLS certificate of the OCI registry.The secret must have a data key `bundle.crt` and must be located in the `garden` namespace.For usage in the gardenlet, the secret must have the label `gardener.cloud/role=oci-ca-bundle`.If not provided, the system's default certificate pool is used."
            - heading "ObservabilityRotation" [level=3] [ref=e5413]
            - paragraph [ref=e5414]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e5415]: "Appears on:"
              - link "ShootCredentialsRotation" [ref=e5416] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#shootcredentialsrotation"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e5417]: ObservabilityRotation contains information about the observability credential rotation.
            - table [ref=e5418]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e5419]:
                - row [ref=e5420]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e5421]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e5422]
              - rowgroup [ref=e5423]:
                - row [ref=e5424]:
                  - cell [ref=e5425]:
                    - code [ref=e5426]: lastInitiationTime
                    - emphasis [ref=e5427]:
                      - link "Time" [ref=e5428] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#time-v1-meta
                  - cell [ref=e5429]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e5430]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e5431]: LastInitiationTime is the most recent time when the observability credential rotation was initiated.
                - row [ref=e5432]:
                  - cell [ref=e5433]:
                    - code [ref=e5434]: lastCompletionTime
                    - emphasis [ref=e5435]:
                      - link "Time" [ref=e5436] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#time-v1-meta
                  - cell [ref=e5437]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e5438]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e5439]: LastCompletionTime is the most recent time when the observability credential rotation was successfully completed.
            - heading "PendingWorkerUpdates" [level=3] [ref=e5440]
            - paragraph [ref=e5441]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e5442]: "Appears on:"
              - link "InPlaceUpdatesStatus" [ref=e5443] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#inplaceupdatesstatus"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e5444]: PendingWorkerUpdates contains information about worker pools pending in-place update.
            - table [ref=e5445]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e5446]:
                - row [ref=e5447]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e5448]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e5449]
              - rowgroup [ref=e5450]:
                - row [ref=e5451]:
                  - cell [ref=e5452]:
                    - code [ref=e5453]: autoInPlaceUpdate
                    - emphasis [ref=e5454]: string array
                  - cell [ref=e5455]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e5456]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e5457]: AutoInPlaceUpdate contains the names of the pending worker pools with strategy AutoInPlaceUpdate.
                - row [ref=e5458]:
                  - cell [ref=e5459]:
                    - code [ref=e5460]: manualInPlaceUpdate
                    - emphasis [ref=e5461]: string array
                  - cell [ref=e5462]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e5463]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e5464]: ManualInPlaceUpdate contains the names of the pending worker pools with strategy ManualInPlaceUpdate.
            - heading "PendingWorkersRollout" [level=3] [ref=e5465]
            - paragraph [ref=e5466]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e5467]: "Appears on:"
              - link "CARotation" [ref=e5468] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#carotation"
              - text: ","
              - link "ManualWorkerPoolRollout" [ref=e5469] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#manualworkerpoolrollout"
              - text: ","
              - link "ServiceAccountKeyRotation" [ref=e5470] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#serviceaccountkeyrotation"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e5471]: PendingWorkersRollout contains the name of a worker pool and the initiation time of their last rollout.
            - table [ref=e5472]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e5473]:
                - row [ref=e5474]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e5475]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e5476]
              - rowgroup [ref=e5477]:
                - row [ref=e5478]:
                  - cell [ref=e5479]:
                    - code [ref=e5480]: name
                    - emphasis [ref=e5481]: string
                  - cell [ref=e5482]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e5483]: Name is the name of a worker pool.
                - row [ref=e5484]:
                  - cell [ref=e5485]:
                    - code [ref=e5486]: lastInitiationTime
                    - emphasis [ref=e5487]:
                      - link "Time" [ref=e5488] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#time-v1-meta
                  - cell [ref=e5489]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e5490]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e5491]: LastInitiationTime is the most recent time when the worker rollout was initiated.
            - heading "Project" [level=3] [ref=e5492]
            - paragraph [ref=e5493]: Project holds certain properties about a Gardener project.
            - table [ref=e5494]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e5495]:
                - row [ref=e5496]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e5497]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e5498]
              - rowgroup [ref=e5499]:
                - row [ref=e5500]:
                  - cell [ref=e5501]:
                    - code [ref=e5502]: metadata
                    - emphasis [ref=e5503]:
                      - link "ObjectMeta" [ref=e5504] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#objectmeta-v1-meta
                  - cell [ref=e5505]:
                    - text: Refer to the Kubernetes API documentation for the fields of the
                    - code [ref=e5506]: metadata
                    - text: field.
                - row [ref=e5507]:
                  - cell [ref=e5508]:
                    - code [ref=e5509]: spec
                    - emphasis [ref=e5510]:
                      - link "ProjectSpec" [ref=e5511] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#projectspec"
                  - cell [ref=e5512]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e5513]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e5514]: Spec defines the project properties.
                - row [ref=e5515]:
                  - cell [ref=e5516]:
                    - code [ref=e5517]: status
                    - emphasis [ref=e5518]:
                      - link "ProjectStatus" [ref=e5519] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#projectstatus"
                  - cell [ref=e5520]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e5521]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e5522]: Most recently observed status of the Project.
            - heading "ProjectMember" [level=3] [ref=e5523]
            - paragraph [ref=e5524]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e5525]: "Appears on:"
              - link "ProjectSpec" [ref=e5526] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#projectspec"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e5527]: ProjectMember is a member of a project.
            - table [ref=e5528]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e5529]:
                - row [ref=e5530]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e5531]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e5532]
              - rowgroup [ref=e5533]:
                - row [ref=e5534]:
                  - cell [ref=e5535]:
                    - code [ref=e5536]: kind
                    - emphasis [ref=e5537]: string
                  - cell [ref=e5538]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e5539]: Kind of object being referenced. Values defined by this API group are "User", "Group", and "ServiceAccount".If the Authorizer does not recognized the kind value, the Authorizer should report an error.
                - row [ref=e5540]:
                  - cell [ref=e5541]:
                    - code [ref=e5542]: apiGroup
                    - emphasis [ref=e5543]: string
                  - cell [ref=e5544]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e5545]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e5546]: APIGroup holds the API group of the referenced subject.Defaults to "" for ServiceAccount subjects.Defaults to "rbac.authorization.k8s.io" for User and Group subjects.
                - row [ref=e5547]:
                  - cell [ref=e5548]:
                    - code [ref=e5549]: name
                    - emphasis [ref=e5550]: string
                  - cell [ref=e5551]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e5552]: Name of the object being referenced.
                - row [ref=e5553]:
                  - cell [ref=e5554]:
                    - code [ref=e5555]: namespace
                    - emphasis [ref=e5556]: string
                  - cell [ref=e5557]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e5558]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e5559]: Namespace of the referenced object. If the object kind is non-namespace, such as "User" or "Group", and this value is not emptythe Authorizer should report an error.
                - row [ref=e5560]:
                  - cell [ref=e5561]:
                    - code [ref=e5562]: role
                    - emphasis [ref=e5563]: string
                  - cell [ref=e5564]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e5565]: "Role represents the role of this member.IMPORTANT: Be aware that this field will be removed in the `v1` version of this API in favor of the `roles`list."
                - row [ref=e5566]:
                  - cell [ref=e5567]:
                    - code [ref=e5568]: roles
                    - emphasis [ref=e5569]: string array
                  - cell [ref=e5570]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e5571]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e5572]: Roles represents the list of roles of this member.
            - heading "ProjectPhase" [level=3] [ref=e5573]
            - paragraph [ref=e5574]:
              - emphasis [ref=e5575]: "Underlying type: string"
            - paragraph [ref=e5576]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e5577]: "Appears on:"
              - link "ProjectStatus" [ref=e5578] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#projectstatus"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e5579]: ProjectPhase is a label for the condition of a project at the current time.
            - heading "ProjectSpec" [level=3] [ref=e5580]
            - paragraph [ref=e5581]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e5582]: "Appears on:"
              - link "Project" [ref=e5583] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#project"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e5584]: ProjectSpec is the specification of a Project.
            - table [ref=e5585]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e5586]:
                - row [ref=e5587]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e5588]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e5589]
              - rowgroup [ref=e5590]:
                - row [ref=e5591]:
                  - cell [ref=e5592]:
                    - code [ref=e5593]: createdBy
                    - emphasis [ref=e5594]:
                      - link "Subject" [ref=e5595] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#subject-v1-rbac
                  - cell [ref=e5596]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e5597]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e5598]: CreatedBy is a subject representing a user name, an email address, or any other identifier of a userwho created the project. This field is immutable.
                - row [ref=e5599]:
                  - cell [ref=e5600]:
                    - code [ref=e5601]: description
                    - emphasis [ref=e5602]: string
                  - cell [ref=e5603]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e5604]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e5605]: Description is a human-readable description of what the project is used for.Only letters, digits and certain punctuation characters are allowed for this field.
                - row [ref=e5606]:
                  - cell [ref=e5607]:
                    - code [ref=e5608]: owner
                    - emphasis [ref=e5609]:
                      - link "Subject" [ref=e5610] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#subject-v1-rbac
                  - cell [ref=e5611]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e5612]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e5613]: "Owner is a subject representing a user name, an email address, or any other identifier of a user owningthe project.IMPORTANT: Be aware that this field will be removed in the `v1` version of this API in favor of the `owner`role. The only way to change the owner will be by moving the `owner` role. In this API version the only wayto change the owner is to use this field."
                - row [ref=e5614]:
                  - cell [ref=e5615]:
                    - code [ref=e5616]: purpose
                    - emphasis [ref=e5617]: string
                  - cell [ref=e5618]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e5619]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e5620]: Purpose is a human-readable explanation of the project's purpose.Only letters, digits and certain punctuation characters are allowed for this field.
                - row [ref=e5621]:
                  - cell [ref=e5622]:
                    - code [ref=e5623]: members
                    - emphasis [ref=e5624]:
                      - link "ProjectMember" [ref=e5625] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#projectmember"
                      - text: array
                  - cell [ref=e5626]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e5627]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e5628]: Members is a list of subjects representing a user name, an email address, or any other identifier of a user,group, or service account that has a certain role.
                - row [ref=e5629]:
                  - cell [ref=e5630]:
                    - code [ref=e5631]: namespace
                    - emphasis [ref=e5632]: string
                  - cell [ref=e5633]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e5634]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e5635]: "Namespace is the name of the namespace that has been created for the Project object.A nil value means that Gardener will determine the name of the namespace.If set, its value must be prefixed with `garden-`.This field is immutable."
                - row [ref=e5636]:
                  - cell [ref=e5637]:
                    - code [ref=e5638]: tolerations
                    - emphasis [ref=e5639]:
                      - link "ProjectTolerations" [ref=e5640] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#projecttolerations"
                  - cell [ref=e5641]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e5642]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e5643]: Tolerations contains the tolerations for taints on seed clusters.
                - row [ref=e5644]:
                  - cell [ref=e5645]:
                    - code [ref=e5646]: dualApprovalForDeletion
                    - emphasis [ref=e5647]:
                      - link "DualApprovalForDeletion" [ref=e5648] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#dualapprovalfordeletion"
                      - text: array
                  - cell [ref=e5649]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e5650]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e5651]: DualApprovalForDeletion contains configuration for the dual approval concept for resource deletion.
            - heading "ProjectStatus" [level=3] [ref=e5652]
            - paragraph [ref=e5653]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e5654]: "Appears on:"
              - link "Project" [ref=e5655] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#project"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e5656]: ProjectStatus holds the most recently observed status of the project.
            - table [ref=e5657]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e5658]:
                - row [ref=e5659]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e5660]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e5661]
              - rowgroup [ref=e5662]:
                - row [ref=e5663]:
                  - cell [ref=e5664]:
                    - code [ref=e5665]: observedGeneration
                    - emphasis [ref=e5666]: integer
                  - cell [ref=e5667]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e5668]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e5669]: ObservedGeneration is the most recent generation observed for this project.
                - row [ref=e5670]:
                  - cell [ref=e5671]:
                    - code [ref=e5672]: phase
                    - emphasis [ref=e5673]:
                      - link "ProjectPhase" [ref=e5674] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#projectphase"
                  - cell [ref=e5675]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e5676]: Phase is the current phase of the project.
                - row [ref=e5677]:
                  - cell [ref=e5678]:
                    - code [ref=e5679]: staleSinceTimestamp
                    - emphasis [ref=e5680]:
                      - link "Time" [ref=e5681] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#time-v1-meta
                  - cell [ref=e5682]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e5683]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e5684]: StaleSinceTimestamp contains the timestamp when the project was first discovered to be stale/unused.
                - row [ref=e5685]:
                  - cell [ref=e5686]:
                    - code [ref=e5687]: staleAutoDeleteTimestamp
                    - emphasis [ref=e5688]:
                      - link "Time" [ref=e5689] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#time-v1-meta
                  - cell [ref=e5690]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e5691]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e5692]: StaleAutoDeleteTimestamp contains the timestamp when the project will be garbage-collected/automatically deletedbecause it's stale/unused.
                - row [ref=e5693]:
                  - cell [ref=e5694]:
                    - code [ref=e5695]: lastActivityTimestamp
                    - emphasis [ref=e5696]:
                      - link "Time" [ref=e5697] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#time-v1-meta
                  - cell [ref=e5698]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e5699]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e5700]: LastActivityTimestamp contains the timestamp from the last activity performed in this project.
                - row [ref=e5701]:
                  - cell [ref=e5702]:
                    - code [ref=e5703]: conditions
                    - emphasis [ref=e5704]:
                      - link "Condition" [ref=e5705] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#condition"
                      - text: array
                  - cell [ref=e5706]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e5707]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e5708]: Conditions represents the latest available observations of a Project's current state.
            - heading "ProjectTolerations" [level=3] [ref=e5709]
            - paragraph [ref=e5710]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e5711]: "Appears on:"
              - link "ProjectSpec" [ref=e5712] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#projectspec"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e5713]: ProjectTolerations contains the tolerations for taints on seed clusters.
            - table [ref=e5714]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e5715]:
                - row [ref=e5716]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e5717]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e5718]
              - rowgroup [ref=e5719]:
                - row [ref=e5720]:
                  - cell [ref=e5721]:
                    - code [ref=e5722]: defaults
                    - emphasis [ref=e5723]:
                      - link "Toleration" [ref=e5724] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#toleration"
                      - text: array
                  - cell [ref=e5725]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e5726]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e5727]: Defaults contains a list of tolerations that are added to the shoots in this project by default.
                - row [ref=e5728]:
                  - cell [ref=e5729]:
                    - code [ref=e5730]: whitelist
                    - emphasis [ref=e5731]:
                      - link "Toleration" [ref=e5732] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#toleration"
                      - text: array
                  - cell [ref=e5733]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e5734]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e5735]: "Whitelist contains a list of tolerations that are allowed to be added to the shoots in this project. Please notethat this list may only be added by users having the `spec-tolerations-whitelist` verb for project resources."
            - heading "Provider" [level=3] [ref=e5736]
            - paragraph [ref=e5737]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e5738]: "Appears on:"
              - link "ShootSpec" [ref=e5739] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#shootspec"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e5740]: Provider contains provider-specific information that are handed-over to the provider-specific extension controller.
            - table [ref=e5741]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e5742]:
                - row [ref=e5743]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e5744]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e5745]
              - rowgroup [ref=e5746]:
                - row [ref=e5747]:
                  - cell [ref=e5748]:
                    - code [ref=e5749]: type
                    - emphasis [ref=e5750]: string
                  - cell [ref=e5751]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e5752]: Type is the type of the provider. This field is immutable.
                - row [ref=e5753]:
                  - cell [ref=e5754]:
                    - code [ref=e5755]: controlPlaneConfig
                    - emphasis [ref=e5756]:
                      - link "RawExtension" [ref=e5757] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#rawextension-runtime-pkg
                  - cell [ref=e5758]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e5759]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e5760]: ControlPlaneConfig contains the provider-specific control plane config blob. Please look up the concretedefinition in the documentation of your provider extension.
                - row [ref=e5761]:
                  - cell [ref=e5762]:
                    - code [ref=e5763]: infrastructureConfig
                    - emphasis [ref=e5764]:
                      - link "RawExtension" [ref=e5765] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#rawextension-runtime-pkg
                  - cell [ref=e5766]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e5767]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e5768]: InfrastructureConfig contains the provider-specific infrastructure config blob. Please look up the concretedefinition in the documentation of your provider extension.
                - row [ref=e5769]:
                  - cell [ref=e5770]:
                    - code [ref=e5771]: workers
                    - emphasis [ref=e5772]:
                      - link "Worker" [ref=e5773] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#worker"
                      - text: array
                  - cell [ref=e5774]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e5775]: (Optional)
