                    - paragraph [ref=e5776]: Workers is a list of worker groups.
                - row [ref=e5777]:
                  - cell [ref=e5778]:
                    - code [ref=e5779]: workersSettings
                    - emphasis [ref=e5780]:
                      - link "WorkersSettings" [ref=e5781] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#workerssettings"
                  - cell [ref=e5782]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e5783]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e5784]: WorkersSettings contains settings for all workers.
            - heading "ProxyMode" [level=3] [ref=e5785]
            - paragraph [ref=e5786]:
              - emphasis [ref=e5787]: "Underlying type: string"
            - paragraph [ref=e5788]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e5789]: "Appears on:"
              - link "KubeProxyConfig" [ref=e5790] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#kubeproxyconfig"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e5791]: "ProxyMode available in Linux platform: 'userspace' (older, going to be EOL), 'iptables' (newer, faster), 'nftables', and 'ipvs' (deprecated starting with Kubernetes 1.35). As of now only 'iptables', 'nftables' and 'ipvs' (deprecated starting with Kubernetes 1.35) is supported by Gardener. In Linux platform, if the iptables proxy is selected, regardless of how, but the system's kernel or iptables versions are insufficient, this always falls back to the userspace proxy."
            - heading "Quota" [level=3] [ref=e5792]
            - paragraph [ref=e5793]: Quota represents a quota on resources consumed by shoot clusters either per project or per provider secret.
            - table [ref=e5794]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e5795]:
                - row [ref=e5796]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e5797]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e5798]
              - rowgroup [ref=e5799]:
                - row [ref=e5800]:
                  - cell [ref=e5801]:
                    - code [ref=e5802]: metadata
                    - emphasis [ref=e5803]:
                      - link "ObjectMeta" [ref=e5804] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#objectmeta-v1-meta
                  - cell [ref=e5805]:
                    - text: Refer to the Kubernetes API documentation for the fields of the
                    - code [ref=e5806]: metadata
                    - text: field.
                - row [ref=e5807]:
                  - cell [ref=e5808]:
                    - code [ref=e5809]: spec
                    - emphasis [ref=e5810]:
                      - link "QuotaSpec" [ref=e5811] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#quotaspec"
                  - cell [ref=e5812]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e5813]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e5814]: Spec defines the Quota constraints.
            - heading "QuotaSpec" [level=3] [ref=e5815]
            - paragraph [ref=e5816]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e5817]: "Appears on:"
              - link "Quota" [ref=e5818] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#quota"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e5819]: QuotaSpec is the specification of a Quota.
            - table [ref=e5820]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e5821]:
                - row [ref=e5822]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e5823]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e5824]
              - rowgroup [ref=e5825]:
                - row [ref=e5826]:
                  - cell [ref=e5827]:
                    - code [ref=e5828]: clusterLifetimeDays
                    - emphasis [ref=e5829]: integer
                  - cell [ref=e5830]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e5831]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e5832]: ClusterLifetimeDays is the lifetime of a Shoot cluster in days before it will be terminated automatically.
                - row [ref=e5833]:
                  - cell [ref=e5834]:
                    - code [ref=e5835]: scope
                    - emphasis [ref=e5836]:
                      - link "ObjectReference" [ref=e5837] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#objectreference-v1-core
                  - cell [ref=e5838]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e5839]: Scope is the scope of the Quota object, either 'project', 'secret' or 'workloadidentity'. This field is immutable.
            - heading "Region" [level=3] [ref=e5840]
            - paragraph [ref=e5841]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e5842]: "Appears on:"
              - link "CloudProfileSpec" [ref=e5843] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#cloudprofilespec"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e5844]: Region contains certain properties of a region.
            - table [ref=e5845]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e5846]:
                - row [ref=e5847]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e5848]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e5849]
              - rowgroup [ref=e5850]:
                - row [ref=e5851]:
                  - cell [ref=e5852]:
                    - code [ref=e5853]: name
                    - emphasis [ref=e5854]: string
                  - cell [ref=e5855]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e5856]: Name is a region name.
                - row [ref=e5857]:
                  - cell [ref=e5858]:
                    - code [ref=e5859]: zones
                    - emphasis [ref=e5860]:
                      - link "AvailabilityZone" [ref=e5861] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#availabilityzone"
                      - text: array
                  - cell [ref=e5862]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e5863]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e5864]: Zones is a list of availability zones in this region.
                - row [ref=e5865]:
                  - cell [ref=e5866]:
                    - code [ref=e5867]: labels
                    - emphasis [ref=e5868]: object (keys:string, values:string)
                  - cell [ref=e5869]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e5870]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e5871]: Labels is an optional set of key-value pairs that contain certain administrator-controlled labels for this region.It can be used by Gardener administrators/operators to provide additional information about a region, e.g. wrtquality, reliability, etc.
                - row [ref=e5872]:
                  - cell [ref=e5873]:
                    - code [ref=e5874]: accessRestrictions
                    - emphasis [ref=e5875]:
                      - link "AccessRestriction" [ref=e5876] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#accessrestriction"
                      - text: array
                  - cell [ref=e5877]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e5878]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e5879]: AccessRestrictions describe a list of access restrictions that can be used for Shoots using this region.
            - heading "ResourceData" [level=3] [ref=e5880]
            - paragraph [ref=e5881]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e5882]: "Appears on:"
              - link "ShootStateSpec" [ref=e5883] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#shootstatespec"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e5884]: ResourceData holds the data of a resource referred to by an extension controller state.
            - table [ref=e5885]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e5886]:
                - row [ref=e5887]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e5888]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e5889]
              - rowgroup [ref=e5890]:
                - row [ref=e5891]:
                  - cell [ref=e5892]:
                    - code [ref=e5893]: kind
                    - emphasis [ref=e5894]: string
                  - cell [ref=e5895]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e5896]: "kind is the kind of the referent; More info: https://git.k8s.io/community/contributors/devel/sig-architecture/api-conventions.md#types-kinds"
                - row [ref=e5897]:
                  - cell [ref=e5898]:
                    - code [ref=e5899]: name
                    - emphasis [ref=e5900]: string
                  - cell [ref=e5901]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e5902]: "name is the name of the referent; More info: https://kubernetes.io/docs/concepts/overview/working-with-objects/names/#names"
                - row [ref=e5903]:
                  - cell [ref=e5904]:
                    - code [ref=e5905]: apiVersion
                    - emphasis [ref=e5906]: string
                  - cell [ref=e5907]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e5908]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e5909]: apiVersion is the API version of the referent
                - row [ref=e5910]:
                  - cell [ref=e5911]:
                    - code [ref=e5912]: data
                    - emphasis [ref=e5913]:
                      - link "RawExtension" [ref=e5914] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#rawextension-runtime-pkg
                  - cell [ref=e5915]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e5916]: Data of the resource
            - heading "ResourceWatchCacheSize" [level=3] [ref=e5917]
            - paragraph [ref=e5918]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e5919]: "Appears on:"
              - link "WatchCacheSizes" [ref=e5920] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#watchcachesizes"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e5921]: ResourceWatchCacheSize contains configuration of the API server's watch cache size for one specific resource.
            - table [ref=e5922]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e5923]:
                - row [ref=e5924]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e5925]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e5926]
              - rowgroup [ref=e5927]:
                - row [ref=e5928]:
                  - cell [ref=e5929]:
                    - code [ref=e5930]: apiGroup
                    - emphasis [ref=e5931]: string
                  - cell [ref=e5932]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e5933]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e5934]: "APIGroup is the API group of the resource for which the watch cache size should be configured.An unset value is used to specify the legacy core API (e.g. for `secrets`)."
                - row [ref=e5935]:
                  - cell [ref=e5936]:
                    - code [ref=e5937]: resource
                    - emphasis [ref=e5938]: string
                  - cell [ref=e5939]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e5940]: "Resource is the name of the resource for which the watch cache size should be configured(in lowercase plural form, e.g. `secrets`)."
                - row [ref=e5941]:
                  - cell [ref=e5942]:
                    - code [ref=e5943]: size
                    - emphasis [ref=e5944]: integer
                  - cell [ref=e5945]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e5946]: CacheSize specifies the watch cache size that should be configured for the specified resource.
            - heading "SSHAccess" [level=3] [ref=e5947]
            - paragraph [ref=e5948]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e5949]: "Appears on:"
              - link "WorkersSettings" [ref=e5950] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#workerssettings"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e5951]: SSHAccess contains settings regarding ssh access to the worker nodes.
            - table [ref=e5952]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e5953]:
                - row [ref=e5954]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e5955]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e5956]
              - rowgroup [ref=e5957]:
                - row [ref=e5958]:
                  - cell [ref=e5959]:
                    - code [ref=e5960]: enabled
                    - emphasis [ref=e5961]: boolean
                  - cell [ref=e5962]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e5963]: Enabled indicates whether the SSH access to the worker nodes is ensured to be enabled or disabled in systemd.Defaults to true.
            - heading "SchedulingProfile" [level=3] [ref=e5964]
            - paragraph [ref=e5965]:
              - emphasis [ref=e5966]: "Underlying type: string"
            - paragraph [ref=e5967]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e5968]: "Appears on:"
              - link "KubeSchedulerConfig" [ref=e5969] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#kubeschedulerconfig"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e5970]: SchedulingProfile is a string alias used for scheduling profile values.
            - heading "SecretBinding" [level=3] [ref=e5971]
            - paragraph [ref=e5972]: "SecretBinding represents a binding to a secret in the same or another namespace. Deprecated: Use CredentialsBinding instead. See /docs/gardener/shoot-operations/secretbinding-to-credentialsbinding-migration/ for migration instructions."
            - table [ref=e5973]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e5974]:
                - row [ref=e5975]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e5976]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e5977]
              - rowgroup [ref=e5978]:
                - row [ref=e5979]:
                  - cell [ref=e5980]:
                    - code [ref=e5981]: metadata
                    - emphasis [ref=e5982]:
                      - link "ObjectMeta" [ref=e5983] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#objectmeta-v1-meta
                  - cell [ref=e5984]:
                    - text: Refer to the Kubernetes API documentation for the fields of the
                    - code [ref=e5985]: metadata
                    - text: field.
                - row [ref=e5986]:
                  - cell [ref=e5987]:
                    - code [ref=e5988]: secretRef
                    - emphasis [ref=e5989]:
                      - link "SecretReference" [ref=e5990] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#secretreference-v1-core
                  - cell [ref=e5991]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e5992]: SecretRef is a reference to a secret object in the same or another namespace.This field is immutable.
                - row [ref=e5993]:
                  - cell [ref=e5994]:
                    - code [ref=e5995]: quotas
                    - emphasis [ref=e5996]:
                      - link "ObjectReference" [ref=e5997] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#objectreference-v1-core
                      - text: array
                  - cell [ref=e5998]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e5999]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e6000]: Quotas is a list of references to Quota objects in the same or another namespace.This field is immutable.
                - row [ref=e6001]:
                  - cell [ref=e6002]:
                    - code [ref=e6003]: provider
                    - emphasis [ref=e6004]:
                      - link "SecretBindingProvider" [ref=e6005] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#secretbindingprovider"
                  - cell [ref=e6006]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e6007]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e6008]: Provider defines the provider type of the SecretBinding.This field is immutable.
            - heading "SecretBindingProvider" [level=3] [ref=e6009]
            - paragraph [ref=e6010]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e6011]: "Appears on:"
              - link "SecretBinding" [ref=e6012] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#secretbinding"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e6013]: "SecretBindingProvider defines the provider type of the SecretBinding. Deprecated: Use CredentialsBindingProvider instead. See /docs/gardener/shoot-operations/secretbinding-to-credentialsbinding-migration/ for migration instructions."
            - table [ref=e6014]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e6015]:
                - row [ref=e6016]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e6017]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e6018]
              - rowgroup [ref=e6019]:
                - row [ref=e6020]:
                  - cell [ref=e6021]:
                    - code [ref=e6022]: type
                    - emphasis [ref=e6023]: string
                  - cell [ref=e6024]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e6025]: Type is the type of the provider.For backwards compatibility, the field can contain multiple providers separated by a comma.However the usage of single SecretBinding (hence Secret) for different cloud providers is strongly discouraged.
            - heading "Seed" [level=3] [ref=e6026]
            - paragraph [ref=e6027]: Seed represents an installation request for an external controller.
            - table [ref=e6028]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e6029]:
                - row [ref=e6030]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e6031]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e6032]
              - rowgroup [ref=e6033]:
                - row [ref=e6034]:
                  - cell [ref=e6035]:
                    - code [ref=e6036]: metadata
                    - emphasis [ref=e6037]:
                      - link "ObjectMeta" [ref=e6038] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#objectmeta-v1-meta
                  - cell [ref=e6039]:
                    - text: Refer to the Kubernetes API documentation for the fields of the
                    - code [ref=e6040]: metadata
                    - text: field.
                - row [ref=e6041]:
                  - cell [ref=e6042]:
                    - code [ref=e6043]: spec
                    - emphasis [ref=e6044]:
                      - link "SeedSpec" [ref=e6045] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#seedspec"
                  - cell [ref=e6046]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e6047]: Spec contains the specification of this installation.
                - row [ref=e6048]:
                  - cell [ref=e6049]:
                    - code [ref=e6050]: status
                    - emphasis [ref=e6051]:
                      - link "SeedStatus" [ref=e6052] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#seedstatus"
                  - cell [ref=e6053]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e6054]: Status contains the status of this installation.
            - heading "SeedDNS" [level=3] [ref=e6055]
            - paragraph [ref=e6056]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e6057]: "Appears on:"
              - link "SeedSpec" [ref=e6058] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#seedspec"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e6059]: SeedDNS contains DNS-relevant information about this seed cluster.
            - table [ref=e6060]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e6061]:
                - row [ref=e6062]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e6063]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e6064]
              - rowgroup [ref=e6065]:
                - row [ref=e6066]:
                  - cell [ref=e6067]:
                    - code [ref=e6068]: provider
                    - emphasis [ref=e6069]:
                      - link "SeedDNSProvider" [ref=e6070] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#seeddnsprovider"
                  - cell [ref=e6071]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e6072]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e6073]: Provider configures a DNSProvider
                - row [ref=e6074]:
                  - cell [ref=e6075]:
                    - code [ref=e6076]: internal
                    - emphasis [ref=e6077]:
                      - link "SeedDNSProviderConfig" [ref=e6078] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#seeddnsproviderconfig"
                  - cell [ref=e6079]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e6080]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e6081]: Internal configures DNS settings related to seed internal domain.
                - row [ref=e6082]:
                  - cell [ref=e6083]:
                    - code [ref=e6084]: defaults
                    - emphasis [ref=e6085]:
                      - link "SeedDNSProviderConfig" [ref=e6086] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#seeddnsproviderconfig"
                      - text: array
                  - cell [ref=e6087]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e6088]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e6089]: Defaults configures DNS settings related to seed default domains.When determining the DNS settings for a Shoot, the first matching entry in this list will take precedence.
            - heading "SeedDNSProvider" [level=3] [ref=e6090]
            - paragraph [ref=e6091]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e6092]: "Appears on:"
              - link "SeedDNS" [ref=e6093] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#seeddns"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e6094]: SeedDNSProvider configures a DNSProvider for Seeds
            - table [ref=e6095]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e6096]:
                - row [ref=e6097]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e6098]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e6099]
              - rowgroup [ref=e6100]:
                - row [ref=e6101]:
                  - cell [ref=e6102]:
                    - code [ref=e6103]: type
                    - emphasis [ref=e6104]: string
                  - cell [ref=e6105]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e6106]: "Type describes the type of the dns-provider, for example `aws-route53`"
                - row [ref=e6107]:
                  - cell [ref=e6108]:
                    - code [ref=e6109]: credentialsRef
                    - emphasis [ref=e6110]:
                      - link "ObjectReference" [ref=e6111] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#objectreference-v1-core
                  - cell [ref=e6112]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e6113]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e6114]: CredentialsRef is a reference to a resource holding the credentials used forauthentication with the DNS provider.Supported referenced resources are v1.Secrets andsecurity.gardener.cloud/v1alpha1.WorkloadIdentity
            - heading "SeedDNSProviderConfig" [level=3] [ref=e6115]
            - paragraph [ref=e6116]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e6117]: "Appears on:"
              - link "SeedDNS" [ref=e6118] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#seeddns"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e6119]: SeedDNSProviderConfig configures a DNS provider.
            - table [ref=e6120]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e6121]:
                - row [ref=e6122]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e6123]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e6124]
              - rowgroup [ref=e6125]:
                - row [ref=e6126]:
                  - cell [ref=e6127]:
                    - code [ref=e6128]: type
                    - emphasis [ref=e6129]: string
                  - cell [ref=e6130]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e6131]: Type is the type of the DNS provider.
                - row [ref=e6132]:
                  - cell [ref=e6133]:
                    - code [ref=e6134]: domain
                    - emphasis [ref=e6135]: string
                  - cell [ref=e6136]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e6137]: Domain is the domain name to be used by the DNS provider.
                - row [ref=e6138]:
                  - cell [ref=e6139]:
                    - code [ref=e6140]: zone
                    - emphasis [ref=e6141]: string
                  - cell [ref=e6142]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e6143]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e6144]: Zone is the zone where the DNS records are managed.
                - row [ref=e6145]:
                  - cell [ref=e6146]:
                    - code [ref=e6147]: credentialsRef
                    - emphasis [ref=e6148]:
                      - link "ObjectReference" [ref=e6149] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#objectreference-v1-core
                  - cell [ref=e6150]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e6151]: CredentialsRef is a reference to a resource holding the credentials used forauthentication with the DNS provider.Supported referenced resources are v1.Secrets andsecurity.gardener.cloud/v1alpha1.WorkloadIdentity
            - heading "SeedNetworks" [level=3] [ref=e6152]
            - paragraph [ref=e6153]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e6154]: "Appears on:"
              - link "SeedSpec" [ref=e6155] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#seedspec"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e6156]: SeedNetworks contains CIDRs for the pod, service and node networks of a Kubernetes cluster.
            - table [ref=e6157]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e6158]:
                - row [ref=e6159]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e6160]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e6161]
              - rowgroup [ref=e6162]:
                - row [ref=e6163]:
                  - cell [ref=e6164]:
                    - code [ref=e6165]: nodes
                    - emphasis [ref=e6166]: string
                  - cell [ref=e6167]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e6168]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e6169]: Nodes is the CIDR of the node network. This field is immutable.
                - row [ref=e6170]:
                  - cell [ref=e6171]:
                    - code [ref=e6172]: pods
                    - emphasis [ref=e6173]: string
                  - cell [ref=e6174]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e6175]: Pods is the CIDR of the pod network. This field is immutable.
                - row [ref=e6176]:
                  - cell [ref=e6177]:
                    - code [ref=e6178]: services
                    - emphasis [ref=e6179]: string
                  - cell [ref=e6180]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e6181]: Services is the CIDR of the service network. This field is immutable.
                - row [ref=e6182]:
                  - cell [ref=e6183]:
                    - code [ref=e6184]: shootDefaults
                    - emphasis [ref=e6185]:
                      - link "ShootNetworks" [ref=e6186] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#shootnetworks"
                  - cell [ref=e6187]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e6188]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e6189]: ShootDefaults contains the default networks CIDRs for shoots.
                - row [ref=e6190]:
                  - cell [ref=e6191]:
                    - code [ref=e6192]: blockCIDRs
                    - emphasis [ref=e6193]: string array
                  - cell [ref=e6194]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e6195]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e6196]: BlockCIDRs is a list of network addresses that should be blocked for shoot control plane components runningin the seed cluster.
                - row [ref=e6197]:
                  - cell [ref=e6198]:
                    - code [ref=e6199]: ipFamilies
                    - emphasis [ref=e6200]:
                      - link "IPFamily" [ref=e6201] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#ipfamily"
                      - text: array
                  - cell [ref=e6202]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e6203]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e6204]: IPFamilies specifies the IP protocol versions to use for seed networking. This field is immutable.See https://github.com/gardener/gardener/blob/master/docs/development/ipv6.md.Defaults to ["IPv4"].
            - heading "SeedProvider" [level=3] [ref=e6205]
            - paragraph [ref=e6206]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e6207]: "Appears on:"
              - link "SeedSpec" [ref=e6208] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#seedspec"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e6209]: SeedProvider defines the provider-specific information of this Seed cluster.
            - table [ref=e6210]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e6211]:
                - row [ref=e6212]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e6213]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e6214]
              - rowgroup [ref=e6215]:
                - row [ref=e6216]:
                  - cell [ref=e6217]:
                    - code [ref=e6218]: type
                    - emphasis [ref=e6219]: string
                  - cell [ref=e6220]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e6221]: Type is the name of the provider.
                - row [ref=e6222]:
                  - cell [ref=e6223]:
                    - code [ref=e6224]: providerConfig
                    - emphasis [ref=e6225]:
                      - link "RawExtension" [ref=e6226] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#rawextension-runtime-pkg
                  - cell [ref=e6227]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e6228]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e6229]: ProviderConfig is the configuration passed to Seed resource.
                - row [ref=e6230]:
                  - cell [ref=e6231]:
                    - code [ref=e6232]: region
                    - emphasis [ref=e6233]: string
                  - cell [ref=e6234]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e6235]: Region is a name of a region.
                - row [ref=e6236]:
                  - cell [ref=e6237]:
                    - code [ref=e6238]: zones
                    - emphasis [ref=e6239]: string array
                  - cell [ref=e6240]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e6241]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e6242]: Zones is the list of availability zones the seed cluster is deployed to.
            - heading "SeedSelector" [level=3] [ref=e6243]
            - paragraph [ref=e6244]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e6245]: "Appears on:"
              - link "CloudProfileSpec" [ref=e6246] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#cloudprofilespec"
              - text: ","
              - link "ExposureClassScheduling" [ref=e6247] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#exposureclassscheduling"
              - text: ","
              - link "ShootSpec" [ref=e6248] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#shootspec"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e6249]: SeedSelector contains constraints for selecting seed to be usable for shoots using a profile
            - table [ref=e6250]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e6251]:
                - row [ref=e6252]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e6253]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e6254]
              - rowgroup [ref=e6255]:
                - row [ref=e6256]:
                  - cell [ref=e6257]:
                    - code [ref=e6258]: matchLabels
                    - emphasis [ref=e6259]: object (keys:string, values:string)
                  - cell [ref=e6260]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e6261]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e6262]: "matchLabels is a map of {key,value} pairs. A single {key,value} in the matchLabelsmap is equivalent to an element of matchExpressions, whose key field is \"key\", theoperator is \"In\", and the values array contains only \"value\". The requirements are ANDed."
                - row [ref=e6263]:
                  - cell [ref=e6264]:
                    - code [ref=e6265]: matchExpressions
                    - emphasis [ref=e6266]:
                      - link "LabelSelectorRequirement" [ref=e6267] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#labelselectorrequirement-v1-meta
                      - text: array
                  - cell [ref=e6268]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e6269]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e6270]: matchExpressions is a list of label selector requirements. The requirements are ANDed.
                - row [ref=e6271]:
                  - cell [ref=e6272]:
                    - code [ref=e6273]: providerTypes
                    - emphasis [ref=e6274]: string array
                  - cell [ref=e6275]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e6276]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e6277]: Providers is optional and can be used by restricting seeds by their provider type. '*' can be used to enable seeds regardless of their provider type.
            - heading "SeedSettingDependencyWatchdog" [level=3] [ref=e6278]
            - paragraph [ref=e6279]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e6280]: "Appears on:"
              - link "SeedSettings" [ref=e6281] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#seedsettings"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e6282]: SeedSettingDependencyWatchdog controls the dependency-watchdog settings for the seed.
            - table [ref=e6283]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e6284]:
                - row [ref=e6285]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e6286]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e6287]
              - rowgroup [ref=e6288]:
                - row [ref=e6289]:
                  - cell [ref=e6290]:
                    - code [ref=e6291]: weeder
                    - emphasis [ref=e6292]:
                      - link "SeedSettingDependencyWatchdogWeeder" [ref=e6293] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#seedsettingdependencywatchdogweeder"
                  - cell [ref=e6294]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e6295]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e6296]: Weeder controls the weeder settings for the dependency-watchdog for the seed.
                - row [ref=e6297]:
                  - cell [ref=e6298]:
                    - code [ref=e6299]: prober
                    - emphasis [ref=e6300]:
                      - link "SeedSettingDependencyWatchdogProber" [ref=e6301] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#seedsettingdependencywatchdogprober"
                  - cell [ref=e6302]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e6303]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e6304]: Prober controls the prober settings for the dependency-watchdog for the seed.
            - heading "SeedSettingDependencyWatchdogProber" [level=3] [ref=e6305]
            - paragraph [ref=e6306]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e6307]: "Appears on:"
              - link "SeedSettingDependencyWatchdog" [ref=e6308] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#seedsettingdependencywatchdog"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e6309]: SeedSettingDependencyWatchdogProber controls the prober settings for the dependency-watchdog for the seed.
            - table [ref=e6310]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e6311]:
                - row [ref=e6312]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e6313]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e6314]
              - rowgroup [ref=e6315]:
                - row [ref=e6316]:
                  - cell [ref=e6317]:
                    - code [ref=e6318]: enabled
                    - emphasis [ref=e6319]: boolean
                  - cell [ref=e6320]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e6321]: Enabled controls whether the probe controller(prober) of the dependency-watchdog should be enabled. This controllerscales down the kube-controller-manager, machine-controller-manager and cluster-autoscaler of shoot clusters in case their respective kube-apiserver is notreachable via its external ingress in order to avoid melt-down situations.
            - heading "SeedSettingDependencyWatchdogWeeder" [level=3] [ref=e6322]
            - paragraph [ref=e6323]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e6324]: "Appears on:"
              - link "SeedSettingDependencyWatchdog" [ref=e6325] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#seedsettingdependencywatchdog"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e6326]: SeedSettingDependencyWatchdogWeeder controls the weeder settings for the dependency-watchdog for the seed.
            - table [ref=e6327]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e6328]:
                - row [ref=e6329]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e6330]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e6331]
              - rowgroup [ref=e6332]:
                - row [ref=e6333]:
                  - cell [ref=e6334]:
                    - code [ref=e6335]: enabled
                    - emphasis [ref=e6336]: boolean
                  - cell [ref=e6337]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e6338]: Enabled controls whether the endpoint controller(weeder) of the dependency-watchdog should be enabled. This controllerhelps to alleviate the delay where control plane components remain unavailable by finding the respective pods inCrashLoopBackoff status and restarting them once their dependants become ready and available again.
            - heading "SeedSettingExcessCapacityReservation" [level=3] [ref=e6339]
            - paragraph [ref=e6340]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e6341]: "Appears on:"
              - link "SeedSettings" [ref=e6342] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#seedsettings"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e6343]: SeedSettingExcessCapacityReservation controls the excess capacity reservation for shoot control planes in the seed.
            - table [ref=e6344]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e6345]:
                - row [ref=e6346]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e6347]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e6348]
              - rowgroup [ref=e6349]:
                - row [ref=e6350]:
                  - cell [ref=e6351]:
                    - code [ref=e6352]: enabled
                    - emphasis [ref=e6353]: boolean
                  - cell [ref=e6354]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e6355]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e6356]: Enabled controls whether the default excess capacity reservation should be enabled. When not specified, the functionality is enabled.
                - row [ref=e6357]:
                  - cell [ref=e6358]:
                    - code [ref=e6359]: configs
                    - emphasis [ref=e6360]:
                      - link "SeedSettingExcessCapacityReservationConfig" [ref=e6361] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#seedsettingexcesscapacityreservationconfig"
                      - text: array
                  - cell [ref=e6362]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e6363]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e6364]: Configs configures excess capacity reservation deployments for shoot control planes in the seed.
            - heading "SeedSettingExcessCapacityReservationConfig" [level=3] [ref=e6365]
            - paragraph [ref=e6366]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e6367]: "Appears on:"
              - link "SeedSettingExcessCapacityReservation" [ref=e6368] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#seedsettingexcesscapacityreservation"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e6369]: SeedSettingExcessCapacityReservationConfig configures excess capacity reservation deployments for shoot control planes in the seed.
            - table [ref=e6370]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e6371]:
                - row [ref=e6372]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e6373]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e6374]
              - rowgroup [ref=e6375]:
                - row [ref=e6376]:
                  - cell [ref=e6377]:
                    - code [ref=e6378]: nodeSelector
                    - emphasis [ref=e6379]: object (keys:string, values:string)
                  - cell [ref=e6380]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e6381]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e6382]: NodeSelector specifies the node where the excess-capacity-reservation pod should run.
                - row [ref=e6383]:
                  - cell [ref=e6384]:
                    - code [ref=e6385]: tolerations
                    - emphasis [ref=e6386]:
                      - link "Toleration" [ref=e6387] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#toleration-v1-core
                      - text: array
                  - cell [ref=e6388]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e6389]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e6390]: Tolerations specify the tolerations for the the excess-capacity-reservation pod.
            - heading "SeedSettingLoadBalancerServices" [level=3] [ref=e6391]
            - paragraph [ref=e6392]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e6393]: "Appears on:"
              - link "SeedSettings" [ref=e6394] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#seedsettings"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e6395]: SeedSettingLoadBalancerServices controls certain settings for services of type load balancer that are created in the seed.
            - table [ref=e6396]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e6397]:
                - row [ref=e6398]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e6399]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e6400]
              - rowgroup [ref=e6401]:
                - row [ref=e6402]:
                  - cell [ref=e6403]:
                    - code [ref=e6404]: annotations
                    - emphasis [ref=e6405]: object (keys:string, values:string)
                  - cell [ref=e6406]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e6407]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e6408]: Annotations is a map of annotations that will be injected/merged into every load balancer service object.
                - row [ref=e6409]:
                  - cell [ref=e6410]:
                    - code [ref=e6411]: externalTrafficPolicy
                    - emphasis [ref=e6412]:
                      - link "ServiceExternalTrafficPolicy" [ref=e6413] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#serviceexternaltrafficpolicy-v1-core
                  - cell [ref=e6414]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e6415]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e6416]: ExternalTrafficPolicy describes how nodes distribute service traffic theyreceive on one of the service's "externally-facing" addresses.Defaults to "Cluster".
                - row [ref=e6417]:
                  - cell [ref=e6418]:
                    - code [ref=e6419]: zones
                    - emphasis [ref=e6420]:
                      - link "SeedSettingLoadBalancerServicesZones" [ref=e6421] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#seedsettingloadbalancerserviceszones"
                      - text: array
                  - cell [ref=e6422]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e6423]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e6424]: Zones controls settings, which are specific to the single-zone load balancers in a multi-zonal setup.Can be empty for single-zone seeds. Each specified zone has to relate to one of the zones in seed.spec.provider.zones.
                - row [ref=e6425]:
                  - cell [ref=e6426]:
                    - code [ref=e6427]: proxyProtocol
                    - emphasis [ref=e6428]:
                      - link "LoadBalancerServicesProxyProtocol" [ref=e6429] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#loadbalancerservicesproxyprotocol"
                  - cell [ref=e6430]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e6431]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e6432]: ProxyProtocol controls whether ProxyProtocol is (optionally) allowed for the load balancer services.Defaults to nil, which is equivalent to not allowing ProxyProtocol.
                - row [ref=e6433]:
                  - cell [ref=e6434]:
                    - code [ref=e6435]: zonalIngress
                    - emphasis [ref=e6436]:
                      - link "SeedSettingLoadBalancerServicesZonalIngress" [ref=e6437] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#seedsettingloadbalancerserviceszonalingress"
                  - cell [ref=e6438]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e6439]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e6440]: ZonalIngress controls whether ingress gateways are deployed per availability zone.Defaults to true.
                - row [ref=e6441]:
                  - cell [ref=e6442]:
                    - code [ref=e6443]: class
                    - emphasis [ref=e6444]: string
                  - cell [ref=e6445]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e6446]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e6447]: Class configures the Service.spec.loadBalancerClass field for the load balancer services on the seed.Note that changing the loadBalancerClass of existing LoadBalancer services is denied by Kubernetes.
            - heading "SeedSettingLoadBalancerServicesZonalIngress" [level=3] [ref=e6448]
            - paragraph [ref=e6449]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e6450]: "Appears on:"
              - link "SeedSettingLoadBalancerServices" [ref=e6451] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#seedsettingloadbalancerservices"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e6452]: SeedSettingLoadBalancerServicesZonalIngress controls the deployment of ingress gateways per availability zone.
            - table [ref=e6453]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e6454]:
                - row [ref=e6455]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e6456]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e6457]
              - rowgroup [ref=e6458]:
                - row [ref=e6459]:
                  - cell [ref=e6460]:
                    - code [ref=e6461]: enabled
                    - emphasis [ref=e6462]: boolean
                  - cell [ref=e6463]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e6464]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e6465]: Enabled controls whether seed ingress gateways are deployed in each availability zone.Defaults to true, which provisions an ingress gateway load balancer for each availability zone.When disabled, only a single ingress gateway is deployed.See https://github.com/gardener/gardener/blob/master/docs/operations/seed_settings.md#zonal-ingress.
            - heading "SeedSettingLoadBalancerServicesZones" [level=3] [ref=e6466]
            - paragraph [ref=e6467]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e6468]: "Appears on:"
              - link "SeedSettingLoadBalancerServices" [ref=e6469] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#seedsettingloadbalancerservices"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e6470]: SeedSettingLoadBalancerServicesZones controls settings, which are specific to the single-zone load balancers in a multi-zonal setup.
            - table [ref=e6471]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e6472]:
                - row [ref=e6473]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e6474]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e6475]
              - rowgroup [ref=e6476]:
                - row [ref=e6477]:
                  - cell [ref=e6478]:
                    - code [ref=e6479]: name
                    - emphasis [ref=e6480]: string
                  - cell [ref=e6481]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e6482]: Name is the name of the zone as specified in seed.spec.provider.zones.
                - row [ref=e6483]:
                  - cell [ref=e6484]:
                    - code [ref=e6485]: annotations
                    - emphasis [ref=e6486]: object (keys:string, values:string)
                  - cell [ref=e6487]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e6488]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e6489]: Annotations is a map of annotations that will be injected/merged into the zone-specific load balancer service object.
                - row [ref=e6490]:
                  - cell [ref=e6491]:
                    - code [ref=e6492]: externalTrafficPolicy
                    - emphasis [ref=e6493]:
                      - link "ServiceExternalTrafficPolicy" [ref=e6494] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#serviceexternaltrafficpolicy-v1-core
                  - cell [ref=e6495]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e6496]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e6497]: ExternalTrafficPolicy describes how nodes distribute service traffic theyreceive on one of the service's "externally-facing" addresses.Defaults to "Cluster".
                - row [ref=e6498]:
                  - cell [ref=e6499]:
                    - code [ref=e6500]: proxyProtocol
                    - emphasis [ref=e6501]:
                      - link "LoadBalancerServicesProxyProtocol" [ref=e6502] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#loadbalancerservicesproxyprotocol"
                  - cell [ref=e6503]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e6504]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e6505]: ProxyProtocol controls whether ProxyProtocol is (optionally) allowed for the load balancer services.Defaults to nil, which is equivalent to not allowing ProxyProtocol.
            - heading "SeedSettingPersistentVolumeClaimAutoscaler" [level=3] [ref=e6506]
            - paragraph [ref=e6507]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e6508]: "Appears on:"
              - link "SeedSettings" [ref=e6509] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#seedsettings"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e6510]: SeedSettingPersistentVolumeClaimAutoscaler controls certain settings for the persistent volume claim autoscaler components deployed in the seed.
            - table [ref=e6511]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e6512]:
                - row [ref=e6513]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e6514]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e6515]
              - rowgroup [ref=e6516]:
                - row [ref=e6517]:
                  - cell [ref=e6518]:
                    - code [ref=e6519]: enabled
                    - emphasis [ref=e6520]: boolean
                  - cell [ref=e6521]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e6522]: Enabled controls whether the PVC Autoscaler components shall be deployed into the garden namespace in the seed cluster.
            - heading "SeedSettingScheduling" [level=3] [ref=e6523]
            - paragraph [ref=e6524]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e6525]: "Appears on:"
              - link "SeedSettings" [ref=e6526] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#seedsettings"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e6527]: SeedSettingScheduling controls settings for scheduling decisions for the seed.
            - table [ref=e6528]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e6529]:
                - row [ref=e6530]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e6531]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e6532]
              - rowgroup [ref=e6533]:
                - row [ref=e6534]:
                  - cell [ref=e6535]:
                    - code [ref=e6536]: visible
                    - emphasis [ref=e6537]: boolean
                  - cell [ref=e6538]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e6539]: Visible controls whether the gardener-scheduler shall consider this seed when scheduling shoots. Invisible seedsare not considered by the scheduler.
            - heading "SeedSettingTopologyAwareRouting" [level=3] [ref=e6540]
            - paragraph [ref=e6541]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e6542]: "Appears on:"
              - link "SeedSettings" [ref=e6543] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#seedsettings"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e6544]: SeedSettingTopologyAwareRouting controls certain settings for topology-aware traffic routing in the seed. See https://github.com/gardener/gardener/blob/master/docs/operations/topology_aware_routing.md.
            - table [ref=e6545]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e6546]:
                - row [ref=e6547]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e6548]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e6549]
              - rowgroup [ref=e6550]:
                - row [ref=e6551]:
                  - cell [ref=e6552]:
                    - code [ref=e6553]: enabled
                    - emphasis [ref=e6554]: boolean
                  - cell [ref=e6555]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e6556]: Enabled controls whether certain Services deployed in the seed cluster should be topology-aware.These Services are etcd-main-client, etcd-events-client, kube-apiserver, gardener-resource-manager and vpa-webhook.
            - heading "SeedSettingVerticalPodAutoscaler" [level=3] [ref=e6557]
            - paragraph [ref=e6558]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e6559]: "Appears on:"
              - link "SeedSettings" [ref=e6560] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#seedsettings"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e6561]: SeedSettingVerticalPodAutoscaler controls certain settings for the vertical pod autoscaler components deployed in the seed.
            - table [ref=e6562]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e6563]:
                - row [ref=e6564]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e6565]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e6566]
              - rowgroup [ref=e6567]:
                - row [ref=e6568]:
                  - cell [ref=e6569]:
                    - code [ref=e6570]: enabled
                    - emphasis [ref=e6571]: boolean
                  - cell [ref=e6572]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e6573]: Enabled controls whether the VPA components shall be deployed into the garden namespace in the seed cluster. Itis enabled by default because Gardener heavily relies on a VPA being deployed. You should only disable this ifyour seed cluster already has another, manually/custom managed VPA deployment.
                - row [ref=e6574]:
                  - cell [ref=e6575]:
                    - code [ref=e6576]: featureGates
                    - emphasis [ref=e6577]: object (keys:string, values:boolean)
                  - cell [ref=e6578]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e6579]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e6580]: FeatureGates contains information about enabled feature gates.
            - heading "SeedSettingZoneSelection" [level=3] [ref=e6581]
            - paragraph [ref=e6582]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e6583]: "Appears on:"
              - link "SeedSettings" [ref=e6584] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#seedsettings"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e6585]: SeedSettingZoneSelection controls whether shoot control plane zone placement is derived from the shoot's worker pool zones rather than randomly selected from seed zones.
            - table [ref=e6586]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e6587]:
                - row [ref=e6588]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e6589]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e6590]
              - rowgroup [ref=e6591]:
                - row [ref=e6592]:
                  - cell [ref=e6593]:
                    - code [ref=e6594]: mode
                    - emphasis [ref=e6595]:
                      - link "ZoneSelectionMode" [ref=e6596] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#zoneselectionmode"
                  - cell [ref=e6597]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e6598]: Mode controls the zone selection behavior."Prefer" tries to match worker pool zones to seed zones, falling back to random selection on mismatch."Enforce" requires worker pool zones to be present in the seed's zone list; scheduling fails otherwise.
            - heading "SeedSettings" [level=3] [ref=e6599]
            - paragraph [ref=e6600]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e6601]: "Appears on:"
              - link "SeedSpec" [ref=e6602] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#seedspec"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e6603]: SeedSettings contains certain settings for this seed cluster.
            - table [ref=e6604]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e6605]:
                - row [ref=e6606]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e6607]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e6608]
              - rowgroup [ref=e6609]:
                - row [ref=e6610]:
                  - cell [ref=e6611]:
                    - code [ref=e6612]: excessCapacityReservation
                    - emphasis [ref=e6613]:
                      - link "SeedSettingExcessCapacityReservation" [ref=e6614] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#seedsettingexcesscapacityreservation"
                  - cell [ref=e6615]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e6616]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e6617]: ExcessCapacityReservation controls the excess capacity reservation for shoot control planes in the seed.
                - row [ref=e6618]:
                  - cell [ref=e6619]:
                    - code [ref=e6620]: scheduling
                    - emphasis [ref=e6621]:
                      - link "SeedSettingScheduling" [ref=e6622] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#seedsettingscheduling"
                  - cell [ref=e6623]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e6624]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e6625]: Scheduling controls settings for scheduling decisions for the seed.
                - row [ref=e6626]:
                  - cell [ref=e6627]:
                    - code [ref=e6628]: loadBalancerServices
                    - emphasis [ref=e6629]:
                      - link "SeedSettingLoadBalancerServices" [ref=e6630] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#seedsettingloadbalancerservices"
                  - cell [ref=e6631]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e6632]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e6633]: LoadBalancerServices controls certain settings for services of type load balancer that are created in the seed.
                - row [ref=e6634]:
                  - cell [ref=e6635]:
                    - code [ref=e6636]: verticalPodAutoscaler
                    - emphasis [ref=e6637]:
                      - link "SeedSettingVerticalPodAutoscaler" [ref=e6638] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#seedsettingverticalpodautoscaler"
                  - cell [ref=e6639]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e6640]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e6641]: VerticalPodAutoscaler controls certain settings for the vertical pod autoscaler components deployed in the seed.
                - row [ref=e6642]:
                  - cell [ref=e6643]:
                    - code [ref=e6644]: dependencyWatchdog
                    - emphasis [ref=e6645]:
                      - link "SeedSettingDependencyWatchdog" [ref=e6646] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#seedsettingdependencywatchdog"
                  - cell [ref=e6647]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e6648]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e6649]: DependencyWatchdog controls certain settings for the dependency-watchdog components deployed in the seed.
                - row [ref=e6650]:
                  - cell [ref=e6651]:
                    - code [ref=e6652]: topologyAwareRouting
                    - emphasis [ref=e6653]:
                      - link "SeedSettingTopologyAwareRouting" [ref=e6654] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#seedsettingtopologyawarerouting"
                  - cell [ref=e6655]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e6656]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e6657]: TopologyAwareRouting controls certain settings for topology-aware traffic routing in the seed.See https://github.com/gardener/gardener/blob/master/docs/operations/topology_aware_routing.md.
                - row [ref=e6658]:
                  - cell [ref=e6659]:
                    - code [ref=e6660]: zoneSelection
                    - emphasis [ref=e6661]:
                      - link "SeedSettingZoneSelection" [ref=e6662] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#seedsettingzoneselection"
                  - cell [ref=e6663]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e6664]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e6665]: ZoneSelection controls whether shoot control plane zone placement is derived from the shoot's worker pool zonesrather than randomly selected from seed zones.See https://github.com/gardener/gardener/blob/master/docs/operations/seed_settings.md#zone-selection.
                - row [ref=e6666]:
                  - cell [ref=e6667]:
                    - code [ref=e6668]: persistentVolumeClaimAutoscaler
                    - emphasis [ref=e6669]:
                      - link "SeedSettingPersistentVolumeClaimAutoscaler" [ref=e6670] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#seedsettingpersistentvolumeclaimautoscaler"
                  - cell [ref=e6671]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e6672]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e6673]: PersistentVolumeClaimAutoscaler controls certain settings for the persistent volume claim autoscaler components deployed in the seed.
            - heading "SeedSpec" [level=3] [ref=e6674]
            - paragraph [ref=e6675]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e6676]: "Appears on:"
              - link "Seed" [ref=e6677] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#seed"
              - text: ","
              - link "SeedTemplate" [ref=e6678] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#seedtemplate"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e6679]: SeedSpec is the specification of a Seed.
            - table [ref=e6680]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e6681]:
                - row [ref=e6682]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e6683]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e6684]
              - rowgroup [ref=e6685]:
                - row [ref=e6686]:
                  - cell [ref=e6687]:
                    - code [ref=e6688]: backup
                    - emphasis [ref=e6689]:
                      - link "Backup" [ref=e6690] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#backup"
                  - cell [ref=e6691]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e6692]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e6693]: Backup holds the object store configuration for the backups of shoot (currently only etcd).If it is not specified, then there won't be any backups taken for shoots associated with this seed.If backup field is present in seed, then backups of the etcd from shoot control plane will be storedunder the configured object store.
                - row [ref=e6694]:
                  - cell [ref=e6695]:
                    - code [ref=e6696]: dns
                    - emphasis [ref=e6697]:
                      - link "SeedDNS" [ref=e6698] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#seeddns"
                  - cell [ref=e6699]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e6700]: DNS contains DNS-relevant information about this seed cluster.
                - row [ref=e6701]:
                  - cell [ref=e6702]:
                    - code [ref=e6703]: networks
                    - emphasis [ref=e6704]:
                      - link "SeedNetworks" [ref=e6705] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#seednetworks"
                  - cell [ref=e6706]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e6707]: Networks defines the pod, service and worker network of the Seed cluster.
                - row [ref=e6708]:
                  - cell [ref=e6709]:
                    - code [ref=e6710]: provider
                    - emphasis [ref=e6711]:
                      - link "SeedProvider" [ref=e6712] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#seedprovider"
                  - cell [ref=e6713]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e6714]: Provider defines the provider type and region for this Seed cluster.
                - row [ref=e6715]:
                  - cell [ref=e6716]:
                    - code [ref=e6717]: taints
                    - emphasis [ref=e6718]:
                      - link "SeedTaint" [ref=e6719] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#seedtaint"
                      - text: array
                  - cell [ref=e6720]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e6721]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e6722]: Taints describes taints on the seed.
                - row [ref=e6723]:
                  - cell [ref=e6724]:
                    - code [ref=e6725]: volume
                    - emphasis [ref=e6726]:
                      - link "SeedVolume" [ref=e6727] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#seedvolume"
                  - cell [ref=e6728]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e6729]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e6730]: Volume contains settings for persistentvolumes created in the seed cluster.
                - row [ref=e6731]:
                  - cell [ref=e6732]:
                    - code [ref=e6733]: settings
                    - emphasis [ref=e6734]:
                      - link "SeedSettings" [ref=e6735] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#seedsettings"
                  - cell [ref=e6736]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e6737]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e6738]: Settings contains certain settings for this seed cluster.
                - row [ref=e6739]:
                  - cell [ref=e6740]:
                    - code [ref=e6741]: ingress
                    - emphasis [ref=e6742]:
                      - link "Ingress" [ref=e6743] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#ingress"
                  - cell [ref=e6744]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e6745]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e6746]: Ingress configures Ingress specific settings of the Seed cluster. This field is immutable.
                - row [ref=e6747]:
                  - cell [ref=e6748]:
                    - code [ref=e6749]: accessRestrictions
                    - emphasis [ref=e6750]:
                      - link "AccessRestriction" [ref=e6751] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#accessrestriction"
                      - text: array
                  - cell [ref=e6752]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e6753]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e6754]: AccessRestrictions describe a list of access restrictions for this seed cluster.
                - row [ref=e6755]:
                  - cell [ref=e6756]:
                    - code [ref=e6757]: extensions
                    - emphasis [ref=e6758]:
                      - link "Extension" [ref=e6759] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#extension"
                      - text: array
                  - cell [ref=e6760]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e6761]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e6762]: Extensions contain type and provider information for Seed extensions.
                - row [ref=e6763]:
                  - cell [ref=e6764]:
                    - code [ref=e6765]: resources
                    - emphasis [ref=e6766]:
                      - link "NamedResourceReference" [ref=e6767] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#namedresourcereference"
                      - text: array
                  - cell [ref=e6768]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e6769]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e6770]: Resources holds a list of named resource references that can be referred to in extension configs by their names.
            - heading "SeedStatus" [level=3] [ref=e6771]
            - paragraph [ref=e6772]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e6773]: "Appears on:"
              - link "Seed" [ref=e6774] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#seed"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e6775]: SeedStatus is the status of a Seed.
            - table [ref=e6776]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e6777]:
                - row [ref=e6778]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e6779]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e6780]
              - rowgroup [ref=e6781]:
                - row [ref=e6782]:
                  - cell [ref=e6783]:
                    - code [ref=e6784]: gardener
                    - emphasis [ref=e6785]:
                      - link "Gardener" [ref=e6786] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#gardener"
                  - cell [ref=e6787]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e6788]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e6789]: Gardener holds information about the Gardener which last acted on the Shoot.
                - row [ref=e6790]:
                  - cell [ref=e6791]:
                    - code [ref=e6792]: kubernetesVersion
                    - emphasis [ref=e6793]: string
                  - cell [ref=e6794]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e6795]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e6796]: KubernetesVersion is the Kubernetes version of the seed cluster.
                - row [ref=e6797]:
                  - cell [ref=e6798]:
                    - code [ref=e6799]: conditions
                    - emphasis [ref=e6800]:
                      - link "Condition" [ref=e6801] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#condition"
                      - text: array
                  - cell [ref=e6802]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e6803]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e6804]: Conditions represents the latest available observations of a Seed's current state based on health checks.
                - row [ref=e6805]:
                  - cell [ref=e6806]:
                    - code [ref=e6807]: observedGeneration
                    - emphasis [ref=e6808]: integer
                  - cell [ref=e6809]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e6810]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e6811]: ObservedGeneration is the most recent generation observed for this Seed. It corresponds to theSeed's generation, which is updated on mutation by the API Server.
                - row [ref=e6812]:
                  - cell [ref=e6813]:
                    - code [ref=e6814]: clusterIdentity
                    - emphasis [ref=e6815]: string
                  - cell [ref=e6816]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e6817]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e6818]: ClusterIdentity is the identity of the Seed cluster. This field is immutable.
                - row [ref=e6819]:
                  - cell [ref=e6820]:
                    - code [ref=e6821]: clientCertificateExpirationTimestamp
                    - emphasis [ref=e6822]:
                      - link "Time" [ref=e6823] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#time-v1-meta
                  - cell [ref=e6824]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e6825]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e6826]: ClientCertificateExpirationTimestamp is the timestamp at which gardenlet's client certificate expires.
                - row [ref=e6827]:
                  - cell [ref=e6828]:
                    - code [ref=e6829]: lastOperation
                    - emphasis [ref=e6830]:
                      - link "LastOperation" [ref=e6831] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#lastoperation"
                  - cell [ref=e6832]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e6833]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e6834]: LastOperation holds information about the last operation on the Seed.
                - row [ref=e6835]:
                  - cell [ref=e6836]:
                    - code [ref=e6837]: constraints
                    - emphasis [ref=e6838]:
                      - link "Condition" [ref=e6839] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#condition"
                      - text: array
                  - cell [ref=e6840]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e6841]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e6842]: Constraints represents conditions of a Seed's current state that constraint some operations on it.
            - heading "SeedTaint" [level=3] [ref=e6843]
            - paragraph [ref=e6844]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e6845]: "Appears on:"
              - link "SeedSpec" [ref=e6846] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#seedspec"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e6847]: SeedTaint describes a taint on a seed.
            - table [ref=e6848]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e6849]:
                - row [ref=e6850]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e6851]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e6852]
              - rowgroup [ref=e6853]:
                - row [ref=e6854]:
                  - cell [ref=e6855]:
                    - code [ref=e6856]: key
                    - emphasis [ref=e6857]: string
                  - cell [ref=e6858]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e6859]: Key is the taint key to be applied to a seed.
                - row [ref=e6860]:
                  - cell [ref=e6861]:
                    - code [ref=e6862]: value
                    - emphasis [ref=e6863]: string
                  - cell [ref=e6864]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e6865]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e6866]: Value is the taint value corresponding to the taint key.
            - heading "SeedTemplate" [level=3] [ref=e6867]
            - paragraph [ref=e6868]: SeedTemplate is a template for creating a Seed object.
            - table [ref=e6869]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e6870]:
                - row [ref=e6871]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e6872]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e6873]
              - rowgroup [ref=e6874]:
                - row [ref=e6875]:
                  - cell [ref=e6876]:
                    - code [ref=e6877]: metadata
                    - emphasis [ref=e6878]:
                      - link "ObjectMeta" [ref=e6879] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#objectmeta-v1-meta
                  - cell [ref=e6880]:
                    - text: Refer to the Kubernetes API documentation for the fields of the
                    - code [ref=e6881]: metadata
                    - text: field.
                - row [ref=e6882]:
                  - cell [ref=e6883]:
                    - code [ref=e6884]: spec
                    - emphasis [ref=e6885]:
                      - link "SeedSpec" [ref=e6886] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#seedspec"
                  - cell [ref=e6887]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e6888]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e6889]: Specification of the desired behavior of the Seed.
            - heading "SeedVolume" [level=3] [ref=e6890]
            - paragraph [ref=e6891]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e6892]: "Appears on:"
              - link "SeedSpec" [ref=e6893] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#seedspec"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e6894]: SeedVolume contains settings for persistentvolumes created in the seed cluster.
            - table [ref=e6895]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e6896]:
                - row [ref=e6897]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e6898]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e6899]
              - rowgroup [ref=e6900]:
                - row [ref=e6901]:
                  - cell [ref=e6902]:
                    - code [ref=e6903]: minimumSize
                    - emphasis [ref=e6904]:
                      - link "Quantity" [ref=e6905] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#quantity-resource-api
                  - cell [ref=e6906]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e6907]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e6908]: MinimumSize defines the minimum size that should be used for PVCs in the seed.
                - row [ref=e6909]:
                  - cell [ref=e6910]:
                    - code [ref=e6911]: providers
                    - emphasis [ref=e6912]:
                      - link "SeedVolumeProvider" [ref=e6913] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#seedvolumeprovider"
                      - text: array
                  - cell [ref=e6914]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e6915]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e6916]: Providers is a list of storage class provisioner types for the seed.
            - heading "SeedVolumeProvider" [level=3] [ref=e6917]
            - paragraph [ref=e6918]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e6919]: "Appears on:"
              - link "SeedVolume" [ref=e6920] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#seedvolume"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e6921]: SeedVolumeProvider is a storage class provisioner type.
            - table [ref=e6922]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e6923]:
                - row [ref=e6924]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e6925]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e6926]
              - rowgroup [ref=e6927]:
                - row [ref=e6928]:
                  - cell [ref=e6929]:
                    - code [ref=e6930]: purpose
                    - emphasis [ref=e6931]: string
                  - cell [ref=e6932]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e6933]: Purpose is the purpose of this provider.
                - row [ref=e6934]:
                  - cell [ref=e6935]:
                    - code [ref=e6936]: name
                    - emphasis [ref=e6937]: string
                  - cell [ref=e6938]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e6939]: Name is the name of the storage class provisioner type.
            - heading "ServiceAccountConfig" [level=3] [ref=e6940]
            - paragraph [ref=e6941]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e6942]: "Appears on:"
              - link "KubeAPIServerConfig" [ref=e6943] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#kubeapiserverconfig"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e6944]: ServiceAccountConfig is the kube-apiserver configuration for service accounts.
            - table [ref=e6945]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e6946]:
                - row [ref=e6947]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e6948]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e6949]
