              - rowgroup [ref=e6950]:
                - row [ref=e6951]:
                  - cell [ref=e6952]:
                    - code [ref=e6953]: issuer
                    - emphasis [ref=e6954]: string
                  - cell [ref=e6955]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e6956]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e6957]: Issuer is the identifier of the service account token issuer. The issuer will assert thisidentifier in "iss" claim of issued tokens. This value is used to generate new service account tokens.This value is a string or URI. Defaults to URI of the API server.
                - row [ref=e6958]:
                  - cell [ref=e6959]:
                    - code [ref=e6960]: extendTokenExpiration
                    - emphasis [ref=e6961]: boolean
                  - cell [ref=e6962]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e6963]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e6964]: ExtendTokenExpiration turns on projected service account expiration extension during token generation, whichhelps safe transition from legacy token to bound service account token feature. If this flag is enabled,admission injected tokens would be extended up to 1 year to prevent unexpected failure during transition,ignoring value of service-account-max-token-expiration.
                - row [ref=e6965]:
                  - cell [ref=e6966]:
                    - code [ref=e6967]: maxTokenExpiration
                    - emphasis [ref=e6968]:
                      - link "Duration" [ref=e6969] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#duration-v1-meta
                  - cell [ref=e6970]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e6971]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e6972]: MaxTokenExpiration is the maximum validity duration of a token created by the service account token issuer. If anotherwise valid TokenRequest with a validity duration larger than this value is requested, a token will be issuedwith a validity duration of this value.This field must be within [30d,90d].
                - row [ref=e6973]:
                  - cell [ref=e6974]:
                    - code [ref=e6975]: acceptedIssuers
                    - emphasis [ref=e6976]: string array
                  - cell [ref=e6977]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e6978]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e6979]: AcceptedIssuers is an additional set of issuers that are used to determine which service account tokens are accepted.These values are not used to generate new service account tokens. Only useful when service account tokens are alsoissued by another external system or a change of the current issuer that is used for generating tokens is being performed.
            - heading "ServiceAccountKeyRotation" [level=3] [ref=e6980]
            - paragraph [ref=e6981]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e6982]: "Appears on:"
              - link "ShootCredentialsRotation" [ref=e6983] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#shootcredentialsrotation"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e6984]: ServiceAccountKeyRotation contains information about the service account key credential rotation.
            - table [ref=e6985]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e6986]:
                - row [ref=e6987]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e6988]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e6989]
              - rowgroup [ref=e6990]:
                - row [ref=e6991]:
                  - cell [ref=e6992]:
                    - code [ref=e6993]: phase
                    - emphasis [ref=e6994]:
                      - link "CredentialsRotationPhase" [ref=e6995] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#credentialsrotationphase"
                  - cell [ref=e6996]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e6997]: Phase describes the phase of the service account key credential rotation.
                - row [ref=e6998]:
                  - cell [ref=e6999]:
                    - code [ref=e7000]: lastCompletionTime
                    - emphasis [ref=e7001]:
                      - link "Time" [ref=e7002] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#time-v1-meta
                  - cell [ref=e7003]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e7004]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e7005]: LastCompletionTime is the most recent time when the service account key credential rotation was successfullycompleted.
                - row [ref=e7006]:
                  - cell [ref=e7007]:
                    - code [ref=e7008]: lastInitiationTime
                    - emphasis [ref=e7009]:
                      - link "Time" [ref=e7010] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#time-v1-meta
                  - cell [ref=e7011]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e7012]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e7013]: LastInitiationTime is the most recent time when the service account key credential rotation was initiated.
                - row [ref=e7014]:
                  - cell [ref=e7015]:
                    - code [ref=e7016]: lastInitiationFinishedTime
                    - emphasis [ref=e7017]:
                      - link "Time" [ref=e7018] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#time-v1-meta
                  - cell [ref=e7019]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e7020]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e7021]: LastInitiationFinishedTime is the recent time when the service account key credential rotation initiation wascompleted.
                - row [ref=e7022]:
                  - cell [ref=e7023]:
                    - code [ref=e7024]: lastCompletionTriggeredTime
                    - emphasis [ref=e7025]:
                      - link "Time" [ref=e7026] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#time-v1-meta
                  - cell [ref=e7027]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e7028]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e7029]: LastCompletionTriggeredTime is the recent time when the service account key credential rotation completion wastriggered.
                - row [ref=e7030]:
                  - cell [ref=e7031]:
                    - code [ref=e7032]: pendingWorkersRollouts
                    - emphasis [ref=e7033]:
                      - link "PendingWorkersRollout" [ref=e7034] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#pendingworkersrollout"
                      - text: array
                  - cell [ref=e7035]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e7036]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e7037]: PendingWorkersRollouts contains the name of a worker pool and the initiation time of their last rollout due tocredentials rotation.
            - heading "Shoot" [level=3] [ref=e7038]
            - paragraph [ref=e7039]: Shoot represents a Shoot cluster created and managed by Gardener.
            - table [ref=e7040]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e7041]:
                - row [ref=e7042]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e7043]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e7044]
              - rowgroup [ref=e7045]:
                - row [ref=e7046]:
                  - cell [ref=e7047]:
                    - code [ref=e7048]: metadata
                    - emphasis [ref=e7049]:
                      - link "ObjectMeta" [ref=e7050] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#objectmeta-v1-meta
                  - cell [ref=e7051]:
                    - text: Refer to the Kubernetes API documentation for the fields of the
                    - code [ref=e7052]: metadata
                    - text: field.
                - row [ref=e7053]:
                  - cell [ref=e7054]:
                    - code [ref=e7055]: spec
                    - emphasis [ref=e7056]:
                      - link "ShootSpec" [ref=e7057] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#shootspec"
                  - cell [ref=e7058]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e7059]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e7060]: Specification of the Shoot cluster.If the object's deletion timestamp is set, this field is immutable.
                - row [ref=e7061]:
                  - cell [ref=e7062]:
                    - code [ref=e7063]: status
                    - emphasis [ref=e7064]:
                      - link "ShootStatus" [ref=e7065] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#shootstatus"
                  - cell [ref=e7066]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e7067]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e7068]: Most recently observed status of the Shoot cluster.
            - heading "ShootAdvertisedAddress" [level=3] [ref=e7069]
            - paragraph [ref=e7070]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e7071]: "Appears on:"
              - link "ShootStatus" [ref=e7072] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#shootstatus"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e7073]: ShootAdvertisedAddress contains information for the shoot's Kube API server.
            - table [ref=e7074]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e7075]:
                - row [ref=e7076]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e7077]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e7078]
              - rowgroup [ref=e7079]:
                - row [ref=e7080]:
                  - cell [ref=e7081]:
                    - code [ref=e7082]: name
                    - emphasis [ref=e7083]: string
                  - cell [ref=e7084]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e7085]: Name of the advertised address. e.g. external
                - row [ref=e7086]:
                  - cell [ref=e7087]:
                    - code [ref=e7088]: url
                    - emphasis [ref=e7089]: string
                  - cell [ref=e7090]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e7091]: The URL of the API Server. e.g. https://api.foo.bar or https://1.2.3.4
                - row [ref=e7092]:
                  - cell [ref=e7093]:
                    - code [ref=e7094]: application
                    - emphasis [ref=e7095]: string
                  - cell [ref=e7096]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e7097]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e7098]: Application is the name of the application this address belongs to. Used by UI clients.
            - heading "ShootCredentials" [level=3] [ref=e7099]
            - paragraph [ref=e7100]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e7101]: "Appears on:"
              - link "ShootStatus" [ref=e7102] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#shootstatus"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e7103]: ShootCredentials contains information about the shoot credentials.
            - table [ref=e7104]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e7105]:
                - row [ref=e7106]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e7107]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e7108]
              - rowgroup [ref=e7109]:
                - row [ref=e7110]:
                  - cell [ref=e7111]:
                    - code [ref=e7112]: rotation
                    - emphasis [ref=e7113]:
                      - link "ShootCredentialsRotation" [ref=e7114] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#shootcredentialsrotation"
                  - cell [ref=e7115]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e7116]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e7117]: Rotation contains information about the credential rotations.
                - row [ref=e7118]:
                  - cell [ref=e7119]:
                    - code [ref=e7120]: encryptionAtRest
                    - emphasis [ref=e7121]:
                      - link "EncryptionAtRest" [ref=e7122] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#encryptionatrest"
                  - cell [ref=e7123]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e7124]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e7125]: EncryptionAtRest contains information about Shoot data encryption at rest.
            - heading "ShootCredentialsRotation" [level=3] [ref=e7126]
            - paragraph [ref=e7127]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e7128]: "Appears on:"
              - link "ShootCredentials" [ref=e7129] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#shootcredentials"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e7130]: ShootCredentialsRotation contains information about the rotation of credentials.
            - table [ref=e7131]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e7132]:
                - row [ref=e7133]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e7134]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e7135]
              - rowgroup [ref=e7136]:
                - row [ref=e7137]:
                  - cell [ref=e7138]:
                    - code [ref=e7139]: certificateAuthorities
                    - emphasis [ref=e7140]:
                      - link "CARotation" [ref=e7141] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#carotation"
                  - cell [ref=e7142]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e7143]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e7144]: CertificateAuthorities contains information about the certificate authority credential rotation.
                - row [ref=e7145]:
                  - cell [ref=e7146]:
                    - code [ref=e7147]: sshKeypair
                    - emphasis [ref=e7148]:
                      - link "ShootSSHKeypairRotation" [ref=e7149] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#shootsshkeypairrotation"
                  - cell [ref=e7150]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e7151]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e7152]: SSHKeypair contains information about the ssh-keypair credential rotation.
                - row [ref=e7153]:
                  - cell [ref=e7154]:
                    - code [ref=e7155]: observability
                    - emphasis [ref=e7156]:
                      - link "ObservabilityRotation" [ref=e7157] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#observabilityrotation"
                  - cell [ref=e7158]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e7159]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e7160]: Observability contains information about the observability credential rotation.
                - row [ref=e7161]:
                  - cell [ref=e7162]:
                    - code [ref=e7163]: serviceAccountKey
                    - emphasis [ref=e7164]:
                      - link "ServiceAccountKeyRotation" [ref=e7165] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#serviceaccountkeyrotation"
                  - cell [ref=e7166]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e7167]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e7168]: ServiceAccountKey contains information about the service account key credential rotation.
                - row [ref=e7169]:
                  - cell [ref=e7170]:
                    - code [ref=e7171]: etcdEncryptionKey
                    - emphasis [ref=e7172]:
                      - link "ETCDEncryptionKeyRotation" [ref=e7173] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#etcdencryptionkeyrotation"
                  - cell [ref=e7174]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e7175]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e7176]: ETCDEncryptionKey contains information about the ETCD encryption key credential rotation.
            - heading "ShootKubeconfigRotation" [level=3] [ref=e7177]
            - paragraph [ref=e7178]: ShootKubeconfigRotation contains information about the kubeconfig credential rotation.
            - table [ref=e7179]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e7180]:
                - row [ref=e7181]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e7182]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e7183]
              - rowgroup [ref=e7184]:
                - row [ref=e7185]:
                  - cell [ref=e7186]:
                    - code [ref=e7187]: lastInitiationTime
                    - emphasis [ref=e7188]:
                      - link "Time" [ref=e7189] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#time-v1-meta
                  - cell [ref=e7190]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e7191]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e7192]: LastInitiationTime is the most recent time when the kubeconfig credential rotation was initiated.
                - row [ref=e7193]:
                  - cell [ref=e7194]:
                    - code [ref=e7195]: lastCompletionTime
                    - emphasis [ref=e7196]:
                      - link "Time" [ref=e7197] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#time-v1-meta
                  - cell [ref=e7198]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e7199]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e7200]: LastCompletionTime is the most recent time when the kubeconfig credential rotation was successfully completed.
            - heading "ShootMachineImage" [level=3] [ref=e7201]
            - paragraph [ref=e7202]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e7203]: "Appears on:"
              - link "Machine" [ref=e7204] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#machine"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e7205]: ShootMachineImage defines the name and the version of the shoot's machine image in any environment. Has to be defined in the respective CloudProfile.
            - table [ref=e7206]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e7207]:
                - row [ref=e7208]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e7209]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e7210]
              - rowgroup [ref=e7211]:
                - row [ref=e7212]:
                  - cell [ref=e7213]:
                    - code [ref=e7214]: name
                    - emphasis [ref=e7215]: string
                  - cell [ref=e7216]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e7217]: Name is the name of the image.
                - row [ref=e7218]:
                  - cell [ref=e7219]:
                    - code [ref=e7220]: providerConfig
                    - emphasis [ref=e7221]:
                      - link "RawExtension" [ref=e7222] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#rawextension-runtime-pkg
                  - cell [ref=e7223]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e7224]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e7225]: ProviderConfig is the shoot's individual configuration passed to an extension resource.
                - row [ref=e7226]:
                  - cell [ref=e7227]:
                    - code [ref=e7228]: version
                    - emphasis [ref=e7229]: string
                  - cell [ref=e7230]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e7231]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e7232]: Version is the version of the shoot's image.If version is not provided, it will be defaulted to the latest version from the CloudProfile.
            - heading "ShootNetworks" [level=3] [ref=e7233]
            - paragraph [ref=e7234]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e7235]: "Appears on:"
              - link "SeedNetworks" [ref=e7236] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#seednetworks"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e7237]: ShootNetworks contains the default networks CIDRs for shoots.
            - table [ref=e7238]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e7239]:
                - row [ref=e7240]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e7241]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e7242]
              - rowgroup [ref=e7243]:
                - row [ref=e7244]:
                  - cell [ref=e7245]:
                    - code [ref=e7246]: pods
                    - emphasis [ref=e7247]: string
                  - cell [ref=e7248]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e7249]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e7250]: Pods is the CIDR of the pod network.
                - row [ref=e7251]:
                  - cell [ref=e7252]:
                    - code [ref=e7253]: services
                    - emphasis [ref=e7254]: string
                  - cell [ref=e7255]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e7256]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e7257]: Services is the CIDR of the service network.
            - heading "ShootPurpose" [level=3] [ref=e7258]
            - paragraph [ref=e7259]:
              - emphasis [ref=e7260]: "Underlying type: string"
            - paragraph [ref=e7261]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e7262]: "Appears on:"
              - link "ShootSpec" [ref=e7263] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#shootspec"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e7264]: ShootPurpose is a type alias for string.
            - heading "ShootSSHKeypairRotation" [level=3] [ref=e7265]
            - paragraph [ref=e7266]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e7267]: "Appears on:"
              - link "ShootCredentialsRotation" [ref=e7268] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#shootcredentialsrotation"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e7269]: ShootSSHKeypairRotation contains information about the ssh-keypair credential rotation.
            - table [ref=e7270]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e7271]:
                - row [ref=e7272]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e7273]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e7274]
              - rowgroup [ref=e7275]:
                - row [ref=e7276]:
                  - cell [ref=e7277]:
                    - code [ref=e7278]: lastInitiationTime
                    - emphasis [ref=e7279]:
                      - link "Time" [ref=e7280] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#time-v1-meta
                  - cell [ref=e7281]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e7282]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e7283]: LastInitiationTime is the most recent time when the ssh-keypair credential rotation was initiated.
                - row [ref=e7284]:
                  - cell [ref=e7285]:
                    - code [ref=e7286]: lastCompletionTime
                    - emphasis [ref=e7287]:
                      - link "Time" [ref=e7288] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#time-v1-meta
                  - cell [ref=e7289]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e7290]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e7291]: LastCompletionTime is the most recent time when the ssh-keypair credential rotation was successfully completed.
            - heading "ShootSpec" [level=3] [ref=e7292]
            - paragraph [ref=e7293]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e7294]: "Appears on:"
              - link "Shoot" [ref=e7295] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#shoot"
              - text: ","
              - link "ShootTemplate" [ref=e7296] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#shoottemplate"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e7297]: ShootSpec is the specification of a Shoot.
            - table [ref=e7298]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e7299]:
                - row [ref=e7300]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e7301]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e7302]
              - rowgroup [ref=e7303]:
                - row [ref=e7304]:
                  - cell [ref=e7305]:
                    - code [ref=e7306]: addons
                    - emphasis [ref=e7307]:
                      - link "Addons" [ref=e7308] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#addons"
                  - cell [ref=e7309]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e7310]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e7311]: "Addons contains information about enabled/disabled addons and their configuration.Deprecated: This field is deprecated. Enabling addons will be forbidden starting from Kubernetes 1.35."
                - row [ref=e7312]:
                  - cell [ref=e7313]:
                    - code [ref=e7314]: cloudProfileName
                    - emphasis [ref=e7315]: string
                  - cell [ref=e7316]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e7317]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e7318]: "CloudProfileName is a name of a CloudProfile object.Deprecated: This field will be removed in a future version of Gardener. Use `CloudProfile` instead.Until Kubernetes v1.33, this field is synced with the `CloudProfile` field.Starting with Kubernetes v1.34, this field is set to empty string and must not be provided anymore."
                - row [ref=e7319]:
                  - cell [ref=e7320]:
                    - code [ref=e7321]: dns
                    - emphasis [ref=e7322]:
                      - link "DNS" [ref=e7323] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#dns"
                  - cell [ref=e7324]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e7325]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e7326]: DNS contains information about the DNS settings of the Shoot.
                - row [ref=e7327]:
                  - cell [ref=e7328]:
                    - code [ref=e7329]: extensions
                    - emphasis [ref=e7330]:
                      - link "Extension" [ref=e7331] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#extension"
                      - text: array
                  - cell [ref=e7332]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e7333]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e7334]: Extensions contain type and provider information for Shoot extensions.
                - row [ref=e7335]:
                  - cell [ref=e7336]:
                    - code [ref=e7337]: hibernation
                    - emphasis [ref=e7338]:
                      - link "Hibernation" [ref=e7339] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#hibernation"
                  - cell [ref=e7340]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e7341]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e7342]: Hibernation contains information whether the Shoot is suspended or not.
                - row [ref=e7343]:
                  - cell [ref=e7344]:
                    - code [ref=e7345]: kubernetes
                    - emphasis [ref=e7346]:
                      - link "Kubernetes" [ref=e7347] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#kubernetes"
                  - cell [ref=e7348]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e7349]: Kubernetes contains the version and configuration settings of the control plane components.
                - row [ref=e7350]:
                  - cell [ref=e7351]:
                    - code [ref=e7352]: networking
                    - emphasis [ref=e7353]:
                      - link "Networking" [ref=e7354] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#networking"
                  - cell [ref=e7355]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e7356]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e7357]: Networking contains information about cluster networking such as CNI Plugin type, CIDRs, ...etc.
                - row [ref=e7358]:
                  - cell [ref=e7359]:
                    - code [ref=e7360]: maintenance
                    - emphasis [ref=e7361]:
                      - link "Maintenance" [ref=e7362] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#maintenance"
                  - cell [ref=e7363]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e7364]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e7365]: Maintenance contains information about the time window for maintenance operations and whichoperations should be performed.
                - row [ref=e7366]:
                  - cell [ref=e7367]:
                    - code [ref=e7368]: monitoring
                    - emphasis [ref=e7369]:
                      - link "Monitoring" [ref=e7370] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#monitoring"
                  - cell [ref=e7371]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e7372]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e7373]: Monitoring contains information about custom monitoring configurations for the shoot.
                - row [ref=e7374]:
                  - cell [ref=e7375]:
                    - code [ref=e7376]: provider
                    - emphasis [ref=e7377]:
                      - link "Provider" [ref=e7378] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#provider"
                  - cell [ref=e7379]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e7380]: Provider contains all provider-specific and provider-relevant information.
                - row [ref=e7381]:
                  - cell [ref=e7382]:
                    - code [ref=e7383]: purpose
                    - emphasis [ref=e7384]:
                      - link "ShootPurpose" [ref=e7385] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#shootpurpose"
                  - cell [ref=e7386]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e7387]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e7388]: Purpose is the purpose class for this cluster.
                - row [ref=e7389]:
                  - cell [ref=e7390]:
                    - code [ref=e7391]: region
                    - emphasis [ref=e7392]: string
                  - cell [ref=e7393]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e7394]: Region is a name of a region. This field is immutable.
                - row [ref=e7395]:
                  - cell [ref=e7396]:
                    - code [ref=e7397]: secretBindingName
                    - emphasis [ref=e7398]: string
                  - cell [ref=e7399]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e7400]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e7401]: "SecretBindingName is the name of a SecretBinding that has a reference to the provider secret.The credentials inside the provider secret will be used to create the shoot in the respective account.The field is mutually exclusive with CredentialsBindingName.This field is immutable.Deprecated: Use CredentialsBindingName instead. See https://github.com/gardener/gardener/blob/master/docs/usage/shoot-operations/secretbinding-to-credentialsbinding-migration.md for migration instructions."
                - row [ref=e7402]:
                  - cell [ref=e7403]:
                    - code [ref=e7404]: seedName
                    - emphasis [ref=e7405]: string
                  - cell [ref=e7406]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e7407]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e7408]: SeedName is the name of the seed cluster that runs the control plane of the Shoot.
                - row [ref=e7409]:
                  - cell [ref=e7410]:
                    - code [ref=e7411]: seedSelector
                    - emphasis [ref=e7412]:
                      - link "SeedSelector" [ref=e7413] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#seedselector"
                  - cell [ref=e7414]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e7415]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e7416]: SeedSelector is an optional selector which must match a seed's labels for the shoot to be scheduled on that seed.Once the shoot is assigned to a seed, the selector can only be changed later if the new one still matches the assigned seed.
                - row [ref=e7417]:
                  - cell [ref=e7418]:
                    - code [ref=e7419]: resources
                    - emphasis [ref=e7420]:
                      - link "NamedResourceReference" [ref=e7421] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#namedresourcereference"
                      - text: array
                  - cell [ref=e7422]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e7423]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e7424]: Resources holds a list of named resource references that can be referred to in extension configs by their names.
                - row [ref=e7425]:
                  - cell [ref=e7426]:
                    - code [ref=e7427]: tolerations
                    - emphasis [ref=e7428]:
                      - link "Toleration" [ref=e7429] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#toleration"
                      - text: array
                  - cell [ref=e7430]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e7431]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e7432]: Tolerations contains the tolerations for taints on seed clusters.
                - row [ref=e7433]:
                  - cell [ref=e7434]:
                    - code [ref=e7435]: exposureClassName
                    - emphasis [ref=e7436]: string
                  - cell [ref=e7437]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e7438]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e7439]: ExposureClassName is the optional name of an exposure class to apply a control plane endpoint exposure strategy.
                - row [ref=e7440]:
                  - cell [ref=e7441]:
                    - code [ref=e7442]: systemComponents
                    - emphasis [ref=e7443]:
                      - link "SystemComponents" [ref=e7444] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#systemcomponents"
                  - cell [ref=e7445]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e7446]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e7447]: SystemComponents contains the settings of system components in the control or data plane of the Shoot cluster.
                - row [ref=e7448]:
                  - cell [ref=e7449]:
                    - code [ref=e7450]: controlPlane
                    - emphasis [ref=e7451]:
                      - link "ControlPlane" [ref=e7452] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#controlplane"
                  - cell [ref=e7453]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e7454]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e7455]: ControlPlane contains general settings for the control plane of the shoot.
                - row [ref=e7456]:
                  - cell [ref=e7457]:
                    - code [ref=e7458]: schedulerName
                    - emphasis [ref=e7459]: string
                  - cell [ref=e7460]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e7461]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e7462]: SchedulerName is the name of the responsible scheduler which schedules the shoot.If not specified, the default scheduler takes over.This field is immutable.
                - row [ref=e7463]:
                  - cell [ref=e7464]:
                    - code [ref=e7465]: cloudProfile
                    - emphasis [ref=e7466]:
                      - link "CloudProfileReference" [ref=e7467] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#cloudprofilereference"
                  - cell [ref=e7468]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e7469]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e7470]: CloudProfile contains a reference to a CloudProfile or a NamespacedCloudProfile.
                - row [ref=e7471]:
                  - cell [ref=e7472]:
                    - code [ref=e7473]: credentialsBindingName
                    - emphasis [ref=e7474]: string
                  - cell [ref=e7475]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e7476]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e7477]: CredentialsBindingName is the name of a CredentialsBinding that has a reference to the provider credentials.The credentials will be used to create the shoot in the respective account. The field is mutually exclusive with SecretBindingName.
                - row [ref=e7478]:
                  - cell [ref=e7479]:
                    - code [ref=e7480]: accessRestrictions
                    - emphasis [ref=e7481]:
                      - link "AccessRestrictionWithOptions" [ref=e7482] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#accessrestrictionwithoptions"
                      - text: array
                  - cell [ref=e7483]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e7484]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e7485]: AccessRestrictions describe a list of access restrictions for this shoot cluster.
            - heading "ShootState" [level=3] [ref=e7486]
            - paragraph [ref=e7487]: ShootState contains a snapshot of the Shoot's state required to migrate the Shoot's control plane to a new Seed.
            - table [ref=e7488]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e7489]:
                - row [ref=e7490]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e7491]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e7492]
              - rowgroup [ref=e7493]:
                - row [ref=e7494]:
                  - cell [ref=e7495]:
                    - code [ref=e7496]: metadata
                    - emphasis [ref=e7497]:
                      - link "ObjectMeta" [ref=e7498] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#objectmeta-v1-meta
                  - cell [ref=e7499]:
                    - text: Refer to the Kubernetes API documentation for the fields of the
                    - code [ref=e7500]: metadata
                    - text: field.
                - row [ref=e7501]:
                  - cell [ref=e7502]:
                    - code [ref=e7503]: spec
                    - emphasis [ref=e7504]:
                      - link "ShootStateSpec" [ref=e7505] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#shootstatespec"
                  - cell [ref=e7506]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e7507]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e7508]: Specification of the ShootState.
            - heading "ShootStateSpec" [level=3] [ref=e7509]
            - paragraph [ref=e7510]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e7511]: "Appears on:"
              - link "ShootState" [ref=e7512] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#shootstate"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e7513]: ShootStateSpec is the specification of the ShootState.
            - table [ref=e7514]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e7515]:
                - row [ref=e7516]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e7517]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e7518]
              - rowgroup [ref=e7519]:
                - row [ref=e7520]:
                  - cell [ref=e7521]:
                    - code [ref=e7522]: gardener
                    - emphasis [ref=e7523]:
                      - link "GardenerResourceData" [ref=e7524] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#gardenerresourcedata"
                      - text: array
                  - cell [ref=e7525]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e7526]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e7527]: Gardener holds the data required to generate resources deployed by the gardenlet
                - row [ref=e7528]:
                  - cell [ref=e7529]:
                    - code [ref=e7530]: extensions
                    - emphasis [ref=e7531]:
                      - link "ExtensionResourceState" [ref=e7532] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#extensionresourcestate"
                      - text: array
                  - cell [ref=e7533]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e7534]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e7535]: Extensions holds the state of custom resources reconciled by extension controllers in the seed
                - row [ref=e7536]:
                  - cell [ref=e7537]:
                    - code [ref=e7538]: resources
                    - emphasis [ref=e7539]:
                      - link "ResourceData" [ref=e7540] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#resourcedata"
                      - text: array
                  - cell [ref=e7541]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e7542]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e7543]: Resources holds the data of resources referred to by extension controller states
            - heading "ShootStatus" [level=3] [ref=e7544]
            - paragraph [ref=e7545]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e7546]: "Appears on:"
              - link "Shoot" [ref=e7547] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#shoot"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e7548]: ShootStatus holds the most recently observed status of the Shoot cluster.
            - table [ref=e7549]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e7550]:
                - row [ref=e7551]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e7552]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e7553]
              - rowgroup [ref=e7554]:
                - row [ref=e7555]:
                  - cell [ref=e7556]:
                    - code [ref=e7557]: conditions
                    - emphasis [ref=e7558]:
                      - link "Condition" [ref=e7559] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#condition"
                      - text: array
                  - cell [ref=e7560]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e7561]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e7562]: Conditions represents the latest available observations of a Shoots's current state based on health checks.
                - row [ref=e7563]:
                  - cell [ref=e7564]:
                    - code [ref=e7565]: constraints
                    - emphasis [ref=e7566]:
                      - link "Condition" [ref=e7567] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#condition"
                      - text: array
                  - cell [ref=e7568]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e7569]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e7570]: Constraints represents conditions of a Shoot's current state that constraint or are relevant for some operations.
                - row [ref=e7571]:
                  - cell [ref=e7572]:
                    - code [ref=e7573]: gardener
                    - emphasis [ref=e7574]:
                      - link "Gardener" [ref=e7575] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#gardener"
                  - cell [ref=e7576]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e7577]: Gardener holds information about the Gardener which last acted on the Shoot.
                - row [ref=e7578]:
                  - cell [ref=e7579]:
                    - code [ref=e7580]: hibernated
                    - emphasis [ref=e7581]: boolean
                  - cell [ref=e7582]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e7583]: IsHibernated indicates whether the Shoot is currently hibernated.
                - row [ref=e7584]:
                  - cell [ref=e7585]:
                    - code [ref=e7586]: lastOperation
                    - emphasis [ref=e7587]:
                      - link "LastOperation" [ref=e7588] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#lastoperation"
                  - cell [ref=e7589]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e7590]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e7591]: LastOperation holds information about the last operation on the Shoot.
                - row [ref=e7592]:
                  - cell [ref=e7593]:
                    - code [ref=e7594]: lastErrors
                    - emphasis [ref=e7595]:
                      - link "LastError" [ref=e7596] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#lasterror"
                      - text: array
                  - cell [ref=e7597]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e7598]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e7599]: LastErrors holds information about the last occurred error(s) during an operation.
                - row [ref=e7600]:
                  - cell [ref=e7601]:
                    - code [ref=e7602]: observedGeneration
                    - emphasis [ref=e7603]: integer
                  - cell [ref=e7604]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e7605]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e7606]: ObservedGeneration is the most recent generation observed for this Shoot. It corresponds to theShoot's generation, which is updated on mutation by the API Server.
                - row [ref=e7607]:
                  - cell [ref=e7608]:
                    - code [ref=e7609]: retryCycleStartTime
                    - emphasis [ref=e7610]:
                      - link "Time" [ref=e7611] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#time-v1-meta
                  - cell [ref=e7612]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e7613]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e7614]: RetryCycleStartTime is the start time of the last retry cycle (used to determine how often an operationmust be retried until we give up).
                - row [ref=e7615]:
                  - cell [ref=e7616]:
                    - code [ref=e7617]: seedName
                    - emphasis [ref=e7618]: string
                  - cell [ref=e7619]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e7620]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e7621]: SeedName is the name of the seed cluster that runs the control plane of the Shoot. This value is only writtenafter a successful create/reconcile operation. It will be used when control planes are moved between Seeds.
                - row [ref=e7622]:
                  - cell [ref=e7623]:
                    - code [ref=e7624]: technicalID
                    - emphasis [ref=e7625]: string
                  - cell [ref=e7626]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e7627]: TechnicalID is a unique technical ID for this Shoot. It is used for the infrastructure resources, andbasically everything that is related to this particular Shoot. For regular shoot clusters, this is also the nameof the namespace in the seed cluster running the shoot's control plane. This field is immutable.
                - row [ref=e7628]:
                  - cell [ref=e7629]:
                    - code [ref=e7630]: uid
                    - emphasis [ref=e7631]:
                      - link "UID" [ref=e7632] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#uid-types-pkg
                  - cell [ref=e7633]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e7634]: UID is a unique identifier for the Shoot cluster to avoid portability between Kubernetes clusters.It is used to compute unique hashes. This field is immutable.
                - row [ref=e7635]:
                  - cell [ref=e7636]:
                    - code [ref=e7637]: clusterIdentity
                    - emphasis [ref=e7638]: string
                  - cell [ref=e7639]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e7640]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e7641]: ClusterIdentity is the identity of the Shoot cluster. This field is immutable.
                - row [ref=e7642]:
                  - cell [ref=e7643]:
                    - code [ref=e7644]: advertisedAddresses
                    - emphasis [ref=e7645]:
                      - link "ShootAdvertisedAddress" [ref=e7646] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#shootadvertisedaddress"
                      - text: array
                  - cell [ref=e7647]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e7648]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e7649]: List of addresses that are relevant to the shoot.These include the Kube API server address and also the service account issuer.
                - row [ref=e7650]:
                  - cell [ref=e7651]:
                    - code [ref=e7652]: migrationStartTime
                    - emphasis [ref=e7653]:
                      - link "Time" [ref=e7654] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#time-v1-meta
                  - cell [ref=e7655]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e7656]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e7657]: MigrationStartTime is the time when a migration to a different seed was initiated.
                - row [ref=e7658]:
                  - cell [ref=e7659]:
                    - code [ref=e7660]: credentials
                    - emphasis [ref=e7661]:
                      - link "ShootCredentials" [ref=e7662] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#shootcredentials"
                  - cell [ref=e7663]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e7664]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e7665]: Credentials contains information about the shoot credentials.
                - row [ref=e7666]:
                  - cell [ref=e7667]:
                    - code [ref=e7668]: lastHibernationTriggerTime
                    - emphasis [ref=e7669]:
                      - link "Time" [ref=e7670] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#time-v1-meta
                  - cell [ref=e7671]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e7672]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e7673]: LastHibernationTriggerTime indicates the last time when the hibernation controllermanaged to change the hibernation settings of the cluster
                - row [ref=e7674]:
                  - cell [ref=e7675]:
                    - code [ref=e7676]: lastMaintenance
                    - emphasis [ref=e7677]:
                      - link "LastMaintenance" [ref=e7678] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#lastmaintenance"
                  - cell [ref=e7679]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e7680]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e7681]: LastMaintenance holds information about the last maintenance operations on the Shoot.
                - row [ref=e7682]:
                  - cell [ref=e7683]:
                    - code [ref=e7684]: networking
                    - emphasis [ref=e7685]:
                      - link "NetworkingStatus" [ref=e7686] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#networkingstatus"
                  - cell [ref=e7687]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e7688]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e7689]: Networking contains information about cluster networking such as CIDRs.
                - row [ref=e7690]:
                  - cell [ref=e7691]:
                    - code [ref=e7692]: inPlaceUpdates
                    - emphasis [ref=e7693]:
                      - link "InPlaceUpdatesStatus" [ref=e7694] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#inplaceupdatesstatus"
                  - cell [ref=e7695]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e7696]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e7697]: InPlaceUpdates contains information about in-place updates for the Shoot workers.
                - row [ref=e7698]:
                  - cell [ref=e7699]:
                    - code [ref=e7700]: manualWorkerPoolRollout
                    - emphasis [ref=e7701]:
                      - link "ManualWorkerPoolRollout" [ref=e7702] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#manualworkerpoolrollout"
                  - cell [ref=e7703]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e7704]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e7705]: ManualWorkerPoolRollout contains information about the worker pool rollout progress.
                - row [ref=e7706]:
                  - cell [ref=e7707]:
                    - code [ref=e7708]: liveMigration
                    - emphasis [ref=e7709]:
                      - link "LiveMigration" [ref=e7710] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#livemigration"
                  - cell [ref=e7711]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e7712]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e7713]: LiveMigration contains information about an ongoing live control plane migration of the Shoot.
            - heading "ShootTemplate" [level=3] [ref=e7714]
            - paragraph [ref=e7715]: ShootTemplate is a template for creating a Shoot object.
            - table [ref=e7716]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e7717]:
                - row [ref=e7718]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e7719]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e7720]
              - rowgroup [ref=e7721]:
                - row [ref=e7722]:
                  - cell [ref=e7723]:
                    - code [ref=e7724]: metadata
                    - emphasis [ref=e7725]:
                      - link "ObjectMeta" [ref=e7726] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#objectmeta-v1-meta
                  - cell [ref=e7727]:
                    - text: Refer to the Kubernetes API documentation for the fields of the
                    - code [ref=e7728]: metadata
                    - text: field.
                - row [ref=e7729]:
                  - cell [ref=e7730]:
                    - code [ref=e7731]: spec
                    - emphasis [ref=e7732]:
                      - link "ShootSpec" [ref=e7733] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#shootspec"
                  - cell [ref=e7734]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e7735]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e7736]: Specification of the desired behavior of the Shoot.
            - heading "StructuredAuthentication" [level=3] [ref=e7737]
            - paragraph [ref=e7738]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e7739]: "Appears on:"
              - link "KubeAPIServerConfig" [ref=e7740] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#kubeapiserverconfig"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e7741]: StructuredAuthentication contains authentication config for kube-apiserver.
            - table [ref=e7742]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e7743]:
                - row [ref=e7744]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e7745]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e7746]
              - rowgroup [ref=e7747]:
                - row [ref=e7748]:
                  - cell [ref=e7749]:
                    - code [ref=e7750]: configMapName
                    - emphasis [ref=e7751]: string
                  - cell [ref=e7752]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e7753]: ConfigMapName is the name of the ConfigMap in the project namespace which contains AuthenticationConfigurationfor the kube-apiserver.
            - heading "StructuredAuthorization" [level=3] [ref=e7754]
            - paragraph [ref=e7755]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e7756]: "Appears on:"
              - link "KubeAPIServerConfig" [ref=e7757] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#kubeapiserverconfig"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e7758]: StructuredAuthorization contains authorization config for kube-apiserver.
            - table [ref=e7759]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e7760]:
                - row [ref=e7761]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e7762]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e7763]
              - rowgroup [ref=e7764]:
                - row [ref=e7765]:
                  - cell [ref=e7766]:
                    - code [ref=e7767]: configMapName
                    - emphasis [ref=e7768]: string
                  - cell [ref=e7769]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e7770]: ConfigMapName is the name of the ConfigMap in the project namespace which contains AuthorizationConfiguration forthe kube-apiserver.
                - row [ref=e7771]:
                  - cell [ref=e7772]:
                    - code [ref=e7773]: kubeconfigs
                    - emphasis [ref=e7774]:
                      - link "AuthorizerKubeconfigReference" [ref=e7775] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#authorizerkubeconfigreference"
                      - text: array
                  - cell [ref=e7776]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e7777]: Kubeconfigs is a list of references for kubeconfigs for the authorization webhooks.
            - heading "SwapBehavior" [level=3] [ref=e7778]
            - paragraph [ref=e7779]:
              - emphasis [ref=e7780]: "Underlying type: string"
            - paragraph [ref=e7781]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e7782]: "Appears on:"
              - link "MemorySwapConfiguration" [ref=e7783] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#memoryswapconfiguration"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e7784]: SwapBehavior configures swap memory available to container workloads
            - heading "SystemComponents" [level=3] [ref=e7785]
            - paragraph [ref=e7786]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e7787]: "Appears on:"
              - link "ShootSpec" [ref=e7788] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#shootspec"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e7789]: SystemComponents contains the settings of system components in the control or data plane of the Shoot cluster.
            - table [ref=e7790]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e7791]:
                - row [ref=e7792]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e7793]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e7794]
              - rowgroup [ref=e7795]:
                - row [ref=e7796]:
                  - cell [ref=e7797]:
                    - code [ref=e7798]: coreDNS
                    - emphasis [ref=e7799]:
                      - link "CoreDNS" [ref=e7800] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#coredns"
                  - cell [ref=e7801]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e7802]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e7803]: CoreDNS contains the settings of the Core DNS components running in the data plane of the Shoot cluster.
                - row [ref=e7804]:
                  - cell [ref=e7805]:
                    - code [ref=e7806]: nodeLocalDNS
                    - emphasis [ref=e7807]:
                      - link "NodeLocalDNS" [ref=e7808] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#nodelocaldns"
                  - cell [ref=e7809]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e7810]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e7811]: NodeLocalDNS contains the settings of the node local DNS components running in the data plane of the Shoot cluster.
            - heading "Toleration" [level=3] [ref=e7812]
            - paragraph [ref=e7813]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e7814]: "Appears on:"
              - link "ExposureClassScheduling" [ref=e7815] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#exposureclassscheduling"
              - text: ","
              - link "ProjectTolerations" [ref=e7816] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#projecttolerations"
              - text: ","
              - link "ShootSpec" [ref=e7817] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#shootspec"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e7818]: Toleration is a toleration for a seed taint.
            - table [ref=e7819]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e7820]:
                - row [ref=e7821]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e7822]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e7823]
              - rowgroup [ref=e7824]:
                - row [ref=e7825]:
                  - cell [ref=e7826]:
                    - code [ref=e7827]: key
                    - emphasis [ref=e7828]: string
                  - cell [ref=e7829]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e7830]: Key is the toleration key to be applied to a project or shoot.
                - row [ref=e7831]:
                  - cell [ref=e7832]:
                    - code [ref=e7833]: value
                    - emphasis [ref=e7834]: string
                  - cell [ref=e7835]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e7836]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e7837]: Value is the toleration value corresponding to the toleration key.
            - heading "VersionClassification" [level=3] [ref=e7838]
            - paragraph [ref=e7839]:
              - emphasis [ref=e7840]: "Underlying type: string"
            - paragraph [ref=e7841]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e7842]: "Appears on:"
              - link "ExpirableVersion" [ref=e7843] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#expirableversion"
              - text: ","
              - link "ExpirableVersionStatus" [ref=e7844] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#expirableversionstatus"
              - text: ","
              - link "LifecycleStage" [ref=e7845] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#lifecyclestage"
              - text: ","
              - link "MachineImageVersion" [ref=e7846] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#machineimageversion"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e7847]: VersionClassification is the logical state of a version.
            - heading "VerticalPodAutoscaler" [level=3] [ref=e7848]
            - paragraph [ref=e7849]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e7850]: "Appears on:"
              - link "Kubernetes" [ref=e7851] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#kubernetes"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e7852]: VerticalPodAutoscaler contains the configuration flags for the Kubernetes vertical pod autoscaler.
            - table [ref=e7853]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e7854]:
                - row [ref=e7855]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e7856]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e7857]
              - rowgroup [ref=e7858]:
                - row [ref=e7859]:
                  - cell [ref=e7860]:
                    - code [ref=e7861]: enabled
                    - emphasis [ref=e7862]: boolean
                  - cell [ref=e7863]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e7864]: Enabled specifies whether the Kubernetes VPA shall be enabled for the shoot cluster.
                - row [ref=e7865]:
                  - cell [ref=e7866]:
                    - code [ref=e7867]: evictAfterOOMThreshold
                    - emphasis [ref=e7868]:
                      - link "Duration" [ref=e7869] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#duration-v1-meta
                  - cell [ref=e7870]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e7871]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e7872]: "EvictAfterOOMThreshold defines the threshold that will lead to pod eviction in case it OOMed in less than the giventhreshold since its start and if it has only one container (default: 10m0s)."
                - row [ref=e7873]:
                  - cell [ref=e7874]:
                    - code [ref=e7875]: evictionRateBurst
                    - emphasis [ref=e7876]: integer
                  - cell [ref=e7877]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e7878]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e7879]: "EvictionRateBurst defines the burst of pods that can be evicted (default: 1)"
                - row [ref=e7880]:
                  - cell [ref=e7881]:
                    - code [ref=e7882]: evictionRateLimit
                    - emphasis [ref=e7883]: float
                  - cell [ref=e7884]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e7885]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e7886]: "EvictionRateLimit defines the number of pods that can be evicted per second. A rate limit set to 0 or -1 willdisable the rate limiter (default: -1)."
                - row [ref=e7887]:
                  - cell [ref=e7888]:
                    - code [ref=e7889]: evictionTolerance
                    - emphasis [ref=e7890]: float
                  - cell [ref=e7891]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e7892]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e7893]: "EvictionTolerance defines the fraction of replica count that can be evicted for update in case more than onepod can be evicted (default: 0.5)."
                - row [ref=e7894]:
                  - cell [ref=e7895]:
                    - code [ref=e7896]: recommendationMarginFraction
                    - emphasis [ref=e7897]: float
                  - cell [ref=e7898]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e7899]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e7900]: "RecommendationMarginFraction is the fraction of usage added as the safety margin to the recommended request(default: 0.15)."
                - row [ref=e7901]:
                  - cell [ref=e7902]:
                    - code [ref=e7903]: updaterInterval
                    - emphasis [ref=e7904]:
                      - link "Duration" [ref=e7905] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#duration-v1-meta
                  - cell [ref=e7906]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e7907]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e7908]: "UpdaterInterval is the interval how often the updater should run (default: 1m0s)."
                - row [ref=e7909]:
                  - cell [ref=e7910]:
                    - code [ref=e7911]: recommenderInterval
                    - emphasis [ref=e7912]:
                      - link "Duration" [ref=e7913] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#duration-v1-meta
                  - cell [ref=e7914]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e7915]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e7916]: "RecommenderInterval is the interval how often metrics should be fetched (default: 1m0s)."
                - row [ref=e7917]:
                  - cell [ref=e7918]:
                    - code [ref=e7919]: targetCPUPercentile
                    - emphasis [ref=e7920]: float
                  - cell [ref=e7921]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e7922]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e7923]: "TargetCPUPercentile is the usage percentile that will be used as a base for CPU target recommendation.Doesn't affect CPU lower bound, CPU upper bound nor memory recommendations.(default: 0.9)"
                - row [ref=e7924]:
                  - cell [ref=e7925]:
                    - code [ref=e7926]: recommendationLowerBoundCPUPercentile
                    - emphasis [ref=e7927]: float
                  - cell [ref=e7928]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e7929]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e7930]: "RecommendationLowerBoundCPUPercentile is the usage percentile that will be used for the lower bound on CPU recommendation.(default: 0.5)"
                - row [ref=e7931]:
                  - cell [ref=e7932]:
                    - code [ref=e7933]: recommendationUpperBoundCPUPercentile
                    - emphasis [ref=e7934]: float
                  - cell [ref=e7935]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e7936]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e7937]: "RecommendationUpperBoundCPUPercentile is the usage percentile that will be used for the upper bound on CPU recommendation.(default: 0.95)"
                - row [ref=e7938]:
                  - cell [ref=e7939]:
                    - code [ref=e7940]: targetMemoryPercentile
                    - emphasis [ref=e7941]: float
                  - cell [ref=e7942]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e7943]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e7944]: "TargetMemoryPercentile is the usage percentile that will be used as a base for memory target recommendation.Doesn't affect memory lower bound nor memory upper bound.(default: 0.9)"
                - row [ref=e7945]:
                  - cell [ref=e7946]:
                    - code [ref=e7947]: recommendationLowerBoundMemoryPercentile
                    - emphasis [ref=e7948]: float
                  - cell [ref=e7949]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e7950]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e7951]: "RecommendationLowerBoundMemoryPercentile is the usage percentile that will be used for the lower bound on memory recommendation.(default: 0.5)"
                - row [ref=e7952]:
                  - cell [ref=e7953]:
                    - code [ref=e7954]: recommendationUpperBoundMemoryPercentile
                    - emphasis [ref=e7955]: float
                  - cell [ref=e7956]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e7957]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e7958]: "RecommendationUpperBoundMemoryPercentile is the usage percentile that will be used for the upper bound on memory recommendation.(default: 0.95)"
                - row [ref=e7959]:
                  - cell [ref=e7960]:
                    - code [ref=e7961]: cpuHistogramDecayHalfLife
                    - emphasis [ref=e7962]:
                      - link "Duration" [ref=e7963] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#duration-v1-meta
                  - cell [ref=e7964]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e7965]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e7966]: "CPUHistogramDecayHalfLife is the amount of time it takes a historical CPU usage sample to lose half of its weight.(default: 24h)"
                - row [ref=e7967]:
                  - cell [ref=e7968]:
                    - code [ref=e7969]: memoryHistogramDecayHalfLife
                    - emphasis [ref=e7970]:
                      - link "Duration" [ref=e7971] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#duration-v1-meta
                  - cell [ref=e7972]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e7973]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e7974]: "MemoryHistogramDecayHalfLife is the amount of time it takes a historical memory usage sample to lose half of its weight.(default: 24h)"
                - row [ref=e7975]:
                  - cell [ref=e7976]:
                    - code [ref=e7977]: memoryAggregationInterval
                    - emphasis [ref=e7978]:
                      - link "Duration" [ref=e7979] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#duration-v1-meta
                  - cell [ref=e7980]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e7981]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e7982]: "MemoryAggregationInterval is the length of a single interval, for which the peak memory usage is computed.(default: 24h)"
                - row [ref=e7983]:
                  - cell [ref=e7984]:
                    - code [ref=e7985]: memoryAggregationIntervalCount
                    - emphasis [ref=e7986]: integer
                  - cell [ref=e7987]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e7988]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e7989]: "MemoryAggregationIntervalCount is the number of consecutive memory-aggregation-intervals which make up theMemoryAggregationWindowLength which in turn is the period for memory usage aggregation by VPA. In other words,`MemoryAggregationWindowLength = memory-aggregation-interval * memory-aggregation-interval-count`.(default: 8)"
                - row [ref=e7990]:
                  - cell [ref=e7991]:
                    - code [ref=e7992]: featureGates
                    - emphasis [ref=e7993]: object (keys:string, values:boolean)
                  - cell [ref=e7994]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e7995]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e7996]: FeatureGates contains information about enabled feature gates.
                - row [ref=e7997]:
                  - cell [ref=e7998]:
                    - code [ref=e7999]: recommenderUpdateWorkerCount
                    - emphasis [ref=e8000]: integer
                  - cell [ref=e8001]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e8002]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e8003]: "RecommenderUpdateWorkerCount is the number of workers used in the vpa-recommender for updating VPAs and VPACheckpoints in parallel.(default: 10)"
            - heading "Volume" [level=3] [ref=e8004]
            - paragraph [ref=e8005]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e8006]: "Appears on:"
              - link "Worker" [ref=e8007] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#worker"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e8008]: Volume contains information about the volume type, size, and encryption.
            - table [ref=e8009]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e8010]:
                - row [ref=e8011]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e8012]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e8013]
              - rowgroup [ref=e8014]:
                - row [ref=e8015]:
                  - cell [ref=e8016]:
                    - code [ref=e8017]: name
                    - emphasis [ref=e8018]: string
                  - cell [ref=e8019]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e8020]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e8021]: Name of the volume to make it referenceable.
                - row [ref=e8022]:
                  - cell [ref=e8023]:
                    - code [ref=e8024]: type
                    - emphasis [ref=e8025]: string
                  - cell [ref=e8026]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e8027]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e8028]: Type is the type of the volume.
                - row [ref=e8029]:
                  - cell [ref=e8030]:
                    - code [ref=e8031]: size
                    - emphasis [ref=e8032]: string
                  - cell [ref=e8033]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e8034]: VolumeSize is the size of the volume.
                - row [ref=e8035]:
                  - cell [ref=e8036]:
                    - code [ref=e8037]: encrypted
                    - emphasis [ref=e8038]: boolean
                  - cell [ref=e8039]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e8040]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e8041]: Encrypted determines if the volume should be encrypted.
            - heading "VolumeType" [level=3] [ref=e8042]
            - paragraph [ref=e8043]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e8044]: "Appears on:"
              - link "CloudProfileSpec" [ref=e8045] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#cloudprofilespec"
              - text: ","
              - link "NamespacedCloudProfileSpec" [ref=e8046] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#namespacedcloudprofilespec"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e8047]: VolumeType contains certain properties of a volume type.
            - table [ref=e8048]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e8049]:
                - row [ref=e8050]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e8051]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e8052]
              - rowgroup [ref=e8053]:
                - row [ref=e8054]:
                  - cell [ref=e8055]:
                    - code [ref=e8056]: class
                    - emphasis [ref=e8057]: string
                  - cell [ref=e8058]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e8059]: Class is the class of the volume type.
                - row [ref=e8060]:
                  - cell [ref=e8061]:
                    - code [ref=e8062]: name
                    - emphasis [ref=e8063]: string
                  - cell [ref=e8064]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e8065]: Name is the name of the volume type.
                - row [ref=e8066]:
                  - cell [ref=e8067]:
                    - code [ref=e8068]: usable
                    - emphasis [ref=e8069]: boolean
                  - cell [ref=e8070]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e8071]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e8072]: Usable defines if the volume type can be used for shoot clusters.
                - row [ref=e8073]:
                  - cell [ref=e8074]:
                    - code [ref=e8075]: minSize
                    - emphasis [ref=e8076]:
                      - link "Quantity" [ref=e8077] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#quantity-resource-api
                  - cell [ref=e8078]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e8079]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e8080]: MinSize is the minimal supported storage size.
            - heading "WatchCacheSizes" [level=3] [ref=e8081]
            - paragraph [ref=e8082]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e8083]: "Appears on:"
              - link "KubeAPIServerConfig" [ref=e8084] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#kubeapiserverconfig"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e8085]: WatchCacheSizes contains configuration of the API server's watch cache sizes.
            - table [ref=e8086]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e8087]:
                - row [ref=e8088]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e8089]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e8090]
              - rowgroup [ref=e8091]:
                - row [ref=e8092]:
                  - cell [ref=e8093]:
                    - code [ref=e8094]: default
                    - emphasis [ref=e8095]: integer
                  - cell [ref=e8096]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e8097]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e8098]: "Default is not respected anymore by kube-apiserver.The cache is sized automatically.Deprecated: This field is deprecated. Setting the default cache size will be forbidden starting from Kubernetes 1.35."
                - row [ref=e8099]:
                  - cell [ref=e8100]:
                    - code [ref=e8101]: resources
                    - emphasis [ref=e8102]:
                      - link "ResourceWatchCacheSize" [ref=e8103] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#resourcewatchcachesize"
                      - text: array
                  - cell [ref=e8104]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e8105]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e8106]: "Resources configures the watch cache size of the kube-apiserver per resource(flag `--watch-cache-sizes`).See: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/command-line-tools-reference/kube-apiserver/"
            - heading "Worker" [level=3] [ref=e8107]
            - paragraph [ref=e8108]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e8109]: "Appears on:"
              - link "Provider" [ref=e8110] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#provider"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e8111]: Worker is the base definition of a worker group.
            - table [ref=e8112]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e8113]:
                - row [ref=e8114]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e8115]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e8116]
              - rowgroup [ref=e8117]:
                - row [ref=e8118]:
                  - cell [ref=e8119]:
                    - code [ref=e8120]: annotations
                    - emphasis [ref=e8121]: object (keys:string, values:string)
                  - cell [ref=e8122]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e8123]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e8124]: "Annotations is a map of key/value pairs for annotations for all the `Node` objects in this worker pool."
                - row [ref=e8125]:
                  - cell [ref=e8126]:
                    - code [ref=e8127]: caBundle
                    - emphasis [ref=e8128]: string
                  - cell [ref=e8129]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e8130]: (Optional)
