                    - paragraph [ref=e8131]: CABundle is a certificate bundle which will be installed onto every machine of this worker pool.
                - row [ref=e8132]:
                  - cell [ref=e8133]:
                    - code [ref=e8134]: cri
                    - emphasis [ref=e8135]:
                      - link "CRI" [ref=e8136] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#cri"
                  - cell [ref=e8137]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e8138]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e8139]: "CRI contains configurations of CRI support of every machine in the worker pool.Defaults to a CRI with name `containerd`."
                - row [ref=e8140]:
                  - cell [ref=e8141]:
                    - code [ref=e8142]: kubernetes
                    - emphasis [ref=e8143]:
                      - link "WorkerKubernetes" [ref=e8144] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#workerkubernetes"
                  - cell [ref=e8145]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e8146]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e8147]: Kubernetes contains configuration for Kubernetes components related to this worker pool.
                - row [ref=e8148]:
                  - cell [ref=e8149]:
                    - code [ref=e8150]: labels
                    - emphasis [ref=e8151]: object (keys:string, values:string)
                  - cell [ref=e8152]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e8153]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e8154]: "Labels is a map of key/value pairs for labels for all the `Node` objects in this worker pool."
                - row [ref=e8155]:
                  - cell [ref=e8156]:
                    - code [ref=e8157]: name
                    - emphasis [ref=e8158]: string
                  - cell [ref=e8159]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e8160]: Name is the name of the worker group.
                - row [ref=e8161]:
                  - cell [ref=e8162]:
                    - code [ref=e8163]: machine
                    - emphasis [ref=e8164]:
                      - link "Machine" [ref=e8165] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#machine"
                  - cell [ref=e8166]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e8167]: Machine contains information about the machine type and image.
                - row [ref=e8168]:
                  - cell [ref=e8169]:
                    - code [ref=e8170]: maximum
                    - emphasis [ref=e8171]: integer
                  - cell [ref=e8172]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e8173]: Maximum is the maximum number of machines to create.This value is divided by the number of configured zones for a fair distribution.
                - row [ref=e8174]:
                  - cell [ref=e8175]:
                    - code [ref=e8176]: minimum
                    - emphasis [ref=e8177]: integer
                  - cell [ref=e8178]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e8179]: Minimum is the minimum number of machines to create.This value is divided by the number of configured zones for a fair distribution.
                - row [ref=e8180]:
                  - cell [ref=e8181]:
                    - code [ref=e8182]: maxSurge
                    - emphasis [ref=e8183]:
                      - link "IntOrString" [ref=e8184] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#intorstring-intstr-util
                  - cell [ref=e8185]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e8186]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e8187]: MaxSurge is maximum number of machines that are created during an update.This value is divided by the number of configured zones for a fair distribution.Defaults to 0 in case of an in-place update.Defaults to 1 in case of a rolling update.
                - row [ref=e8188]:
                  - cell [ref=e8189]:
                    - code [ref=e8190]: maxUnavailable
                    - emphasis [ref=e8191]:
                      - link "IntOrString" [ref=e8192] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#intorstring-intstr-util
                  - cell [ref=e8193]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e8194]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e8195]: MaxUnavailable is the maximum number of machines that can be unavailable during an update.This value is divided by the number of configured zones for a fair distribution.Defaults to 1 in case of an in-place update.Defaults to 0 in case of a rolling update.
                - row [ref=e8196]:
                  - cell [ref=e8197]:
                    - code [ref=e8198]: providerConfig
                    - emphasis [ref=e8199]:
                      - link "RawExtension" [ref=e8200] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#rawextension-runtime-pkg
                  - cell [ref=e8201]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e8202]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e8203]: ProviderConfig is the provider-specific configuration for this worker pool.
                - row [ref=e8204]:
                  - cell [ref=e8205]:
                    - code [ref=e8206]: taints
                    - emphasis [ref=e8207]:
                      - link "Taint" [ref=e8208] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.33/#taint-v1-core
                      - text: array
                  - cell [ref=e8209]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e8210]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e8211]: "Taints is a list of taints for all the `Node` objects in this worker pool."
                - row [ref=e8212]:
                  - cell [ref=e8213]:
                    - code [ref=e8214]: volume
                    - emphasis [ref=e8215]:
                      - link "Volume" [ref=e8216] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#volume"
                  - cell [ref=e8217]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e8218]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e8219]: Volume contains information about the volume type and size.
                - row [ref=e8220]:
                  - cell [ref=e8221]:
                    - code [ref=e8222]: dataVolumes
                    - emphasis [ref=e8223]:
                      - link "DataVolume" [ref=e8224] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#datavolume"
                      - text: array
                  - cell [ref=e8225]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e8226]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e8227]: DataVolumes contains a list of additional worker volumes.
                - row [ref=e8228]:
                  - cell [ref=e8229]:
                    - code [ref=e8230]: kubeletDataVolumeName
                    - emphasis [ref=e8231]: string
                  - cell [ref=e8232]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e8233]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e8234]: KubeletDataVolumeName contains the name of a dataVolume that should be used for storing kubelet state.
                - row [ref=e8235]:
                  - cell [ref=e8236]:
                    - code [ref=e8237]: zones
                    - emphasis [ref=e8238]: string array
                  - cell [ref=e8239]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e8240]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e8241]: Zones is a list of availability zones that are used to evenly distribute this worker pool. Optionalas not every provider may support availability zones.
                - row [ref=e8242]:
                  - cell [ref=e8243]:
                    - code [ref=e8244]: systemComponents
                    - emphasis [ref=e8245]:
                      - link "WorkerSystemComponents" [ref=e8246] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#workersystemcomponents"
                  - cell [ref=e8247]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e8248]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e8249]: SystemComponents contains configuration for system components related to this worker pool
                - row [ref=e8250]:
                  - cell [ref=e8251]:
                    - code [ref=e8252]: machineControllerManager
                    - emphasis [ref=e8253]:
                      - link "MachineControllerManagerSettings" [ref=e8254] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#machinecontrollermanagersettings"
                  - cell [ref=e8255]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e8256]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e8257]: MachineControllerManagerSettings contains configurations for different worker-pools. Eg. MachineDrainTimeout, MachineHealthTimeout.
                - row [ref=e8258]:
                  - cell [ref=e8259]:
                    - code [ref=e8260]: sysctls
                    - emphasis [ref=e8261]: object (keys:string, values:string)
                  - cell [ref=e8262]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e8263]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e8264]: Sysctls is a map of kernel settings to apply on all machines in this worker pool.
                - row [ref=e8265]:
                  - cell [ref=e8266]:
                    - code [ref=e8267]: clusterAutoscaler
                    - emphasis [ref=e8268]:
                      - link "ClusterAutoscalerOptions" [ref=e8269] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#clusterautoscaleroptions"
                  - cell [ref=e8270]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e8271]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e8272]: ClusterAutoscaler contains the cluster autoscaler configurations for the worker pool.
                - row [ref=e8273]:
                  - cell [ref=e8274]:
                    - code [ref=e8275]: priority
                    - emphasis [ref=e8276]: integer
                  - cell [ref=e8277]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e8278]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e8279]: Priority (or weight) is the importance by which this worker group will be scaled by cluster autoscaling.
                - row [ref=e8280]:
                  - cell [ref=e8281]:
                    - code [ref=e8282]: updateStrategy
                    - emphasis [ref=e8283]:
                      - link "MachineUpdateStrategy" [ref=e8284] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#machineupdatestrategy"
                  - cell [ref=e8285]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e8286]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e8287]: UpdateStrategy specifies the machine update strategy for the worker pool.
                - row [ref=e8288]:
                  - cell [ref=e8289]:
                    - code [ref=e8290]: controlPlane
                    - emphasis [ref=e8291]:
                      - link "WorkerControlPlane" [ref=e8292] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#workercontrolplane"
                  - cell [ref=e8293]:
                    - emphasis [ref=e8294]: (Optional)
                    - paragraph [ref=e8295]: ControlPlane specifies that the shoot cluster control plane components should be running in this worker pool.This is only relevant for self-hosted shoot clusters.
            - heading "WorkerControlPlane" [level=3] [ref=e8296]
            - paragraph [ref=e8297]:
              - text: (
              - emphasis [ref=e8298]: "Appears on:"
              - link "Worker" [ref=e8299] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: "#worker"
              - text: )
            - paragraph [ref=e8300]: WorkerControlPlane specifies that the shoot cluster control plane components should be running in this worker pool.
            - table [ref=e8301]:
              - rowgroup [ref=e8302]:
                - row [ref=e8303]:
                  - columnheader "Field" [ref=e8304]
                  - columnheader "Description" [ref=e8305]

  8  | function slug(route: string): string {

  10 |   return s === '' ? 'index' : s;
